        "from dataclasses import dataclass\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "@dataclass\n",
        "class ResearchQuestion:\n",
        "    question: str\n",
        "    field: str\n",
        "    objective: str\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def create_research_plan(question, field):\n",
        "    question = question.strip()\n",
        "    field = field.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not field:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research field.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return ResearchQuestion(\n",
        "        question=question,\n",
        "        field=field,\n",
        "        objective=\"Find evidence, evaluate results, and document limitations.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "plan = create_research_plan(\n",
        "    \"Does learning rate affect neural network accuracy?\",\n",
        "    \"Artificial Intelligence\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"RESEARCH PLAN\")\n",
        "print(\"Question:\", plan.question)\n",
        "print(\"Field:\", plan.field)\n",
        "print(\"Objective:\", plan.objective)"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "from dataclasses import dataclass\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Define the structure of a research question\n",
        "@dataclass\n",
        "class ResearchQuestion:\n",
        "    question: str\n",
        "    field: str\n",
        "    objective: str\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. Create and validate a research plan\n",
        "def create_research_plan(question, field):\n",
        "    question = question.strip()\n",
        "    field = field.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not field:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research field.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return ResearchQuestion(\n",
        "        question=question,\n",
        "        field=field,\n",
        "        objective=(\n",
        "            \"Find evidence, evaluate results, \"\n",
        "            \"and document limitations.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 3. Create the database and its table\n",
        "def initialize_database():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research_questions (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                question TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                field TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                objective TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n",
        "                UNIQUE(question, field)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 4. Save a research plan in the database\n",
        "def save_research_plan(plan):\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO research_questions\n",
        "                (question, field, objective)\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (plan.question, plan.field, plan.objective))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 5. Retrieve all saved research questions\n",
        "def get_research_questions():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, question, field, objective, created_at\n",
        "            FROM research_questions\n",
        "            ORDER BY id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return rows\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 6. Start the research planner\n",
        "initialize_database()\n",
        "\n",
        "plan1 = create_research_plan(\n",
        "    \"Does learning rate affect neural network accuracy?\",\n",
        "    \"Artificial Intelligence\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "plan2 = create_research_plan(\n",
        "    \"Can neural networks learn mathematical patterns?\",\n",
        "    \"Mathematics and Artificial Intelligence\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "save_research_plan(plan1)\n",
        "save_research_plan(plan2)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 7. Display the saved research plans\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "\n",
        "saved_plans = get_research_questions()\n",
        "\n",
        "for item in saved_plans:\n",
        "    print(\"\\nResearch ID:\", item[0])\n",
        "    print(\"Question:\", item[1])\n",
        "    print(\"Field:\", item[2])\n",
        "    print(\"Objective:\", item[3])\n",
        "    print(\"Created:\", item[4])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nTotal saved research questions:\", len(saved_plans))"
      ],

        "    \"Artificial Intelligence\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "save_research_plan(duplicate_plan)\n",
        "\n",
        "after = len(get_research_questions())\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Records before test:\", before)\n",

        "\n",
        "if before == after:\n",
        "    print(\"PASS: Duplicate record was prevented.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"FAIL: A duplicate record was created.\")"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "from pypdf import PdfReader\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def read_research_pdf(file_path):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Extract text from a PDF while preserving page numbers.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = Path(file_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not path.exists():\n",
        "        raise FileNotFoundError(\"The selected PDF file was not found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if path.suffix.lower() != \".pdf\":\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please upload a PDF file.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    reader = PdfReader(str(path))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if reader.is_encrypted:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"This PDF is encrypted and cannot be read.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_number, page in enumerate(reader.pages, start=1):\n",
        "        text = page.extract_text() or \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        pages.append({\n",
        "            \"page\": page_number,\n",
        "            \"text\": text.strip()\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    return pages\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Upload a research paper from your phone\n",
        "print(\"Choose a scientific research paper in PDF format.\")\n",
        "uploaded = files.upload()\n",
        "\n",
        "if not uploaded:\n",
        "    print(\"No file was uploaded. Please run this cell again.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    filename = next(iter(uploaded))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not filename.lower().endswith(\".pdf\"):\n",
        "        print(\"Error: Please upload a PDF file.\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            extracted_pages = read_research_pdf(filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "            total_pages = len(extracted_pages)\n",
        "            total_characters = sum(\n",
        "                len(page[\"text\"]) for page in extracted_pages\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"\\nPDF READER RESULTS\")\n",
        "            print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "            print(\"Filename:\", filename)\n",
        "            print(\"Total pages:\", total_pages)\n",
        "            print(\"Extracted characters:\", total_characters)\n",
        "\n",
        "            for page in extracted_pages[:3]:\n",
        "                print(f\"\\n--- Page {page['page']} ---\")\n",
        "                print(page[\"text\"][:1500] or \"[No extractable text]\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if total_pages > 3:\n",
        "                print(\"\\nOnly the first 3 pages are displayed.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if total_characters == 0:\n",
        "                print(\n",
        "                    \"\\nWarning: No text was extracted. \"\n",
        "                    \"The PDF may contain scanned images.\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as error:\n",
        "            print(\"Could not read the PDF:\", error)"
      ],

        {
          "output_type": "display_data",
          "data": {
            "text/plain": [
              "<IPython.core.display.HTML object>"
            ],
            "text/html": [
              "\n",
              "     <input type=\"file\" id=\"files-8c946a4b-06d1-41c5-b973-4766cd45a4b9\" name=\"files[]\" multiple disabled\n",
              "        style=\"border:none\" />\n",
              "     <output id=\"result-8c946a4b-06d1-41c5-b973-4766cd45a4b9\">\n",
              "      Upload widget is only available when the cell has been executed in the\n",
              "      current browser session. Please rerun this cell to enable.\n",
              "      </output>\n",
              "      <script>// Copyright 2017 Google LLC\n",
              "//\n",
              "// Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n",
              "// you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n",
              "// You may obtain a copy of the License at\n",
              "//\n",
              "//      http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n",
              "//\n",
              "// Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n",
              "// distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n",
              "// WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n",
              "// See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n",
              "// limitations under the License.\n",
              "\n",
              "/**\n",
              " * @fileoverview Helpers for google.colab Python module.\n",
              " */\n",
              "(function(scope) {\n",
              "function span(text, styleAttributes = {}) {\n",
              "  const element = document.createElement('span');\n",
              "  element.textContent = text;\n",
              "  for (const key of Object.keys(styleAttributes)) {\n",
              "    element.style[key] = styleAttributes[key];\n",
              "  }\n",
              "  return element;\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "// Max number of bytes which will be uploaded at a time.\n",
              "const MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE = 100 * 1024;\n",
              "\n",
              "function _uploadFiles(inputId, outputId) {\n",
              "  const steps = uploadFilesStep(inputId, outputId);\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  // Cache steps on the outputElement to make it available for the next call\n",
              "  // to uploadFilesContinue from Python.\n",
              "  outputElement.steps = steps;\n",
              "\n",
              "  return _uploadFilesContinue(outputId);\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "// This is roughly an async generator (not supported in the browser yet),\n",
              "// where there are multiple asynchronous steps and the Python side is going\n",
              "// to poll for completion of each step.\n",
              "// This uses a Promise to block the python side on completion of each step,\n",
              "// then passes the result of the previous step as the input to the next step.\n",
              "function _uploadFilesContinue(outputId) {\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  const steps = outputElement.steps;\n",
              "\n",
              "  const next = steps.next(outputElement.lastPromiseValue);\n",
              "  return Promise.resolve(next.value.promise).then((value) => {\n",
              "    // Cache the last promise value to make it available to the next\n",
              "    // step of the generator.\n",
              "    outputElement.lastPromiseValue = value;\n",
              "    return next.value.response;\n",
              "  });\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "/**\n",
              " * Generator function which is called between each async step of the upload\n",
              " * process.\n",
              " * @param {string} inputId Element ID of the input file picker element.\n",
              " * @param {string} outputId Element ID of the output display.\n",
              " * @return {!Iterable<!Object>} Iterable of next steps.\n",
              " */\n",
              "function* uploadFilesStep(inputId, outputId) {\n",
              "  const inputElement = document.getElementById(inputId);\n",
              "  inputElement.disabled = false;\n",
              "\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  outputElement.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "\n",
              "  const pickedPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "    inputElement.addEventListener('change', (e) => {\n",
              "      resolve(e.target.files);\n",
              "    });\n",
              "  });\n",
              "\n",
              "  const cancel = document.createElement('button');\n",
              "  inputElement.parentElement.appendChild(cancel);\n",
              "  cancel.textContent = 'Cancel upload';\n",
              "  const cancelPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "    cancel.onclick = () => {\n",
              "      resolve(null);\n",
              "    };\n",
              "  });\n",
              "\n",
              "  // Wait for the user to pick the files.\n",
              "  const files = yield {\n",
              "    promise: Promise.race([pickedPromise, cancelPromise]),\n",
              "    response: {\n",
              "      action: 'starting',\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  };\n",
              "\n",
              "  cancel.remove();\n",
              "\n",
              "  // Disable the input element since further picks are not allowed.\n",
              "  inputElement.disabled = true;\n",
              "\n",
              "  if (!files) {\n",
              "    return {\n",
              "      response: {\n",
              "        action: 'complete',\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    };\n",
              "  }\n",
              "\n",
              "  for (const file of files) {\n",
              "    const li = document.createElement('li');\n",
              "    li.append(span(file.name, {fontWeight: 'bold'}));\n",
              "    li.append(span(\n",
              "        `(${file.type || 'n/a'}) - ${file.size} bytes, ` +\n",
              "        `last modified: ${\n",
              "            file.lastModifiedDate ? file.lastModifiedDate.toLocaleDateString() :\n",
              "                                    'n/a'} - `));\n",
              "    const percent = span('0% done');\n",
              "    li.appendChild(percent);\n",
              "\n",
              "    outputElement.appendChild(li);\n",
              "\n",
              "    const fileDataPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "      const reader = new FileReader();\n",
              "      reader.onload = (e) => {\n",
              "        resolve(e.target.result);\n",
              "      };\n",
              "      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);\n",
              "    });\n",
              "    // Wait for the data to be ready.\n",
              "    let fileData = yield {\n",
              "      promise: fileDataPromise,\n",
              "      response: {\n",
              "        action: 'continue',\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    };\n",
              "\n",
              "    // Use a chunked sending to avoid message size limits. See b/62115660.\n",
              "    let position = 0;\n",
              "    do {\n",
              "      const length = Math.min(fileData.byteLength - position, MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE);\n",
              "      const chunk = new Uint8Array(fileData, position, length);\n",
              "      position += length;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const base64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode.apply(null, chunk));\n",
              "      yield {\n",
              "        response: {\n",
              "          action: 'append',\n",
              "          file: file.name,\n",
              "          data: base64,\n",
              "        },\n",
              "      };\n",
              "\n",
              "      let percentDone = fileData.byteLength === 0 ?\n",
              "          100 :\n",
              "          Math.round((position / fileData.byteLength) * 100);\n",
              "      percent.textContent = `${percentDone}% done`;\n",
              "\n",
              "    } while (position < fileData.byteLength);\n",
              "  }\n",
              "\n",
              "  // All done.\n",
              "  yield {\n",

            "sequential nature precludes parallelization within training examples, which becomes critical at longer\n",
            "sequence lengths, as memory constraints limit batching across examples. Recent work has achieved\n",

            "computation [32], while also improving model performance in case of the latter. The fundamental\n",
            "constraint of sequential computation, however, remains.\n",

            "the two sub-layers, followed by layer normalization [ 1]. That is, the output of each sub-layer is\n",
            "LayerNorm(x + Sublayer(x)), where Sublayer(x) is the function implemented by the sub-layer\n",
            "itself. To facilitate these residual connections, all sub-layers in the model, as well as the embedding\n",
            "layers, produce outputs of dimension dmodel = 512.\n",
            "Decoder: The decoder is also composed of a stack of N = 6identical layers. In addition to the two\n",
            "sub-layers in each encoder layer, the decoder inserts a third sub-layer, which performs multi-head\n",
            "attention over the output of the encoder stack. Similar to the encoder, we employ residual connections\n",
            "around each of the sub-layers, followed by layer normalization. We also modify the self-attention\n",

        "\n",
        "# AI Research Scientist — Paper Search Engine\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_research_paper(pages, keyword):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Find occurrences of a keyword in extracted PDF pages.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    keyword = keyword.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not keyword:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a search keyword.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages:\n",
        "        text = page[\"text\"]\n",
        "        lower_text = text.lower()\n",
        "        search_term = keyword.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        position = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        while True:\n",
        "            position = lower_text.find(search_term, position)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if position == -1:\n",
        "                break\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Capture text surrounding the keyword\n",
        "            start = max(0, position - 150)\n",
        "            end = min(len(text), position + len(keyword) + 150)\n",
        "\n",
        "            results.append({\n",
        "                \"page\": page[\"page\"],\n",
        "                \"keyword\": keyword,\n",
        "                \"context\": text[start:end].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "            position += len(keyword)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Search the PDF that you already uploaded\n",
        "keyword = \"neural network\"\n",
        "\n",
        "results = search_research_paper(extracted_pages, keyword)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"RESEARCH PAPER SEARCH\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "print(\"Keyword:\", keyword)\n",
        "print(\"Matching passages:\", len(results))\n",
        "\n",
        "for number, result in enumerate(results[:5], start=1):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nResult {number} — Page {result['page']}\")\n",
        "    print(result[\"context\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "if not results:\n",
        "    print(\"No matching passages found. Try another keyword.\")\n",
        "elif len(results) > 5:\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nShowing 5 of {len(results)} matches.\")"
      ],

        "\n",
        "import re\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def find_research_evidence(pages, question, max_results=5):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Find paper passages containing important words from a question.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    question = question.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Extract useful words and ignore common words\n",
        "    stop_words = {\n",
        "        \"the\", \"is\", \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"does\", \"do\",\n",
        "        \"a\", \"an\", \"of\", \"to\", \"in\", \"on\", \"for\", \"and\",\n",
        "        \"or\", \"how\", \"what\", \"why\", \"can\", \"could\", \"does\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    words = re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\", question.lower())\n",
        "    keywords = list(dict.fromkeys(\n",
        "        word for word in words if word not in stop_words\n",
        "    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not keywords:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\n",
        "            \"Please include meaningful words in your question.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    matches = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages:\n",
        "        text = page[\"text\"]\n",
        "        sentences = re.split(r\"(?<=[.!?])\\s+\", text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        for sentence in sentences:\n",
        "            sentence_lower = sentence.lower()\n",
        "            matched_words = [\n",
        "                word for word in keywords if word in sentence_lower\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            if matched_words:\n",
        "                matches.append({\n",
        "                    \"page\": page[\"page\"],\n",
        "                    \"sentence\": sentence.strip(),\n",
        "                    \"matched_keywords\": matched_words,\n",
        "                    \"score\": len(matched_words)\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Show passages matching the greatest number of keywords first\n",
        "    matches.sort(key=lambda item: item[\"score\"], reverse=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return keywords, matches[:max_results]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test the evidence finder on your uploaded PDF\n",
        "question = \"How do neural networks learn?\"\n",
        "\n",
        "keywords, evidence = find_research_evidence(\n",
        "    extracted_pages,\n",
        "    question\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"RESEARCH QUESTION:\", question)\n",
        "print(\"SEARCH KEYWORDS:\", keywords)\n",
        "print(\"\\nRELEVANT EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "\n",
        "if evidence:\n",
        "    for number, item in enumerate(evidence, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nEvidence {number} — Page {item['page']}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Matched words:\", \", \".join(item[\"matched_keywords\"]))\n",
        "        print(item[\"sentence\"])\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No matching passages found in the extracted text.\")"
      ],

      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import re\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def generate_evidence_report(pages, research_question, max_results=5):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Create a report of passages related to a research question.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not research_question.strip():\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if max_results < 1:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"max_results must be at least 1.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    stop_words = {\n",
        "        \"the\", \"is\", \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"does\", \"did\",\n",
        "        \"do\", \"a\", \"an\", \"of\", \"to\", \"in\", \"on\", \"for\",\n",
        "        \"and\", \"or\", \"how\", \"what\", \"why\", \"can\", \"could\",\n",
        "        \"would\", \"should\", \"with\", \"from\", \"this\", \"that\",\n",
        "        \"it\", \"by\", \"be\", \"about\", \"which\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    words = re.findall(\n",
        "        r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\",\n",
        "        research_question.lower()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    keywords = list(dict.fromkeys(\n",
        "        word for word in words if word not in stop_words\n",
        "    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not keywords:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\n",
        "            \"Your question must contain meaningful search words.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    evidence = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages:\n",
        "        # Split extracted page text into sentence-like passages\n",
        "        sentences = re.split(\n",
        "            r\"(?<=[.!?])\\s+|\\n+\",\n",
        "            page[\"text\"]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        for sentence in sentences:\n",
        "            sentence = sentence.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not sentence:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            sentence_lower = sentence.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "            matched = [\n",
        "                word for word in keywords\n",
        "                if re.search(\n",
        "                    r\"\\b\" + re.escape(word) + r\"\\b\",\n",
        "                    sentence_lower\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            if matched:\n",
        "                evidence.append({\n",
        "                    \"page\": page[\"page\"],\n",
        "                    \"passage\": sentence,\n",
        "                    \"matched_keywords\": matched,\n",
        "                    \"score\": len(matched)\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Rank passages by the number of distinct matching keywords\n",
        "    evidence.sort(\n",
        "        key=lambda item: item[\"score\"],\n",
        "        reverse=True\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    selected = evidence[:max_results]\n",
        "\n",
        "    report = {\n",
        "        \"research_question\": research_question,\n",
        "        \"keywords\": keywords,\n",
        "        \"evidence\": selected,\n",
        "        \"total_matches\": len(evidence)\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    return report\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Generate a report from your uploaded PDF\n",
        "report = generate_evidence_report(\n",
        "    extracted_pages,\n",
        "    \"How do neural networks learn?\",\n",
        "    max_results=5\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"RESEARCH QUESTION:\")\n",
        "print(report[\"research_question\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nSEARCH KEYWORDS:\")\n",
        "print(\", \".join(report[\"keywords\"]))\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nTOTAL MATCHING PASSAGES:\")\n",
        "print(report[\"total_matches\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nRETRIEVED EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "if report[\"evidence\"]:\n",
        "    for number, item in enumerate(report[\"evidence\"], start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nEvidence {number}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Source page:\", item[\"page\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Relevance score:\", item[\"score\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Matched keywords:\",\n",
        "              \", \".join(item[\"matched_keywords\"]))\n",
        "        print(\"Passage:\", item[\"passage\"])\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No matching passages found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nLIMITATION\")\n",
        "print(\"Keyword matches are not proof that a passage answers\")\n",
        "print(\"the question. Each passage must be checked in context.\")"
      ],

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create tables for research papers and retrieved evidence\n",
        "def initialize_evidence_database():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research_papers (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                filename TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n",
        "                page_count INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                character_count INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                added_at TEXT NOT NULL\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research_evidence (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                paper_id INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                research_question TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                source_page INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                passage TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                relevance_score INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                matched_keywords TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                FOREIGN KEY (paper_id)\n",
        "                    REFERENCES research_papers(id),\n",
        "                UNIQUE (\n",
        "                    paper_id, research_question, source_page,\n",
        "                    passage\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save paper information and retrieved evidence\n",
        "def save_evidence_report(filename, pages, report):\n",
        "    character_count = sum(len(page[\"text\"]) for page in pages)\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO research_papers\n",
        "                (filename, page_count, character_count, added_at)\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "            ON CONFLICT(filename) DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                page_count = excluded.page_count,\n",
        "                character_count = excluded.character_count\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            filename,\n",
        "            len(pages),\n",
        "            character_count,\n",
        "            datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        paper = connection.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT id FROM research_papers WHERE filename = ?\",\n",
        "            (filename,)\n",
        "        ).fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "        paper_id = paper[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        for item in report[\"evidence\"]:\n",
        "            connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO research_evidence (\n",
        "                    paper_id,\n",
        "                    research_question,\n",
        "                    source_page,\n",
        "                    passage,\n",
        "                    relevance_score,\n",
        "                    matched_keywords\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "            \"\"\", (\n",
        "                paper_id,\n",
        "                report[\"research_question\"],\n",
        "                item[\"page\"],\n",
        "                item[\"passage\"],\n",
        "                item[\"score\"],\n",
        "                json.dumps(item[\"matched_keywords\"])\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return paper_id\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Initialize the database\n",
        "initialize_evidence_database()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save the report created in the previous code cell\n",
        "paper_id = save_evidence_report(\n",
        "    filename,\n",
        "    extracted_pages,\n",
        "    report\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Research evidence saved successfully!\")\n",
        "print(\"Paper ID:\", paper_id)\n",
        "print(\"Paper filename:\", filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Retrieve the saved evidence\n",
        "with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "    saved_evidence = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT source_page, relevance_score, passage\n",
        "        FROM research_evidence\n",
        "        WHERE paper_id = ? AND research_question = ?\n",
        "        ORDER BY relevance_score DESC, source_page\n",
        "    \"\"\", (\n",
        "        paper_id,\n",
        "        report[\"research_question\"]\n",
        "    )).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nSAVED EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "print(\"Number of saved passages:\", len(saved_evidence))\n",
        "\n",
        "for page, score, passage in saved_evidence:\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nPage {page} | Score {score}\")\n",
        "    print(passage)"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def retrieve_saved_evidence(search_term, limit=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Search evidence saved in the research database.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    search_term = search_term.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not search_term:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a search term.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if limit < 1:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"The result limit must be at least 1.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                p.filename,\n",
        "                e.research_question,\n",
        "                e.source_page,\n",
        "                e.passage,\n",
        "                e.relevance_score,\n",
        "                e.matched_keywords\n",
        "            FROM research_evidence AS e\n",
        "            JOIN research_papers AS p\n",
        "                ON e.paper_id = p.id\n",
        "            WHERE\n",
        "                e.passage LIKE ?\n",
        "                OR e.research_question LIKE ?\n",
        "            ORDER BY e.relevance_score DESC\n",
        "            LIMIT ?\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            f\"%{search_term}%\",\n",
        "            f\"%{search_term}%\",\n",
        "            limit\n",
        "        )).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        results.append({\n",
        "            \"filename\": row[0],\n",
        "            \"question\": row[1],\n",
        "            \"page\": row[2],\n",
        "            \"passage\": row[3],\n",
        "            \"score\": row[4],\n",
        "            \"keywords\": json.loads(row[5])\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Search previously saved evidence\n",
        "\n",
        "search_term = \"neural\"\n",
        "\n",
        "results = retrieve_saved_evidence(search_term)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"SAVED RESEARCH EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "print(\"Search term:\", search_term)\n",
        "print(\"Results found:\", len(results))\n",
        "\n",
        "for number, item in enumerate(results, start=1):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nResult {number}\")\n",
        "    print(\"Paper:\", item[\"filename\"])\n",
        "    print(\"Research question:\", item[\"question\"])\n",
        "    print(\"Source page:\", item[\"page\"])\n",
        "    print(\"Relevance score:\", item[\"score\"])\n",
        "    print(\"Matched keywords:\", \", \".join(item[\"keywords\"]))\n",
        "    print(\"Passage:\", item[\"passage\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "if not results:\n",
        "    print(\"No saved evidence matched this search.\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {

        "    print(\"Passage:\", item[\"passage\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "if not results:\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "\n",
        "def answer_research_question(question, max_results=5):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Retrieve relevant saved evidence for a research question.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    question = question.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if max_results < 1:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"max_results must be at least 1.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    stop_words = {\n",
        "        \"the\", \"is\", \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"does\", \"did\",\n",
        "        \"do\", \"a\", \"an\", \"of\", \"to\", \"in\", \"on\", \"for\",\n",
        "        \"and\", \"or\", \"how\", \"what\", \"why\", \"can\", \"could\",\n",
        "        \"would\", \"should\", \"with\", \"from\", \"this\", \"that\",\n",
        "        \"it\", \"by\", \"be\", \"about\", \"which\", \"learn\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    words = re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\", question.lower())\n",
        "    keywords = list(dict.fromkeys(\n",
        "        word for word in words if word not in stop_words\n",
        "    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not keywords:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\n",
        "            \"Please ask a question containing meaningful keywords.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                p.filename,\n",
        "                e.source_page,\n",
        "                e.passage,\n",
        "                e.relevance_score\n",
        "            FROM research_evidence AS e\n",
        "            JOIN research_papers AS p\n",
        "                ON e.paper_id = p.id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for filename, page, passage, old_score in rows:\n",
        "        passage_lower = passage.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        matched = [\n",
        "            word for word in keywords\n",
        "            if re.search(\n",
        "                r\"\\b\" + re.escape(word) + r\"\\b\",\n",
        "                passage_lower\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if matched:\n",
        "            results.append({\n",
        "                \"filename\": filename,\n",
        "                \"page\": page,\n",
        "                \"passage\": passage,\n",
        "                \"score\": len(matched),\n",
        "                \"matched_keywords\": matched\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "    results.sort(\n",
        "        key=lambda item: item[\"score\"],\n",
        "        reverse=True\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"question\": question,\n",
        "        \"keywords\": keywords,\n",
        "        \"evidence\": results[:max_results],\n",
        "        \"total_matches\": len(results)\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Try asking a research question\n",
        "question = \"How do neural networks learn?\"\n",
        "\n",
        "answer = answer_research_question(question)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"Research question:\", answer[\"question\"])\n",
        "print(\"Search keywords:\", \", \".join(answer[\"keywords\"]))\n",
        "print(\"Matching passages:\", answer[\"total_matches\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nRETRIEVED EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "if answer[\"evidence\"]:\n",
        "    for number, item in enumerate(answer[\"evidence\"], start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nEvidence {number}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Paper:\", item[\"filename\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Source page:\", item[\"page\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Matched keywords:\",\n",
        "              \", \".join(item[\"matched_keywords\"]))\n",
        "        print(\"Passage:\", item[\"passage\"])\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No relevant saved passages were found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nIMPORTANT LIMITATION\")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"These passages are possible evidence, not a verified answer. \"\n",
        "    \"Check the original paper before drawing a conclusion.\"\n",
        ")"
      ],
      "metadata": {

        "def evaluate_research_evidence(answer):\n",
        "    evidence = answer[\"evidence\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"EVIDENCE EVALUATION\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "    print(\"Research question:\", answer[\"question\"])\n",
        "    print(\"Passages retrieved:\", len(evidence))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not evidence:\n",
        "        print(\"Status: No evidence retrieved.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Action: Try another question or upload another paper.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    supported_keywords = set()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for item in evidence:\n",
        "        supported_keywords.update(item[\"matched_keywords\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Question keywords:\", \", \".join(answer[\"keywords\"]))\n",
        "    print(\"Keywords found in evidence:\", \", \".join(sorted(supported_keywords)))\n",
        "\n",
        "    coverage = (\n",
        "        len(supported_keywords) / len(answer[\"keywords\"]) * 100\n",
        "        if answer[\"keywords\"] else 0\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Keyword coverage: {coverage:.1f}%\")\n",
        "    print(\"\\nEvaluation:\")\n",
        "    print(\"Retrieved passages contain some matching keywords.\")\n",
        "    print(\"This does not prove that they answer the question.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Read the original passages before drawing conclusions.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "evaluate_research_evidence(answer)"
      ],

        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "\n",
        "def generate_research_report(answer):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Generate a research report from retrieved evidence.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    question = answer[\"question\"]\n",
        "    keywords = answer[\"keywords\"]\n",
        "    evidence = answer[\"evidence\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = [\n",
        "        \"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 55,\n",
        "        \"RESEARCH EVIDENCE REPORT\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 55,\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"Research question:\",\n",
        "        question,\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"Search keywords:\",\n",
        "        \", \".join(keywords),\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"Report generated (UTC):\",\n",
        "        datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"),\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"EVIDENCE SUMMARY\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 55,\n",
        "        f\"Matching passages retrieved: {answer['total_matches']}\",\n",
        "        f\"Passages included in this report: {len(evidence)}\",\n",
        "        \"\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if evidence:\n",
        "        for number, item in enumerate(evidence, start=1):\n",
        "            lines.extend([\n",
        "                f\"Evidence {number}\",\n",
        "                f\"Paper: {item['filename']}\",\n",
        "                f\"Source page: {item['page']}\",\n",
        "                f\"Matched keywords: \"\n",
        "                f\"{', '.join(item['matched_keywords'])}\",\n",
        "                f\"Passage: {item['passage']}\",\n",
        "                \"\"\n",
        "            ])\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        lines.extend([\n",
        "            \"No matching evidence was found in the saved database.\",\n",
        "            \"\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.extend([\n",
        "        \"LIMITATIONS\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 55,\n",
        "        \"Evidence was retrieved using keyword matching.\",\n",
        "        \"Retrieved passages may not answer the research question.\",\n",
        "        \"The report does not establish scientific proof or causation.\",\n",
        "        \"Verify each passage in the original paper before citing it.\",\n",
        "        \"\"\n",
        "    ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "research_report = generate_research_report(answer)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(research_report)"

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}

        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_paper_library():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS paper_library (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                filename TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n",
        "                file_path TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                page_count INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                character_count INTEGER NOT NULL\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def add_paper_to_library(filename, file_path, pages):\n",
        "    path = Path(file_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not path.exists():\n",
        "        raise FileNotFoundError(\"The PDF file could not be found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if path.suffix.lower() != \".pdf\":\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Only PDF files can be added.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not pages:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"No PDF pages were extracted.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_count = len(pages)\n",
        "    character_count = sum(len(page[\"text\"]) for page in pages)\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO paper_library (\n",
        "                filename, file_path, page_count, character_count\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "            ON CONFLICT(filename) DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                file_path = excluded.file_path,\n",
        "                page_count = excluded.page_count,\n",
        "                character_count = excluded.character_count\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            filename,\n",
        "            str(path),\n",
        "            page_count,\n",
        "            character_count\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Paper added to the library!\")\n",
        "    print(\"Filename:\", filename)\n",
        "    print(\"Pages:\", page_count)\n",
        "    print(\"Extracted characters:\", character_count)\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_paper_library():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        papers = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, filename, page_count, character_count\n",
        "            FROM paper_library\n",
        "            ORDER BY id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nRESEARCH PAPER LIBRARY\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 50)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not papers:\n",
        "        print(\"The library is empty.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper_id, filename, pages, characters in papers:\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nPaper ID: {paper_id}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Filename:\", filename)\n",
        "        print(\"Pages:\", pages)\n",
        "        print(\"Extracted characters:\", characters)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nTotal papers:\", len(papers))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "initialize_paper_library()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Add the PDF already extracted in your notebook.\n",
        "add_paper_to_library(\n",
        "    filename,\n",
        "    filename,\n",
        "    extracted_pages\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "show_paper_library()"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_all_research_papers(question, max_results=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Search saved evidence across multiple research papers.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    question = question.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if max_results < 1:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"max_results must be at least 1.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    stop_words = {\n",
        "        \"the\", \"is\", \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"does\", \"did\",\n",
        "        \"do\", \"a\", \"an\", \"of\", \"to\", \"in\", \"on\", \"for\",\n",
        "        \"and\", \"or\", \"how\", \"what\", \"why\", \"can\", \"could\",\n",
        "        \"would\", \"should\", \"with\", \"from\", \"this\", \"that\",\n",
        "        \"it\", \"by\", \"be\", \"about\", \"which\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    words = re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\", question.lower())\n",
        "    keywords = list(dict.fromkeys(\n",
        "        word for word in words if word not in stop_words\n",
        "    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not keywords:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please include meaningful search words.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                p.filename,\n",
        "                e.research_question,\n",
        "                e.source_page,\n",
        "                e.passage,\n",
        "                e.matched_keywords\n",
        "            FROM research_evidence AS e\n",
        "            JOIN research_papers AS p\n",
        "                ON e.paper_id = p.id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for filename, saved_question, page, passage, saved_keywords in rows:\n",
        "        passage_lower = passage.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        matched = [\n",
        "            word for word in keywords\n",
        "            if re.search(\n",
        "                r\"\\b\" + re.escape(word) + r\"\\b\",\n",
        "                passage_lower\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if matched:\n",
        "            results.append({\n",
        "                \"paper\": filename,\n",
        "                \"saved_question\": saved_question,\n",
        "                \"page\": page,\n",
        "                \"passage\": passage,\n",
        "                \"matched_keywords\": matched,\n",
        "                \"score\": len(matched)\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "    results.sort(key=lambda item: item[\"score\"], reverse=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"question\": question,\n",
        "        \"keywords\": keywords,\n",
        "        \"total_matches\": len(results),\n",
        "        \"results\": results[:max_results]\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test the multi-paper search\n",
        "search_results = search_all_research_papers(\n",
        "    \"How do neural networks learn?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"Research question:\", search_results[\"question\"])\n",
        "print(\"Keywords:\", \", \".join(search_results[\"keywords\"]))\n",
        "print(\"Matching passages:\", search_results[\"total_matches\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nSEARCH RESULTS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "if search_results[\"results\"]:\n",
        "    for number, item in enumerate(search_results[\"results\"], start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nResult {number}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Paper:\", item[\"paper\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Page:\", item[\"page\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Matched keywords:\", \", \".join(item[\"matched_keywords\"]))\n",
        "        print(\"Passage:\", item[\"passage\"])\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No matching saved evidence was found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nNote: Results are keyword matches, not verified scientific answers.\")"

        "import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_knowledge_graph():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS knowledge_nodes (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                node_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                UNIQUE(name, node_type)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS knowledge_edges (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                source_id INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                relation TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                target_id INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                FOREIGN KEY(source_id) REFERENCES knowledge_nodes(id),\n",
        "                FOREIGN KEY(target_id) REFERENCES knowledge_nodes(id),\n",
        "                UNIQUE(source_id, relation, target_id)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def add_knowledge_node(name, node_type):\n",
        "    name = name.strip()\n",
        "    node_type = node_type.strip().lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not name or not node_type:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Node name and type are required.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO knowledge_nodes (name, node_type)\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (name, node_type))\n",
        "\n",
        "        node = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id FROM knowledge_nodes\n",
        "            WHERE name = ? AND node_type = ?\n",
        "        \"\"\", (name, node_type)).fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return node[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def connect_knowledge_nodes(source_id, relation, target_id):\n",
        "    relation = relation.strip().lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not relation:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please provide a relationship.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO knowledge_edges\n",
        "                (source_id, relation, target_id)\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (source_id, relation, target_id))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_knowledge_graph():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                source.name,\n",
        "                source.node_type,\n",
        "                edges.relation,\n",
        "                target.name,\n",
        "                target.node_type\n",
        "            FROM knowledge_edges AS edges\n",
        "            JOIN knowledge_nodes AS source\n",
        "                ON source.id = edges.source_id\n",
        "            JOIN knowledge_nodes AS target\n",
        "                ON target.id = edges.target_id\n",
        "            ORDER BY source.name, target.name\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"RESEARCH KNOWLEDGE GRAPH\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No relationships have been created yet.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    for source, source_type, relation, target, target_type in rows:\n",
        "        print(f\"{source} [{source_type}]\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  -- {relation} -->\")\n",
        "        print(f\"{target} [{target_type}]\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Initialize the graph tables\n",
        "initialize_knowledge_graph()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create example research concepts\n",
        "question_id = add_knowledge_node(\n",
        "    \"How do neural networks learn?\",\n",
        "    \"research_question\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "concept_id = add_knowledge_node(\n",
        "    \"Neural Networks\",\n",
        "    \"concept\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "learning_id = add_knowledge_node(\n",
        "    \"Learning Rate\",\n",
        "    \"concept\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Connect the concepts\n",
        "connect_knowledge_nodes(\n",
        "    question_id, \"investigates\", concept_id\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "connect_knowledge_nodes(\n",
        "    concept_id, \"uses_parameter\", learning_id\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Display the graph\n",
        "show_knowledge_graph()"
      ],

        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "\n",
        "def link_saved_research_to_graph():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Connect saved papers and evidence to knowledge graph nodes.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        papers = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, filename\n",
        "            FROM research_papers\n",
        "            ORDER BY id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        evidence_rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                p.id,\n",
        "                p.filename,\n",
        "                e.research_question,\n",
        "                e.source_page,\n",
        "                e.passage\n",
        "            FROM research_evidence AS e\n",
        "            JOIN research_papers AS p\n",
        "                ON p.id = e.paper_id\n",
        "            ORDER BY p.id, e.source_page\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    linked = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper_id, filename in papers:\n",
        "        paper_node = add_knowledge_node(filename, \"paper\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        evidence_for_paper = [\n",
        "            row for row in evidence_rows if row[0] == paper_id\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        for _, _, question, page, passage in evidence_for_paper:\n",
        "            question_node = add_knowledge_node(\n",
        "                question, \"research_question\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            connect_knowledge_nodes(\n",
        "                question_node,\n",
        "                \"uses_evidence_from\",\n",
        "                paper_node\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            evidence_name = f\"{filename} — Page {page}: {passage[:100]}\"\n",
        "            evidence_node = add_knowledge_node(\n",
        "                evidence_name, \"evidence\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            connect_knowledge_nodes(\n",
        "                paper_node,\n",
        "                \"contains_evidence\",\n",
        "                evidence_node\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            connect_knowledge_nodes(\n",
        "                evidence_node,\n",
        "                \"relevant_to\",\n",
        "                question_node\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            linked += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"RESEARCH GRAPH INTEGRATION\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "    print(\"Papers registered:\", len(papers))\n",
        "    print(\"Evidence records processed:\", linked)\n",
        "    print(\"Graph updated successfully.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    show_knowledge_graph()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "link_saved_research_to_graph()"
      ],

      "source": [
        "import re\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from collections import Counter\n",
        "\n",
        "def extract_research_concepts(pages, top_n=15):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Extract frequently occurring candidate concepts from PDF text.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if top_n < 1:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"top_n must be at least 1.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    stop_words = {\n",
        "        \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"that\", \"with\", \"from\", \"this\",\n",
        "        \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"has\", \"have\", \"had\", \"not\",\n",
        "        \"but\", \"can\", \"may\", \"will\", \"would\", \"could\", \"should\",\n",
        "        \"their\", \"there\", \"which\", \"when\", \"where\", \"what\",\n",
        "        \"how\", \"why\", \"than\", \"then\", \"into\", \"also\", \"using\",\n",
        "        \"used\", \"use\", \"based\", \"such\", \"these\", \"those\",\n",
        "        \"our\", \"its\", \"they\", \"them\", \"been\", \"being\",\n",
        "        \"between\", \"each\", \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\",\n",
        "        \"research\", \"results\", \"figure\", \"table\", \"section\",\n",
        "        \"paper\", \"study\", \"method\", \"methods\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    counts = Counter()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages:\n",
        "        text = page[\"text\"].lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Find candidate single-word scientific terms.\n",
        "        words = re.findall(r\"\\b[a-z][a-z-]{3,}\\b\", text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        for word in words:\n",
        "            if word not in stop_words:\n",
        "                counts[word] += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        {\"concept\": word, \"frequency\": count}\n",
        "        for word, count in counts.most_common(top_n)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_concepts_to_graph(filename, concepts):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Save candidate concepts and connect them to their paper.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    paper_node = add_knowledge_node(filename, \"paper\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for item in concepts:\n",
        "        concept_node = add_knowledge_node(\n",
        "            item[\"concept\"], \"candidate_concept\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        connect_knowledge_nodes(\n",
        "            paper_node,\n",
        "            \"mentions\",\n",
        "            concept_node\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Candidate concepts saved:\", len(concepts))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Use the PDF text extracted earlier in your notebook.\n",
        "concepts = extract_research_concepts(extracted_pages, top_n=15)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AUTOMATIC CONCEPT EXTRACTION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "\n",
        "for number, item in enumerate(concepts, start=1):\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"{number}. {item['concept']} \"\n",
        "        f\"(occurrences: {item['frequency']})\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "save_concepts_to_graph(filename, concepts)"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_research_concept(term, limit=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Find papers and saved evidence related to a search term.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    term = term.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not term:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a concept to search for.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if limit < 1:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"The result limit must be at least 1.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        # Find matching concepts in the knowledge graph.\n",
        "        concepts = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, name, node_type\n",
        "            FROM knowledge_nodes\n",
        "            WHERE name LIKE ?\n",
        "            ORDER BY name\n",
        "        \"\"\", (f\"%{term}%\",)).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Find papers whose saved evidence mentions the term.\n",
        "        evidence = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                p.filename,\n",
        "                e.research_question,\n",
        "                e.source_page,\n",
        "                e.passage\n",
        "            FROM research_evidence AS e\n",
        "            JOIN research_papers AS p\n",
        "                ON p.id = e.paper_id\n",
        "            WHERE e.passage LIKE ?\n",
        "               OR e.research_question LIKE ?\n",
        "               OR p.filename LIKE ?\n",
        "            ORDER BY e.relevance_score DESC, p.filename\n",
        "            LIMIT ?\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            f\"%{term}%\",\n",
        "            f\"%{term}%\",\n",
        "            f\"%{term}%\",\n",
        "            limit\n",
        "        )).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return concepts, evidence\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Try searching your research library.\n",
        "search_term = \"neural\"\n",
        "\n",
        "concepts, evidence = search_research_concept(search_term)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"Concept search:\", search_term)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nMATCHING GRAPH CONCEPTS\")\n",
        "print(\"-\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "if concepts:\n",
        "    for node_id, name, node_type in concepts:\n",
        "        print(f\"ID: {node_id} | {name} [{node_type}]\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No matching concepts found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nRELATED PAPERS AND EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "print(\"-\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "if evidence:\n",
        "    for number, item in enumerate(evidence, start=1):\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_research_concept(term, limit=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Find papers and saved evidence related to a search term.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    term = term.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not term:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a concept to search for.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if limit < 1:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"The result limit must be at least 1.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        # Find matching concepts in the knowledge graph.\n",
        "        concepts = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, name, node_type\n",
        "            FROM knowledge_nodes\n",
        "            WHERE name LIKE ?\n",
        "            ORDER BY name\n",
        "        \"\"\", (f\"%{term}%\",)).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Find papers whose saved evidence mentions the term.\n",
        "        evidence = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                p.filename,\n",
        "                e.research_question,\n",
        "                e.source_page,\n",
        "                e.passage\n",
        "            FROM research_evidence AS e\n",
        "            JOIN research_papers AS p\n",
        "                ON p.id = e.paper_id\n",
        "            WHERE e.passage LIKE ?\n",
        "               OR e.research_question LIKE ?\n",
        "               OR p.filename LIKE ?\n",
        "            ORDER BY e.relevance_score DESC, p.filename\n",
        "            LIMIT ?\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            f\"%{term}%\",\n",
        "            f\"%{term}%\",\n",
        "            f\"%{term}%\",\n",
        "            limit\n",
        "        )).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return concepts, evidence\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Try searching your research library.\n",
        "search_term = \"learning\"\n",
        "\n",
        "concepts, evidence = search_research_concept(search_term)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"Concept search:\", search_term)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nMATCHING GRAPH CONCEPTS\")\n",
        "print(\"-\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "if concepts:\n",
        "    for node_id, name, node_type in concepts:\n",
        "        print(f\"ID: {node_id} | {name} [{node_type}]\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No matching concepts found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nRELATED PAPERS AND EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "print(\"-\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "if evidence:\n",
        "    for number, item in enumerate(evidence, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nResult {number}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Paper:\", item[0])\n",
        "        print(\"Research question:\", item[1])\n",
        "        print(\"Source page:\", item[2])\n",
        "        print(\"Passage:\", item[3])\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No matching saved evidence found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nNote: Search matches text; they do not establish scientific support.\")"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_experiment_tracker():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiments (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                hypothesis TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                settings TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                result TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                conclusion TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                created_at TEXT NOT NULL\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def record_experiment(\n",
        "    name,\n",
        "    hypothesis,\n",
        "    settings,\n",
        "    result,\n",
        "    conclusion\n",
        "):\n",
        "    fields = [name, hypothesis, settings, result, conclusion]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if any(not str(value).strip() for value in fields):\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please fill in every experiment field.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        cursor = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO experiments (\n",
        "                name,\n",
        "                hypothesis,\n",
        "                settings,\n",
        "                result,\n",
        "                conclusion,\n",
        "                created_at\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            name.strip(),\n",
        "            hypothesis.strip(),\n",
        "            settings.strip(),\n",
        "            result.strip(),\n",
        "            conclusion.strip(),\n",
        "            datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        return cursor.lastrowid\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_experiments():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        experiments = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, name, hypothesis, settings,\n",
        "                   result, conclusion, created_at\n",
        "            FROM experiments\n",
        "            ORDER BY id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — EXPERIMENTS\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not experiments:\n",
        "        print(\"No experiments recorded yet.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    for experiment in experiments:\n",
        "        (\n",
        "            experiment_id, name, hypothesis, settings,\n",
        "            result, conclusion, created_at\n",
        "        ) = experiment\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nExperiment ID: {experiment_id}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Name:\", name)\n",
        "        print(\"Hypothesis:\", hypothesis)\n",
        "        print(\"Settings:\", settings)\n",
        "        print(\"Result:\", result)\n",
        "        print(\"Conclusion:\", conclusion)\n",
        "        print(\"Recorded at:\", created_at)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Initialize the experiment database table.\n",
        "initialize_experiment_tracker()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Record a clearly labelled example experiment.\n",
        "experiment_id = record_experiment(\n",
        "    name=\"Learning Rate Comparison — Example\",\n",
        "    hypothesis=\"A learning rate of 0.01 may perform differently \"\n",
        "               \"from a learning rate of 0.001.\",\n",
        "    settings=\"Example only; no model was trained. \"\n",
        "             \"Learning rates to compare: 0.01 and 0.001.\",\n",
        "    result=\"Not measured.\",\n",
        "    conclusion=\"No conclusion yet. Run a controlled experiment \"\n",
        "               \"and record actual measurements.\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Example experiment saved. ID:\", experiment_id)\n",
        "print()\n",
        "show_experiments()"
      ],

        "%pip install -q scikit-learn"
