      "metadata": {
        "id": "2rH_goA-O5KT"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "from sklearn.datasets import load_digits\n",
        "from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Prepare a real handwritten-digit dataset.\n",
        "digits = load_digits()\n",
        "X = digits.data\n",
        "y = digits.target\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(\n",
        "    X,\n",
        "    y,\n",
        "    test_size=0.20,\n",
        "    random_state=42,\n",
        "    stratify=y\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Fit scaling only on the training data to avoid data leakage.\n",
        "scaler = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_train = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)\n",
        "X_test = scaler.transform(X_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. Ensure the experiment table exists.\n",
        "with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "    connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiments (\n",
        "            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "            name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "            hypothesis TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "            settings TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "            result TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "            conclusion TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "            created_at TEXT NOT NULL\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# 3. Train and evaluate two neural networks.\n",
        "learning_rates = [0.001, 0.01]\n",
        "results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for learning_rate in learning_rates:\n",
        "    model = MLPClassifier(\n",
        "        hidden_layer_sizes=(32,),\n",
        "        learning_rate_init=learning_rate,\n",
        "        max_iter=100,\n",
        "        random_state=42,\n",
        "        early_stopping=True\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    model.fit(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "    predictions = model.predict(X_test)\n",
        "    accuracy = accuracy_score(y_test, predictions)\n",
        "\n",
        "    results.append({\n",
        "        \"learning_rate\": learning_rate,\n",
        "        \"accuracy\": accuracy,\n",
        "        \"iterations\": model.n_iter_,\n",
        "        \"converged_within_limit\": model.n_iter_ < 100\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "# 4. Compare measured test accuracy.\n",
        "best_result = max(results, key=lambda item: item[\"accuracy\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"NEURAL NETWORK EXPERIMENT\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"Dataset: Handwritten digits\")\n",
        "print(\"Training examples:\", len(X_train))\n",
        "print(\"Test examples:\", len(X_test))\n",
        "\n",
        "for result in results:\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLearning rate: {result['learning_rate']}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Test accuracy: {result['accuracy']:.2%}\")\n",
        "    print(\"Training iterations:\", result[\"iterations\"])\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"Stopped before iteration limit:\",\n",
        "        result[\"converged_within_limit\"]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nHIGHEST TEST ACCURACY IN THIS RUN\")\n",
        "print(\"Learning rate:\", best_result[\"learning_rate\"])\n",
        "print(f\"Test accuracy: {best_result['accuracy']:.2%}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# 5. Save the measured experiment results.\n",
        "with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "    for result in results:\n",
        "        settings = {\n",
        "            \"learning_rate\": result[\"learning_rate\"],\n",
        "            \"hidden_layer_sizes\": [32],\n",
        "            \"max_iter\": 100,\n",
        "            \"random_state\": 42,\n",
        "            \"dataset\": \"sklearn handwritten digits\",\n",
        "            \"test_size\": 0.20\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO experiments (\n",
        "                name, hypothesis, settings, result,\n",
        "                conclusion, created_at\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            f\"Digits learning rate {result['learning_rate']}\",\n",
        "            \"Learning rate can affect neural network performance.\",\n",
        "            json.dumps(settings),\n",
        "            json.dumps({\n",
        "                \"test_accuracy\": result[\"accuracy\"],\n",
        "                \"iterations\": result[\"iterations\"],\n",
        "                \"converged_within_limit\":\n",
        "                    result[\"converged_within_limit\"]\n",
        "            }),\n",
        "            \"Measured result from one train/test split. \"\n",
        "            \"Repeat across multiple random seeds before drawing \"\n",
        "            \"a general conclusion.\",\n",
        "            datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nBoth experiment results were saved to research.db.\")"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "from IPython.display import display\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Load the saved neural-network experiments.\n",
        "with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "    rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT id, name, settings, result, created_at\n",
        "        FROM experiments\n",
        "        WHERE name LIKE 'Digits learning rate %'\n",
        "        ORDER BY id\n",
        "    \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. Prepare the results for display.\n",
        "records = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for experiment_id, name, settings_text, result_text, created_at in rows:\n",
        "    settings = json.loads(settings_text)\n",
        "    result = json.loads(result_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "    records.append({\n",
        "        \"Experiment ID\": experiment_id,\n",
        "        \"Learning Rate\": settings[\"learning_rate\"],\n",
        "        \"Test Accuracy (%)\": round(\n",
        "            result[\"test_accuracy\"] * 100, 2\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"Training Iterations\": result[\"iterations\"],\n",
        "        \"Recorded At (UTC)\": created_at\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"EXPERIMENT COMPARISON DASHBOARD\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if not records:\n",
        "    print(\"\\nNo matching experiment results found.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Run the neural-network experiment cell first.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    df = pd.DataFrame(records)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nRESULTS TABLE\")\n",
        "    display(df)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 3. Compare average accuracy for each learning rate.\n",
        "    summary = (\n",
        "        df.groupby(\"Learning Rate\", as_index=False)\n",
        "        [\"Test Accuracy (%)\"].mean()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nAVERAGE TEST ACCURACY BY LEARNING RATE\")\n",
        "    display(summary)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 4. Plot the measured results.\n",
        "    plt.figure(figsize=(8, 5))\n",
        "    plt.bar(\n",
        "        summary[\"Learning Rate\"].astype(str),\n",
        "        summary[\"Test Accuracy (%)\"]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    plt.xlabel(\"Learning Rate\")\n",
        "    plt.ylabel(\"Test Accuracy (%)\")\n",
        "    plt.title(\"Neural Network: Learning Rate Comparison\")\n",
        "    plt.ylim(0, 100)\n",
        "    plt.grid(axis=\"y\", alpha=0.3)\n",
        "    plt.show()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nScientific note: these are results from the recorded runs, \"\n",
        "        \"not proof that one learning rate is always superior.\"\n",
        "    )"
      ],

              "      async function convertToInteractive(key) {\n",
              "        const element = document.querySelector('#df-8752d0ca-974f-450e-9ce7-dc8469184fad');\n",
              "        const dataTable =\n",
              "          await google.colab.kernel.invokeFunction('convertToInteractive',\n",
              "                                                    [key], {});\n",
              "        if (!dataTable) return;\n",
              "\n",
              "        const docLinkHtml = 'Like what you see? Visit the ' +\n",
              "          '<a target=\"_blank\" href=https://colab.research.google.com/notebooks/data_table.ipynb>data table notebook</a>'\n",
              "          + ' to learn more about interactive tables.';\n",
              "        element.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "        dataTable['output_type'] = 'display_data';\n",
              "        await google.colab.output.renderOutput(dataTable, element);\n",
              "        const docLink = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "        docLink.innerHTML = docLinkHtml;\n",
              "        element.appendChild(docLink);\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    </script>\n",
              "  </div>\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import statistics\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "from sklearn.datasets import load_digits\n",
        "from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "# Load the dataset.\n",
        "digits = load_digits()\n",
        "X = digits.data\n",
        "y = digits.target\n",
        "\n",
        "# Use several different random seeds.\n",
        "seeds = [11, 22, 33, 44, 55]\n",
        "learning_rates = [0.001, 0.01]\n",
        "\n",
        "results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"MULTI-SEED EXPERIMENT\")\n",
        "print(\"Seeds:\", seeds)\n",
        "\n",
        "for learning_rate in learning_rates:\n",
        "    accuracies = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for seed in seeds:\n",
        "        # Create a reproducible split for this seed.\n",
        "        X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(\n",
        "            X,\n",
        "            y,\n",
        "            test_size=0.20,\n",
        "            random_state=seed,\n",
        "            stratify=y\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Fit scaling on training data only.\n",
        "        scaler = StandardScaler()\n",
        "        X_train_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)\n",
        "        X_test_scaled = scaler.transform(X_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "        model = MLPClassifier(\n",
        "            hidden_layer_sizes=(32,),\n",
        "            learning_rate_init=learning_rate,\n",
        "            max_iter=150,\n",
        "            random_state=seed,\n",
        "            early_stopping=True\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        model.fit(X_train_scaled, y_train)\n",
        "        predictions = model.predict(X_test_scaled)\n",
        "\n",
        "        accuracy = accuracy_score(y_test, predictions)\n",
        "        accuracies.append(accuracy)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"Learning rate {learning_rate} | \"\n",
        "            f\"Seed {seed} | Accuracy {accuracy:.2%}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    mean_accuracy = statistics.mean(accuracies)\n",
        "    std_accuracy = statistics.stdev(accuracies)\n",
        "\n",
        "    result = {\n",
        "        \"learning_rate\": learning_rate,\n",
        "        \"accuracies\": accuracies,\n",
        "        \"mean_accuracy\": mean_accuracy,\n",
        "        \"std_accuracy\": std_accuracy\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    results.append(result)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLearning rate: {learning_rate}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Mean accuracy: {mean_accuracy:.2%}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Standard deviation: {std_accuracy:.2%}\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save the summary of each learning-rate experiment.\n",
        "with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "    connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiment_summaries (\n",
        "            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "            learning_rate REAL NOT NULL,\n",
        "            seeds TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "            accuracies TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "            mean_accuracy REAL NOT NULL,\n",
        "            std_accuracy REAL NOT NULL,\n",
        "            created_at TEXT NOT NULL\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for result in results:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO experiment_summaries (\n",
        "                learning_rate,\n",
        "                seeds,\n",
        "                accuracies,\n",
        "                mean_accuracy,\n",
        "                std_accuracy,\n",
        "                created_at\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            result[\"learning_rate\"],\n",
        "            json.dumps(seeds),\n",
        "            json.dumps(result[\"accuracies\"]),\n",
        "            result[\"mean_accuracy\"],\n",
        "            result[\"std_accuracy\"],\n",
        "            datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nExperiment summaries saved successfully.\")"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "\n",
        "# Read the multi-seed experiment summaries.\n",
        "with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "    rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT\n",
        "            learning_rate,\n",
        "            seeds,\n",
        "            accuracies,\n",
        "            mean_accuracy,\n",
        "            std_accuracy,\n",
        "            created_at\n",
        "        FROM experiment_summaries\n",
        "        ORDER BY id\n",
        "    \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"REPRODUCIBILITY DASHBOARD\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if not rows:\n",
        "    print(\"No summaries found. Run the multi-seed experiment first.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    # Keep the most recent summary for each learning rate.\n",
        "    latest = {}\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        latest[row[0]] = row\n",
        "\n",
        "    records = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for learning_rate, seeds, accuracies, mean_acc, std_acc, created_at in latest.values():\n",
        "        records.append({\n",
        "            \"Learning Rate\": learning_rate,\n",
        "            \"Mean Accuracy (%)\": mean_acc * 100,\n",
        "            \"Standard Deviation (percentage points)\": std_acc * 100,\n",
        "            \"Runs\": len(__import__(\"json\").loads(accuracies)),\n",
        "            \"Recorded At (UTC)\": created_at\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    df = pd.DataFrame(records).sort_values(\"Learning Rate\")\n",
        "    display(df.round(2))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Plot average accuracy with standard-deviation error bars.\n",
        "    plt.figure(figsize=(8, 5))\n",
        "    plt.bar(\n",
        "        df[\"Learning Rate\"].astype(str),\n",
        "        df[\"Mean Accuracy (%)\"],\n",
        "        yerr=df[\"Standard Deviation (percentage points)\"],\n",
        "        capsize=6\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    plt.xlabel(\"Learning Rate\")\n",
        "    plt.ylabel(\"Mean Test Accuracy (%)\")\n",
        "    plt.title(\"Mean Accuracy and Variation Across Runs\")\n",
        "    plt.ylim(0, 100)\n",
        "    plt.grid(axis=\"y\", alpha=0.3)\n",
        "    plt.show()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nInterpretation: error bars show one standard deviation \"\n",
        "        \"across the recorded runs, not a confidence interval.\"\n",
        "    )"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import statistics\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "\n",
        "def analyze_experiment_results():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Compare saved multi-seed results and write a report.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    database_path = Path(\"research.db\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not database_path.exists():\n",
        "        raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "            \"research.db was not found. Run the previous \"\n",
        "            \"experiment cells first.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(database_path) as connection:\n",
        "        rows = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT learning_rate, seeds, accuracies,\n",
        "                   mean_accuracy, std_accuracy, created_at\n",
        "            FROM experiment_summaries\n",
        "            ORDER BY id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No experiment summaries found.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Run the multi-seed experiment cell first.\")\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Keep the most recent saved result for each learning rate.\n",
        "    latest = {}\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        latest[row[0]] = row\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for learning_rate, seeds_json, accuracies_json, mean_acc, std_acc, created_at in latest.values():\n",
        "        accuracies = json.loads(accuracies_json)\n",
        "        seeds = json.loads(seeds_json)\n",
        "\n",
        "        results.append({\n",
        "            \"learning_rate\": learning_rate,\n",
        "            \"seeds\": seeds,\n",
        "            \"accuracies\": accuracies,\n",
        "            \"mean\": mean_acc,\n",
        "            \"std\": std_acc,\n",
        "            \"recorded_at\": created_at\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    results.sort(key=lambda item: item[\"learning_rate\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = [\n",
        "        \"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 55,\n",
        "        \"AUTOMATIC EXPERIMENT ANALYSIS\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 55,\n",
        "        f\"Generated at (UTC): {datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"EXPERIMENT RESULTS\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 55\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for item in results:\n",
        "        lines.extend([\n",
        "            f\"Learning rate: {item['learning_rate']}\",\n",
        "            f\"Number of runs: {len(item['accuracies'])}\",\n",
        "            f\"Random seeds: {item['seeds']}\",\n",
        "            f\"Mean test accuracy: {item['mean']:.2%}\",\n",
        "            f\"Standard deviation: {item['std']:.2%}\",\n",
        "            \"Individual accuracies: \" + \", \".join(\n",
        "                f\"{value:.2%}\" for value in item[\"accuracies\"]\n",
        "            ),\n",
        "            f\"Results recorded at: {item['recorded_at']}\",\n",
        "            \"\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.extend([\n",
        "        \"COMPARISON\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 55\n",
        "    ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(results) < 2:\n",
        "        lines.append(\n",
        "            \"At least two different learning rates are required \"\n",
        "            \"for a comparison.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        best = max(results, key=lambda item: item[\"mean\"])\n",
        "        worst = min(results, key=lambda item: item[\"mean\"])\n",
        "        difference = best[\"mean\"] - worst[\"mean\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        lines.extend([\n",
        "            f\"Higher observed mean accuracy: {best['learning_rate']}\",\n",
        "            f\"Observed mean accuracy: {best['mean']:.2%}\",\n",
        "            f\"Lower observed mean accuracy: {worst['mean']:.2%}\",\n",
        "            f\"Difference in mean accuracy: {difference:.2%}\",\n",
        "            \"\",\n",
        "            \"INTERPRETATION\",\n",
        "            \"-\" * 55,\n",
        "            f\"In these recorded runs, learning rate {best['learning_rate']} \"\n",
        "            f\"had the highest mean test accuracy.\",\n",
        "            f\"Its observed standard deviation was {best['std']:.2%}.\",\n",
        "            f\"The comparison learning rate {worst['learning_rate']} \"\n",
        "            f\"had an observed standard deviation of {worst['std']:.2%}.\",\n",
        "            \"This is a descriptive comparison, not a statistical \"\n",
        "            \"significance test.\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.extend([\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"LIMITATIONS\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 55,\n",
        "        \"1. Only the saved runs were analyzed.\",\n",
        "        \"2. Different random seeds also change the train/test split.\",\n",
        "        \"3. A higher mean does not establish general superiority.\",\n",
        "        \"4. No statistical significance test was performed.\",\n",
        "        \"5. The test set was used to compare settings, so an independent \"\n",
        "        \"final test set would be preferable for a final evaluation.\",\n",
        "        \"6. Conclusions apply only to this dataset and configuration.\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"RECOMMENDED NEXT EXPERIMENT\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 55,\n",
        "        \"Use a fixed set of validation splits to select settings, \"\n",
        "        \"then evaluate the chosen configuration once on an untouched \"\n",
        "        \"test set.\"\n",
        "    ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_text = \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_path = Path(\"experiment_analysis_report.txt\")\n",
        "    report_path.write_text(report_text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(report_text)\n",
        "    print(\"\\nReport saved to:\", report_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(report_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "analysis_report_path = analyze_experiment_results()"
      ],

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",

              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_research_workspace():\n",
        "    database = Path(\"research.db\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not database.exists():\n",
        "        raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "            \"research.db was not found. Run your earlier setup cells first.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    sections = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(database) as connection:\n",
        "        connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workspace_notes (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                title TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                content TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                created_at TEXT NOT NULL\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Research questions\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            sections[\"questions\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT id, question, field, objective, created_at\n",
        "                FROM research_questions\n",
        "                ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "        except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n",
        "            sections[\"questions\"] = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Registered research papers\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            sections[\"papers\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT id, filename, page_count, character_count\n",
        "                FROM research_papers\n",
        "                ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "        except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n",
        "            sections[\"papers\"] = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Saved evidence passages\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            sections[\"evidence\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT p.filename, e.research_question,\n",
        "                       e.source_page, e.relevance_score, e.passage\n",
        "                FROM research_evidence e\n",
        "                JOIN research_papers p ON p.id = e.paper_id\n",
        "                ORDER BY e.id DESC\n",
        "                LIMIT 20\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "        except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n",
        "            sections[\"evidence\"] = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Knowledge graph\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            sections[\"nodes\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT id, name, node_type\n",
        "                FROM knowledge_nodes\n",
        "                ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "            sections[\"edges\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT s.name, e.relation, t.name\n",
        "                FROM knowledge_edges e\n",
        "                JOIN knowledge_nodes s ON s.id = e.source_id\n",
        "                JOIN knowledge_nodes t ON t.id = e.target_id\n",
        "                ORDER BY e.id DESC\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "        except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n",
        "            sections[\"nodes\"] = []\n",
        "            sections[\"edges\"] = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Experiments\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            sections[\"experiments\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT id, name, result, conclusion, created_at\n",
        "                FROM experiments\n",
        "                ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "        except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n",
        "            sections[\"experiments\"] = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Multi-seed experiment summaries\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            sections[\"summaries\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT learning_rate, mean_accuracy,\n",
        "                       std_accuracy, created_at\n",
        "                FROM experiment_summaries\n",
        "                ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "        except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n",
        "            sections[\"summaries\"] = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        sections[\"notes\"] = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, title, content, created_at\n",
        "            FROM workspace_notes\n",
        "            ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return sections\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def display_research_workspace():\n",
        "    data = build_research_workspace()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — UNIFIED WORKSPACE\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Generated (UTC):\", datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n1. RESEARCH QUESTIONS\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Total:\", len(data[\"questions\"]))\n",
        "    for row in data[\"questions\"][:5]:\n",
        "        print(f\"ID {row[0]} | {row[1]} | Field: {row[2]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n2. RESEARCH PAPERS\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Total:\", len(data[\"papers\"]))\n",
        "    for row in data[\"papers\"][:10]:\n",
        "        print(f\"ID {row[0]} | {row[1]} | Pages: {row[2]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n3. SAVED EVIDENCE\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Recent passages displayed:\", len(data[\"evidence\"]))\n",
        "    for row in data[\"evidence\"][:5]:\n",
        "        print(f\"Paper: {row[0]} | Page: {row[2]} | Score: {row[3]}\")\n",
        "        print(row[4][:250], \"\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n4. KNOWLEDGE GRAPH\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Nodes:\", len(data[\"nodes\"]))\n",
        "    print(\"Relationships:\", len(data[\"edges\"]))\n",
        "    for source, relation, target in data[\"edges\"][:5]:\n",
        "        print(f\"{source} --{relation}--> {target}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n5. EXPERIMENTS\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Recorded experiments:\", len(data[\"experiments\"]))\n",
        "    for row in data[\"experiments\"][:5]:\n",
        "        print(f\"ID {row[0]} | {row[1]}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Result:\", row[2][:250])\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n6. MULTI-SEED RESULTS\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Saved summaries:\", len(data[\"summaries\"]))\n",
        "    for learning_rate, mean_acc, std_acc, created_at in data[\"summaries\"][:5]:\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"Learning rate: {learning_rate} | \"\n",
        "            f\"Mean accuracy: {mean_acc:.2%} | \"\n",
        "            f\"Standard deviation: {std_acc:.2%}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n7. WORKSPACE NOTES\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Saved notes:\", len(data[\"notes\"]))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nWorkspace overview complete.\")\n",
        "    return data\n",
        "\n",

        "\n",
        "def save_workspace_note(title, content):\n",
        "    title = title.strip()\n",
        "    content = content.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not title or not content:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Both title and content are required.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(\"research.db\") as connection:\n",
        "        cursor = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO workspace_notes (title, content, created_at)\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            title,\n",
        "            content,\n",
        "            datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        note_id = cursor.lastrowid\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Research note saved.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Note ID:\", note_id)\n",
        "    return note_id\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Example note — edit this to record your own interpretation.\n",
        "save_workspace_note(\n",
        "    \"Learning-rate experiment review\",\n",
        "    \"Compared learning rates 0.001 and 0.01 on the digits dataset. \"\n",
        "    \"Review the measured accuracy and variability before deciding \"\n",
        "    \"which configuration to investigate further.\"\n",
        ")"

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "\n",
        "def backup_research_database():\n",
        "    source = Path(\"research.db\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not source.exists():\n",
        "        raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "            \"research.db was not found. Run your project setup first.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S\")\n",
        "    backup_path = Path(f\"research_backup_{timestamp}.db\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # SQLite's backup API safely copies the database.\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(source) as source_db:\n",
        "        with sqlite3.connect(backup_path) as backup_db:\n",
        "            source_db.backup(backup_db)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Verify that the backup can be opened.\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(backup_path) as connection:\n",
        "        integrity = connection.execute(\n",
        "            \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        tables = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type = 'table'\n",
        "            ORDER BY name\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if integrity != \"ok\":\n",
        "        backup_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"Backup verification failed.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Backup created successfully!\")\n",
        "    print(\"File:\", backup_path.name)\n",
        "    print(\"Database integrity:\", integrity)\n",
        "    print(\"Tables included:\", len(tables))\n",
        "\n",
        "    for (table_name,) in tables:\n",
        "        print(\"-\", table_name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    files.download(str(backup_path))\n",
        "    return str(backup_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "backup_file = backup_research_database()"
      ],

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },
        {
          "output_type": "display_data",
          "data": {
            "text/plain": [

            ]

      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "\n",
        "def restore_research_database():\n",
        "    print(\"Upload your research_backup_....db file.\")\n",
        "    uploaded = files.upload()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not uploaded:\n",
        "        print(\"No file uploaded. Nothing was changed.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    filename = next(iter(uploaded))\n",
        "    backup_path = Path(filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if backup_path.suffix.lower() != \".db\":\n",
        "        print(\"Error: Please upload a .db backup file.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Validate the uploaded database before touching research.db.\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        with sqlite3.connect(backup_path) as connection:\n",
        "            integrity = connection.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "            tables = {\n",
        "                row[0] for row in connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                    SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "                    WHERE type = 'table'\n",
        "                \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if integrity != \"ok\" or not tables:\n",
        "            print(\"Backup is invalid. The current database is unchanged.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Preserve the current database before restoring.\n",
        "        current = Path(\"research.db\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if current.exists():\n",
        "            with sqlite3.connect(current) as source_db:\n",
        "                with sqlite3.connect(\"research_before_restore.db\") as backup_db:\n",
        "                    source_db.backup(backup_db)\n",
        "            print(\"Previous database saved as research_before_restore.db\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Restore using SQLite backup API.\n",
        "        with sqlite3.connect(backup_path) as source_db:\n",
        "            with sqlite3.connect(current) as destination_db:\n",
        "                source_db.backup(destination_db)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"Restore completed successfully.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Verified integrity:\", integrity)\n",
        "        print(\"Tables restored:\", len(tables))\n",
        "\n",
        "    except sqlite3.DatabaseError as error:\n",
        "        print(\"Could not restore this database:\", error)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Run this only when you actually need to restore a backup.\n",
        "# restore_research_database()"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "fSziSyuiP_8J"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def connect_research_db():\n",
        "    return sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def ensure_console_tables():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Create the notes table if it does not already exist.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with connect_research_db() as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workspace_notes (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                title TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                content TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "    print(\"Research console database is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_table_count(conn, table_name):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Safely count records in an existing table.\"\"\"\n",
        "    exists = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "        WHERE type = 'table' AND name = ?\n",
        "    \"\"\", (table_name,)).fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not exists:\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    return conn.execute(\n",
        "        f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table_name}\"'\n",
        "    ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def console_show_overview():\n",
        "    tables = [\n",
        "        \"research_questions\",\n",
        "        \"research_papers\",\n",
        "        \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "        \"paper_library\",\n",
        "        \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "        \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "        \"experiments\",\n",
        "        \"experiment_summaries\",\n",
        "        \"workspace_notes\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n===== RESEARCH WORKSPACE OVERVIEW =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with connect_research_db() as conn:\n",
        "        for table in tables:\n",
        "            print(f\"{table}: {show_table_count(conn, table)} records\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def console_search_evidence():\n",
        "    term = input(\"Enter a topic or keyword: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not term:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a keyword.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    pattern = f\"%{term}%\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with connect_research_db() as conn:\n",
        "        exists = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type='table' AND name='research_evidence'\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not exists:\n",
        "            print(\"No research evidence table exists yet.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        columns = {\n",
        "            row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA table_info(research_evidence)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        searchable = [\n",
        "            c for c in (\"passage\", \"research_question\", \"filename\")\n",
        "            if c in columns\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not searchable:\n",
        "            print(\"The evidence table has no recognized searchable columns.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        condition = \" OR \".join(f'\"{c}\" LIKE ?' for c in searchable)\n",
        "        query = f\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT * FROM research_evidence\n",
        "            WHERE {condition}\n",
        "            LIMIT 10\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(query, [pattern] * len(searchable)).fetchall()\n",
        "        names = [\n",
        "            row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA table_info(research_evidence)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No matching saved evidence found.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nFound {len(rows)} matching record(s):\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for number, row in enumerate(rows, start=1):\n",
        "        record = dict(zip(names, row))\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n--- Result {number} ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        for key, value in record.items():\n",
        "            if value is not None:\n",
        "                text = str(value)\n",
        "                print(f\"{key}: {text[:900]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def console_generate_report():\n",
        "    question = input(\"Enter your research question: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    stopwords = {\n",
        "        \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"with\", \"what\", \"when\", \"where\",\n",
        "        \"why\", \"how\", \"does\", \"can\", \"are\", \"is\", \"in\", \"of\",\n",
        "        \"to\", \"a\", \"an\", \"on\", \"do\", \"from\", \"this\", \"that\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    keywords = [\n",
        "        word.lower()\n",
        "        for word in re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9-]*\\b\", question)\n",
        "        if word.lower() not in stopwords and len(word) > 2\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    with connect_research_db() as conn:\n",
        "        exists = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type='table' AND name='research_evidence'\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not exists:\n",
        "            print(\"No saved evidence table exists yet.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        columns = {\n",
        "            row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA table_info(research_evidence)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"passage\" not in columns:\n",
        "            print(\"The saved evidence table has no passage column.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT * FROM research_evidence\"\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        names = [\n",
        "            row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA table_info(research_evidence)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    scored = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        record = dict(zip(names, row))\n",
        "        passage = str(record.get(\"passage\") or \"\")\n",
        "        passage_lower = passage.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        matched = [\n",
        "            keyword for keyword in keywords\n",
        "            if re.search(r\"\\b\" + re.escape(keyword) + r\"\\b\", passage_lower)\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if matched:\n",
        "            scored.append((len(matched), matched, record))\n",
        "\n",
        "    scored.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)\n",
        "    scored = scored[:10]\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = [\n",
        "        \"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — EVIDENCE REPORT\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 50,\n",
        "        f\"Research question: {question}\",\n",
        "        f\"Generated: {datetime.now().isoformat(timespec='seconds')}\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"Important: This report retrieves saved passages using keyword overlap.\",\n",
        "        \"It does not independently verify claims or establish causation.\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        f\"Search terms: {', '.join(keywords) if keywords else 'None'}\",\n",
        "        f\"Matching passages: {len(scored)}\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not scored:\n",
        "        lines.append(\n",
        "            \"No matching saved passages were found. Add papers and save \"\n",
        "            \"their extracted evidence before trying again.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    for index, (score, matched, record) in enumerate(scored, start=1):\n",
        "        lines.extend([\n",
        "            f\"RESULT {index}\",\n",
        "            f\"Matched terms: {', '.join(matched)}\",\n",
        "            f\"Stored record ID: {record.get('id', 'Unknown')}\",\n",
        "            f\"Paper: {record.get('filename', 'Unknown')}\",\n",
        "            f\"Page: {record.get('page_number', record.get('page', 'Unknown'))}\",\n",
        "            \"Passage:\",\n",
        "            str(record.get(\"passage\", \"\")),\n",
        "            \"\",\n",
        "            \"-\" * 50,\n",
        "            \"\",\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_text = \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "    report_path = \"console_research_report.txt\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(report_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        file.write(report_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n\" + report_text[:10000])\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nFull report saved to: {report_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    download = input(\"Download the report? (y/n): \").strip().lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if download == \"y\":\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            from google.colab import files\n",
        "            files.download(report_path)\n",
        "        except ImportError:\n",
        "            print(\"Download is available from the notebook's file browser.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def console_show_papers():\n",
        "    with connect_research_db() as conn:\n",
        "        for table in (\"paper_library\", \"research_papers\"):\n",
        "            exists = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",

        "                    f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")'\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"\\n===== {table} =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not rows:\n",
        "                print(\"No records yet.\")\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            for row in rows:\n",

        "                print(\" | \".join(\n",
        "                    f\"{key}={str(value)[:150]}\"\n",
        "                    for key, value in record.items()\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def console_show_graph():\n",
        "    with connect_research_db() as conn:\n",
        "        for table in (\"knowledge_nodes\", \"knowledge_edges\"):\n",
        "            exists = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "                WHERE type='table' AND name=?\n",
        "            \"\"\", (table,)).fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"\\n===== {table} =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not exists:\n",
        "                print(\"Table not created yet.\")\n",
        "                continue\n",

        "            rows = conn.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT * FROM \"{table}\" LIMIT 20'\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",

        "            names = [\n",

        "                    f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")'\n",

        "            ]\n",

        "            if not rows:\n",

        "            else:\n",
        "                for row in rows:\n",

        "                row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",

        "\n",

        "\n",

        "        files.download(path)\n",
        "    except ImportError:\n",
        "        print(\"Find the file in your notebook's file browser.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_research_console():\n",
        "    ensure_console_tables()\n",
        "\n",
        "    menu = \"\"\"\n",
        "========================================\n",
        "       AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",

        "1. Workspace overview\n",

        "4. Show paper library\n",
        "5. Show knowledge graph\n",

        "            break\n",
        "\n",
        "        action = actions.get(choice)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if action is None:\n",
        "            print(\"Invalid option. Choose a number from 0 to 8.\")\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            action()\n",
        "        except sqlite3.Error as error:\n",
        "            print(f\"Database error: {error}\")\n",
        "        except Exception as error:\n",
        "            print(f\"Something went wrong: {error}\")\n",
        "\n",

        "\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import requests\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "OPENALEX_URL = \"https://api.openalex.org/works\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_paper_discovery():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS discovered_papers (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                openalex_id TEXT UNIQUE,\n",
        "                title TEXT,\n",
        "                authors TEXT,\n",
        "                publication_year INTEGER,\n",
        "                doi TEXT,\n",
        "                abstract TEXT,\n",
        "                cited_by_count INTEGER,\n",
        "                paper_url TEXT,\n",
        "                search_topic TEXT,\n",
        "                discovered_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Academic paper discovery database is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def reconstruct_abstract(inverted_index):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Convert OpenAlex's inverted abstract index into readable text.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if not inverted_index:\n",
        "        return \"Abstract not available.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    words = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for word, positions in inverted_index.items():\n",
        "        for position in positions:\n",
        "            words[position] = word\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not words:\n",
        "        return \"Abstract not available.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \" \".join(words[i] for i in sorted(words))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_academic_papers(topic, limit=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Search OpenAlex and save the returned academic papers.\"\"\"\n",
        "    topic = topic.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not topic:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a research topic.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    limit = max(1, min(int(limit), 25))\n",
        "\n",
        "    params = {\n",
        "        \"search\": topic,\n",
        "        \"per_page\": limit,\n",
        "        \"select\": (\n",
        "            \"id,doi,title,publication_year,authorships,\"\n",
        "            \"abstract_inverted_index,cited_by_count,type\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nSearching academic papers for: {topic}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        response = requests.get(\n",
        "            OPENALEX_URL,\n",
        "            params=params,\n",
        "            timeout=30\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        response.raise_for_status()\n",
        "        data = response.json()\n",
        "\n",
        "    except requests.RequestException as error:\n",
        "        print(\"Paper search failed:\", error)\n",
        "        print(\"Check your internet connection and try again.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    papers = data.get(\"results\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not papers:\n",
        "        print(\"No papers found for this topic.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    saved_papers = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        for paper in papers:\n",
        "            paper_id = paper.get(\"id\")\n",
        "            title = paper.get(\"title\") or \"Untitled paper\"\n",
        "            year = paper.get(\"publication_year\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            authors = [\n",
        "                item.get(\"author\", {}).get(\"display_name\", \"\")\n",
        "                for item in paper.get(\"authorships\", [])\n",
        "                if item.get(\"author\", {}).get(\"display_name\")\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            doi = paper.get(\"doi\") or \"\"\n",
        "            abstract = reconstruct_abstract(\n",
        "                paper.get(\"abstract_inverted_index\")\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            cited_by = paper.get(\"cited_by_count\", 0) or 0\n",
        "            paper_url = doi or paper_id or \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                INSERT INTO discovered_papers (\n",
        "                    openalex_id,\n",
        "                    title,\n",
        "                    authors,\n",
        "                    publication_year,\n",
        "                    doi,\n",
        "                    abstract,\n",
        "                    cited_by_count,\n",
        "                    paper_url,\n",
        "                    search_topic\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "                ON CONFLICT(openalex_id) DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                    title = excluded.title,\n",
        "                    authors = excluded.authors,\n",
        "                    publication_year = excluded.publication_year,\n",
        "                    doi = excluded.doi,\n",
        "                    abstract = excluded.abstract,\n",
        "                    cited_by_count = excluded.cited_by_count,\n",
        "                    paper_url = excluded.paper_url,\n",
        "                    search_topic = excluded.search_topic\n",
        "            \"\"\", (\n",
        "                paper_id,\n",
        "                title,\n",
        "                \", \".join(authors),\n",
        "                year,\n",
        "                doi,\n",
        "                abstract,\n",
        "                cited_by,\n",
        "                paper_url,\n",
        "                topic\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "            saved_papers.append({\n",
        "                \"openalex_id\": paper_id,\n",
        "                \"title\": title,\n",
        "                \"authors\": authors,\n",
        "                \"year\": year,\n",
        "                \"doi\": doi,\n",
        "                \"abstract\": abstract,\n",
        "                \"citations\": cited_by,\n",
        "                \"url\": paper_url\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nFound {len(saved_papers)} papers.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Results saved in research.db.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for number, paper in enumerate(saved_papers, start=1):\n",
        "        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 55)\n",
        "        print(f\"PAPER {number}: {paper['title']}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Year:\", paper[\"year\"] or \"Not listed\")\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            \"Authors:\",\n",
        "            \", \".join(paper[\"authors\"][:6]) or \"Not listed\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        print(\"Citations recorded by OpenAlex:\", paper[\"citations\"])\n",
        "        print(\"DOI:\", paper[\"doi\"] or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(\"Source:\", paper[\"url\"] or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(\"\\nAbstract:\")\n",
        "        print(paper[\"abstract\"][:1200])\n",
        "\n",
        "    return saved_papers\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_discovered_papers(limit=20):\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT title, authors, publication_year,\n",
        "                   cited_by_count, paper_url, search_topic\n",
        "            FROM discovered_papers\n",
        "            ORDER BY discovered_at DESC, id DESC\n",
        "            LIMIT ?\n",
        "        \"\"\", (limit,)).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No discovered papers saved yet.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n===== SAVED DISCOVERED PAPERS =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for number, row in enumerate(rows, start=1):\n",
        "        title, authors, year, citations, url, topic = row\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n{number}. {title}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Authors:\", authors or \"Not listed\")\n",
        "        print(\"Year:\", year or \"Not listed\")\n",
        "        print(\"Citation count:\", citations)\n",
        "        print(\"Search topic:\", topic)\n",
        "        print(\"Link:\", url or \"Not available\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_paper_discovery():\n",
        "    initialize_paper_discovery()\n",
        "\n",
        "    while True:\n",
        "        print(\"\"\"\n",
        "===== ACADEMIC PAPER DISCOVERY =====\n",
        "1. Search academic papers\n",
        "2. Show saved discovered papers\n",
        "0. Exit this module\n",
        "\"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        choice = input(\"Choose an option: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if choice == \"1\":\n",
        "            topic = input(\n",
        "                \"Enter a research topic: \"\n",
        "            ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "            limit_text = input(\n",
        "                \"Number of papers to retrieve (1-25, default 10): \"\n",
        "            ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                limit = int(limit_text) if limit_text else 10\n",
        "            except ValueError:\n",
        "                print(\"Please enter a whole number.\")\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            search_academic_papers(topic, limit)\n",
        "\n",
        "        elif choice == \"2\":\n",
        "            show_discovered_papers()\n",
        "\n",
        "        elif choice == \"0\":\n",
        "            print(\"Paper discovery module closed.\")\n",
        "            break\n",
        "\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"Invalid choice. Enter 1, 2, or 0.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import csv\n",
        "import re\n",
        "from urllib.parse import urlparse\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def validate_doi(doi):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Basic format check; this does not prove a DOI resolves.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if not doi:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    doi = doi.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if doi.startswith(\"https://doi.org/\"):\n",
        "        doi = doi[len(\"https://doi.org/\"):]\n",
        "\n",
        "    return bool(re.fullmatch(r\"10\\.\\d{4,9}/\\S+\", doi))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def audit_discovered_papers():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, openalex_id, title, authors,\n",
        "                   publication_year, doi, abstract,\n",
        "                   cited_by_count, paper_url, search_topic\n",
        "            FROM discovered_papers\n",
        "            ORDER BY id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No discovered papers found. Search for papers first.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"===== PAPER METADATA AUDIT =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        (\n",
        "            record_id, openalex_id, title, authors, year,\n",
        "            doi, abstract, citations, paper_url, topic\n",
        "        ) = row\n",
        "\n",
        "        problems = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not title or not title.strip():\n",
        "            problems.append(\"Missing title\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not authors or not authors.strip():\n",
        "            problems.append(\"Missing author information\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not year:\n",
        "            problems.append(\"Missing publication year\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not doi:\n",
        "            problems.append(\"No DOI supplied\")\n",
        "        elif not validate_doi(doi):\n",
        "            problems.append(\"DOI format needs checking\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not abstract or abstract == \"Abstract not available.\":\n",
        "            problems.append(\"Abstract unavailable\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nRecord {record_id}: {title}\")\n",
        "        print(\"DOI:\", doi or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(\"Status:\", \"; \".join(problems) if problems else \"Basic checks passed\")\n",
        "        print(\"OpenAlex ID:\", openalex_id or \"Not available\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nAudit complete.\")\n",
        "    print(\"A passing format check does not guarantee a valid DOI or reliable paper.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_discovered_papers_csv():\n",
        "    output_file = \"discovered_papers_catalogue.csv\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT openalex_id, title, authors,\n",
        "                   publication_year, doi, abstract,\n",
        "                   cited_by_count, paper_url, search_topic\n",
        "            FROM discovered_papers\n",
        "            ORDER BY publication_year DESC, title\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No papers to export.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    columns = [\n",
        "        \"openalex_id\", \"title\", \"authors\",\n",
        "        \"publication_year\", \"doi\", \"abstract\",\n",
        "        \"cited_by_count\", \"paper_url\", \"search_topic\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(output_file, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8-sig\") as file:\n",
        "        writer = csv.writer(file)\n",
        "        writer.writerow(columns)\n",
        "        writer.writerows(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Exported {len(rows)} papers to {output_file}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from google.colab import files\n",
        "        files.download(output_file)\n",
        "    except ImportError:\n",
        "        print(\"Open the file from your notebook's file browser.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "audit_discovered_papers()\n",
        "export_discovered_papers_csv()"
      ],

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },
