      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "STOPWORDS = {\n",
        "    \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"with\", \"from\", \"that\", \"this\",\n",
        "    \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"why\", \"how\", \"does\", \"can\",\n",
        "    \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"is\", \"in\", \"of\", \"to\", \"a\", \"an\",\n",
        "    \"on\", \"by\", \"about\", \"into\", \"using\", \"use\", \"research\",\n",
        "    \"study\", \"studies\", \"paper\", \"papers\"\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_keywords(text):\n",
        "    words = re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9-]*\\b\", (text or \"\").lower())\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        word for word in words\n",
        "        if len(word) > 2 and word not in STOPWORDS\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_question_paper_links():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS question_paper_links (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                question_id INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                openalex_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                match_score REAL NOT NULL,\n",
        "                matched_keywords TEXT,\n",
        "                review_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Suggested',\n",
        "                created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n",
        "                UNIQUE(question_id, openalex_id)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Question-paper link table is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def link_questions_to_discovered_papers():\n",
        "    initialize_question_paper_links()\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "        question_table = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type='table' AND name='research_questions'\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "        paper_table = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type='table' AND name='discovered_papers'\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not question_table or not paper_table:\n",
        "            print(\"Missing research questions or discovered papers.\")\n",
        "            print(\"Create some research questions and discover papers first.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        questions = [\n",
        "            dict(row) for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT * FROM research_questions\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        papers = [\n",
        "            dict(row) for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT * FROM discovered_papers\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not questions:\n",
        "            print(\"No saved research questions found.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not papers:\n",
        "            print(\"No discovered papers found.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        question_columns = set(questions[0].keys())\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"id\" not in question_columns:\n",
        "            print(\"Research questions table has no id column.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not {\"openalex_id\", \"title\"}.issubset(papers[0].keys()):\n",
        "            print(\"Discovered papers table is missing required columns.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        created = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        for question in questions:\n",
        "            question_text = \" \".join(\n",
        "                str(question.get(column, \"\") or \"\")\n",
        "                for column in (\"question\", \"field\", \"objective\")\n",
        "            ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "            question_keywords = get_keywords(question_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not question_keywords:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            for paper in papers:\n",
        "                paper_text = \" \".join(\n",
        "                    str(paper.get(column, \"\") or \"\")\n",
        "                    for column in (\"title\", \"abstract\", \"authors\")\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                paper_keywords = get_keywords(paper_text)\n",
        "                matched = sorted(question_keywords & paper_keywords)\n",
        "\n",
        "                if not matched:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                # Fraction of question keywords found in the paper metadata.\n",
        "                score = len(matched) / len(question_keywords)\n",
        "\n",
        "                cursor = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO question_paper_links (\n",
        "                        question_id,\n",
        "                        openalex_id,\n",
        "                        match_score,\n",
        "                        matched_keywords,\n",
        "                        review_status\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'Suggested')\n",
        "                \"\"\", (\n",
        "                    question[\"id\"],\n",
        "                    paper[\"openalex_id\"],\n",
        "                    score,\n",
        "                    \", \".join(matched)\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "                created += cursor.rowcount\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Added {created} new suggested link(s).\")\n",
        "    print(\"These are keyword matches, not verified scientific relationships.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def review_question_paper_links():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                l.id AS link_id,\n",
        "                q.question,\n",
        "                p.title,\n",
        "                p.publication_year,\n",
        "                p.doi,\n",
        "                l.match_score,\n",
        "                l.matched_keywords,\n",
        "                l.review_status\n",
        "            FROM question_paper_links AS l\n",
        "            JOIN research_questions AS q\n",
        "                ON q.id = l.question_id\n",
        "            JOIN discovered_papers AS p\n",
        "                ON p.openalex_id = l.openalex_id\n",
        "            ORDER BY l.match_score DESC, p.publication_year DESC\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No suggested links found. Run the linking function first.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n===== QUESTION–PAPER LINK REVIEW =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        print(\"\\nLink ID:\", row[\"link_id\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Question:\", row[\"question\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Paper:\", row[\"title\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Year:\", row[\"publication_year\"] or \"Unknown\")\n",
        "        print(\"DOI:\", row[\"doi\"] or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Keyword overlap: {row['match_score']:.0%}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Matched keywords:\", row[\"matched_keywords\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Status:\", row[\"review_status\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def set_question_paper_link_status(link_id, status):\n",
        "    allowed = {\"Suggested\", \"Relevant\", \"Not relevant\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "    if status not in allowed:\n",
        "        print(\"Status must be Suggested, Relevant, or Not relevant.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        cursor = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            UPDATE question_paper_links\n",
        "            SET review_status = ?\n",
        "            WHERE id = ?\n",
        "        \"\"\", (status, int(link_id)))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if cursor.rowcount:\n",
        "        print(f\"Link {link_id} updated to: {status}\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(\"Link ID not found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Run the first matching pass and display the suggestions.\n",
        "link_questions_to_discovered_papers()\n",
        "review_question_paper_links()"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "STOPWORDS = {\n",
        "    \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"with\", \"from\", \"that\", \"this\",\n",
        "    \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"why\", \"how\", \"does\", \"can\",\n",
        "    \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"is\", \"in\", \"of\", \"to\", \"a\", \"an\",\n",
        "    \"on\", \"by\", \"about\", \"into\", \"using\", \"use\", \"research\",\n",
        "    \"study\", \"studies\", \"paper\", \"papers\"\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def clean_keywords(text):\n",
        "    words = re.findall(\n",
        "        r\"\\b[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9-]*\\b\",\n",
        "        (text or \"\").lower()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        word for word in words\n",
        "        if len(word) > 2 and word not in STOPWORDS\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def audit_paper_matches():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                l.id,\n",
        "                q.question,\n",
        "                p.title,\n",
        "                p.authors,\n",
        "                p.publication_year,\n",
        "                p.doi,\n",
        "                p.paper_url,\n",
        "                p.abstract,\n",
        "                l.matched_keywords,\n",
        "                l.match_score,\n",
        "                l.review_status\n",
        "            FROM question_paper_links l\n",
        "            JOIN research_questions q\n",
        "                ON q.id = l.question_id\n",
        "            JOIN discovered_papers p\n",
        "                ON p.openalex_id = l.openalex_id\n",
        "            ORDER BY l.id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No paper matches found.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"===== PAPER MATCH QUALITY CHECK =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        (\n",
        "            link_id, question, title, authors, year,\n",
        "            doi, url, abstract, old_keywords, old_score, status\n",
        "        ) = row\n",
        "\n",
        "        question_terms = clean_keywords(question)\n",
        "        paper_terms = clean_keywords(\n",
        "            f\"{title or ''} {abstract or ''}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        matched = sorted(question_terms & paper_terms)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Jaccard similarity gives a more balanced overlap measure.\n",
        "        union = question_terms | paper_terms\n",
        "        score = len(matched) / len(union) if union else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        if score >= 0.20 and len(matched) >= 2:\n",
        "            quality = \"Potential match — review manually\"\n",
        "        elif matched:\n",
        "            quality = \"Weak match — needs review\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            quality = \"No keyword match\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nLink ID: {link_id}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Question:\", question)\n",
        "        print(\"Paper:\", title)\n",
        "        print(\"Authors:\", authors or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(\"Year:\", year or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(\"Full DOI:\", doi or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(\"Paper URL:\", url or \"Not available\")\n",
        "        print(\"Matched terms:\", \", \".join(matched) or \"None\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Recalculated overlap: {score:.1%}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Assessment:\", quality)\n",
        "        print(\"Current review status:\", status)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nNote: this is a simple keyword-based screening method, \"\n",
        "        \"not a scientific relevance judgment.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "audit_paper_matches()"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# MODULE 7: MULTI-PDF EVIDENCE EXTRACTION\n",
        "# Upload PDFs, extract page text, save passages, and search across papers.\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_multi_pdf_module():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS multi_pdf_passages (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                paper_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                page_number INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                passage_number INTEGER NOT NULL,\n",
        "                passage_text TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                uploaded_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n",
        "                UNIQUE(paper_name, page_number, passage_number)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "    print(\"Multi-PDF evidence database is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def split_into_passages(text, max_words=180, overlap=35):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Split text into overlapping passages to preserve context.\"\"\"\n",
        "    words = text.split()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not words:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    passages = []\n",
        "    step = max(1, max_words - overlap)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for start in range(0, len(words), step):\n",
        "        chunk = words[start:start + max_words]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if chunk:\n",
        "            passages.append(\" \".join(chunk))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if start + max_words >= len(words):\n",
        "            break\n",
        "\n",
        "    return passages\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def upload_and_extract_pdfs():\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from google.colab import files\n",
        "        from pypdf import PdfReader\n",
        "    except ImportError:\n",
        "        print(\"This upload function is designed for Google Colab.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Install pypdf in a separate cell using: !pip -q install pypdf\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    uploaded = files.upload()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not uploaded:\n",
        "        print(\"No files selected.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    initialize_multi_pdf_module()\n",
        "\n",
        "    total_pages = 0\n",
        "    total_passages = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for filename, file_bytes in uploaded.items():\n",
        "        if not filename.lower().endswith(\".pdf\"):\n",
        "            print(f\"Skipping non-PDF file: {filename}\")\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        temporary_path = os.path.join(\"/content\", os.path.basename(filename))\n",
        "\n",
        "        with open(temporary_path, \"wb\") as file:\n",
        "            file.write(file_bytes)\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            reader = PdfReader(temporary_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if reader.is_encrypted:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    reader.decrypt(\"\")\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    print(f\"Skipping encrypted PDF: {filename}\")\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            paper_pages = 0\n",
        "            paper_passages = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "            with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "                for page_index, page in enumerate(reader.pages, start=1):\n",
        "                    try:\n",
        "                        text = page.extract_text() or \"\"\n",
        "                    except Exception as error:\n",
        "                        print(\n",
        "                            f\"Could not extract page {page_index} \"\n",
        "                            f\"from {filename}: {error}\"\n",
        "                        )\n",
        "                        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                    if not text.strip():\n",
        "                        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                    passages = split_into_passages(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "                    for passage_index, passage in enumerate(passages, start=1):\n",
        "                        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                            INSERT INTO multi_pdf_passages (\n",
        "                                paper_name,\n",
        "                                page_number,\n",
        "                                passage_number,\n",
        "                                passage_text\n",
        "                            )\n",
        "                            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "                            ON CONFLICT(\n",
        "                                paper_name, page_number, passage_number\n",
        "                            ) DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                                passage_text = excluded.passage_text,\n",
        "                                uploaded_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n",
        "                        \"\"\", (\n",
        "                            filename,\n",
        "                            page_index,\n",
        "                            passage_index,\n",
        "                            passage\n",
        "                        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "                        paper_passages += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "                    paper_pages += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            total_pages += paper_pages\n",
        "            total_passages += paper_passages\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"\\nProcessed: {filename}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"Pages containing extractable text: {paper_pages}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"Passages saved: {paper_passages}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if paper_pages == 0:\n",
        "                print(\n",
        "                    \"No selectable text was extracted. \"\n",
        "                    \"This PDF may need OCR if it contains scanned pages.\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as error:\n",
        "            print(f\"Could not process {filename}: {error}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        finally:\n",
        "            if os.path.exists(temporary_path):\n",
        "                os.remove(temporary_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n===== UPLOAD SUMMARY =====\")\n",
        "    print(\"Pages processed:\", total_pages)\n",
        "    print(\"Passages saved or updated:\", total_passages)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_multi_pdf_evidence(query, limit=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Find keyword-matching passages across all uploaded PDFs.\"\"\"\n",
        "    query = query.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not query:\n",
        "        print(\"Enter a search term.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    words = re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z0-9-]+\\b\", query.lower())\n",
        "    words = list(dict.fromkeys(word for word in words if len(word) > 2))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not words:\n",
        "        print(\"Enter a more specific search term.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    conditions = \" OR \".join(\n",
        "        \"LOWER(passage_text) LIKE ?\" for _ in words\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    parameters = [f\"%{word}%\" for word in words]\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(f\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT paper_name, page_number, passage_number,\n",
        "                   passage_text\n",
        "            FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "            WHERE {conditions}\n",
        "        \"\"\", parameters).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper_name, page_number, passage_number, text in rows:\n",
        "        matched = [\n",
        "            word for word in words\n",
        "            if word in text.lower()\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        results.append({\n",
        "            \"paper\": paper_name,\n",
        "            \"page\": page_number,\n",
        "            \"passage_number\": passage_number,\n",
        "            \"matched_terms\": matched,\n",
        "            \"text\": text,\n",
        "            \"score\": len(matched)\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    results.sort(key=lambda result: result[\"score\"], reverse=True)\n",
        "    results = results[:max(1, min(int(limit), 50))]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not results:\n",
        "        print(\"No matching passages found.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Upload PDFs first or try different keywords.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nFound {len(results)} matching passage(s).\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for number, result in enumerate(results, start=1):\n",
        "        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 65)\n",
        "        print(f\"RESULT {number}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Paper:\", result[\"paper\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Page:\", result[\"page\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Passage:\", result[\"passage_number\"])\n",
        "        print(\"Matched terms:\", \", \".join(result[\"matched_terms\"]))\n",
        "        print(\"\\nText:\")\n",
        "        print(result[\"text\"][:1600])\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_multi_pdf_library():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT paper_name,\n",
        "                   COUNT(DISTINCT page_number) AS pages,\n",
        "                   COUNT(*) AS passages\n",
        "            FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "            GROUP BY paper_name\n",
        "            ORDER BY paper_name\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n===== MULTI-PDF LIBRARY =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No PDFs have been processed yet.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    for filename, pages, passages in rows:\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nPaper: {filename}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Pages with extracted text:\", pages)\n",
        "        print(\"Stored passages:\", passages)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_multi_pdf_evidence():\n",
        "    import csv\n",
        "\n",
        "    output_path = \"multi_pdf_evidence.csv\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT paper_name, page_number,\n",
        "                   passage_number, passage_text, uploaded_at\n",
        "            FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "            ORDER BY paper_name, page_number, passage_number\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No evidence to export.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(output_path, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8-sig\") as file:\n",
        "        writer = csv.writer(file)\n",
        "        writer.writerow([\n",
        "            \"paper_name\", \"page_number\",\n",
        "            \"passage_number\", \"passage_text\", \"uploaded_at\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "        writer.writerows(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Exported {len(rows)} passages to {output_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from google.colab import files\n",
        "        files.download(output_path)\n",
        "    except ImportError:\n",
        "        print(\"Find the CSV in your notebook's file browser.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

              "(function(scope) {\n",
              "function span(text, styleAttributes = {}) {\n",
              "  const element = document.createElement('span');\n",
              "  element.textContent = text;\n",
              "  for (const key of Object.keys(styleAttributes)) {\n",
              "    element.style[key] = styleAttributes[key];\n",
              "  }\n",
              "  return element;\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "// Max number of bytes which will be uploaded at a time.\n",
              "const MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE = 100 * 1024;\n",
              "\n",
              "function _uploadFiles(inputId, outputId) {\n",
              "  const steps = uploadFilesStep(inputId, outputId);\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  // Cache steps on the outputElement to make it available for the next call\n",
              "  // to uploadFilesContinue from Python.\n",
              "  outputElement.steps = steps;\n",
              "\n",
              "  return _uploadFilesContinue(outputId);\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "// This is roughly an async generator (not supported in the browser yet),\n",
              "// where there are multiple asynchronous steps and the Python side is going\n",
              "// to poll for completion of each step.\n",
              "// This uses a Promise to block the python side on completion of each step,\n",
              "// then passes the result of the previous step as the input to the next step.\n",
              "function _uploadFilesContinue(outputId) {\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  const steps = outputElement.steps;\n",
              "\n",
              "  const next = steps.next(outputElement.lastPromiseValue);\n",
              "  return Promise.resolve(next.value.promise).then((value) => {\n",
              "    // Cache the last promise value to make it available to the next\n",
              "    // step of the generator.\n",
              "    outputElement.lastPromiseValue = value;\n",
              "    return next.value.response;\n",
              "  });\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "/**\n",
              " * Generator function which is called between each async step of the upload\n",
              " * process.\n",
              " * @param {string} inputId Element ID of the input file picker element.\n",
              " * @param {string} outputId Element ID of the output display.\n",
              " * @return {!Iterable<!Object>} Iterable of next steps.\n",
              " */\n",
              "function* uploadFilesStep(inputId, outputId) {\n",
              "  const inputElement = document.getElementById(inputId);\n",
              "  inputElement.disabled = false;\n",
              "\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  outputElement.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "\n",
              "  const pickedPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "    inputElement.addEventListener('change', (e) => {\n",
              "      resolve(e.target.files);\n",
              "    });\n",
              "  });\n",
              "\n",
              "  const cancel = document.createElement('button');\n",
              "  inputElement.parentElement.appendChild(cancel);\n",
              "  cancel.textContent = 'Cancel upload';\n",
              "  const cancelPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "    cancel.onclick = () => {\n",
              "      resolve(null);\n",
              "    };\n",
              "  });\n",
              "\n",
              "  // Wait for the user to pick the files.\n",
              "  const files = yield {\n",
              "    promise: Promise.race([pickedPromise, cancelPromise]),\n",
              "    response: {\n",
              "      action: 'starting',\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  };\n",
              "\n",
              "  cancel.remove();\n",
              "\n",
              "  // Disable the input element since further picks are not allowed.\n",
              "  inputElement.disabled = true;\n",
              "\n",
              "  if (!files) {\n",
              "    return {\n",
              "      response: {\n",
              "        action: 'complete',\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    };\n",
              "  }\n",
              "\n",
              "  for (const file of files) {\n",
              "    const li = document.createElement('li');\n",
              "    li.append(span(file.name, {fontWeight: 'bold'}));\n",
              "    li.append(span(\n",
              "        `(${file.type || 'n/a'}) - ${file.size} bytes, ` +\n",
              "        `last modified: ${\n",
              "            file.lastModifiedDate ? file.lastModifiedDate.toLocaleDateString() :\n",
              "                                    'n/a'} - `));\n",
              "    const percent = span('0% done');\n",
              "    li.appendChild(percent);\n",
              "\n",
              "    outputElement.appendChild(li);\n",
              "\n",
              "    const fileDataPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "      const reader = new FileReader();\n",
              "      reader.onload = (e) => {\n",
              "        resolve(e.target.result);\n",
              "      };\n",
              "      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);\n",
              "    });\n",
              "    // Wait for the data to be ready.\n",
              "    let fileData = yield {\n",
              "      promise: fileDataPromise,\n",
              "      response: {\n",
              "        action: 'continue',\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    };\n",
              "\n",
              "    // Use a chunked sending to avoid message size limits. See b/62115660.\n",
              "    let position = 0;\n",
              "    do {\n",
              "      const length = Math.min(fileData.byteLength - position, MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE);\n",
              "      const chunk = new Uint8Array(fileData, position, length);\n",
              "      position += length;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const base64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode.apply(null, chunk));\n",
              "      yield {\n",
              "        response: {\n",
              "          action: 'append',\n",
              "          file: file.name,\n",
              "          data: base64,\n",
              "        },\n",
              "      };\n",
              "\n",
              "      let percentDone = fileData.byteLength === 0 ?\n",
              "          100 :\n",
              "          Math.round((position / fileData.byteLength) * 100);\n",
              "      percent.textContent = `${percentDone}% done`;\n",
              "\n",
              "    } while (position < fileData.byteLength);\n",
              "  }\n",
              "\n",
              "  // All done.\n",
              "  yield {\n",
              "    response: {\n",
              "      action: 'complete',\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  };\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",

              "};\n",
              "})(self);\n",

The best performing models also connect the encoder and decoder through an attention mechanism. We propose a new simple network architecture, the Transformer, based solely on attention mechanisms, dispensing with recurrence and convolutions entirely. Experiments on two machine translation tasks show these models to be superior in quality while being more parallelizable and requiring significantly less time to train. Our model achieves 28.4 BLEU on the WMT 2014 English- to-German translation task, improving over the existing best results, including ensembles, by over 2 BLEU. On\n",

            "1 Introduction Recurrent neural networks, long short-term memory [13] and gated recurrent [7] neural networks in particular, have been firmly established as state of the art approaches in sequence modeling and transduction problems such as language modeling and machine translation [ 35, 2, 5]. Numerous efforts have since continued to push the boundaries of recurrent language models and encoder-decoder architectures [38, 24, 15]. Recurrent models typically factor computation along the symbol positions of the input and output sequences. Aligning the positions to steps in computation time, they generate a sequence of hidden states ht, as a function of the previous hidden state ht−1 and the input for position t. This inherently sequential nature precludes parallelization within training examples, which becomes critical at longer sequence lengths, as memory constraints limit batching across examples. Recent work has achieved significant improvements in computational efficiency through factorization tricks [21] and conditional computation [32], while also improving model performance in case of the latter. The fundamental constraint of sequential computation, however, remains. Attention mechanisms have become an integral part of compelling sequence modeling and transduc-\n",
            "\n",
            "=================================================================\n",
            "RESULT 3\n",
            "Paper: neural networks (1).pdf\n",
            "Page: 2\n",
            "Passage: 2\n",
            "Matched terms: neural, network\n",
            "\n",
            "Text:\n",
            "and conditional computation [32], while also improving model performance in case of the latter. The fundamental constraint of sequential computation, however, remains. Attention mechanisms have become an integral part of compelling sequence modeling and transduc- tion models in various tasks, allowing modeling of dependencies without regard to their distance in the input or output sequences [2, 19]. In all but a few cases [27], however, such attention mechanisms are used in conjunction with a recurrent network. In this work we propose the Transformer, a model architecture eschewing recurrence and instead relying entirely on an attention mechanism to draw global dependencies between input and output. The Transformer allows for significantly more parallelization and can reach a new state of the art in translation quality after being trained for as little as twelve hours on eight P100 GPUs. 2 Background The goal of reducing sequential computation also forms the foundation of the Extended Neural GPU [16], ByteNet [18] and ConvS2S [9], all of which use convolutional neural networks as basic building block, computing hidden representations in parallel for all input and output\n",
            "\n",
            "=================================================================\n",

            "Page: 2\n",
            "Passage: 3\n",
            "Matched terms: neural, network\n",
            "\n",
            "Text:\n",
            "forms the foundation of the Extended Neural GPU [16], ByteNet [18] and ConvS2S [9], all of which use convolutional neural networks as basic building block, computing hidden representations in parallel for all input and output positions. In these models, the number of operations required to relate signals from two arbitrary input or output positions grows in the distance between positions, linearly for ConvS2S and logarithmically for ByteNet. This makes it more difficult to learn dependencies between distant positions [ 12]. In the Transformer this is reduced to a constant number of operations, albeit at the cost of reduced effective resolution due to averaging attention-weighted positions, an effect we counteract with Multi-Head Attention as described in section 3.2. Self-attention, sometimes called intra-attention is an attention mechanism relating different positions of a single sequence in order to compute a representation of the sequence. Self-attention has been used successfully in a variety of tasks including reading comprehension, abstractive summarization, textual entailment and learning task-independent sentence representations [4, 27, 28, 22]. End-to-end memory networks are based on a recurrent attention mechanism instead of sequence-\n",
            "\n",

            "successfully in a variety of tasks including reading comprehension, abstractive summarization, textual entailment and learning task-independent sentence representations [4, 27, 28, 22]. End-to-end memory networks are based on a recurrent attention mechanism instead of sequence- aligned recurrence and have been shown to perform well on simple-language question answering and language modeling tasks [34]. To the best of our knowledge, however, the Transformer is the first transduction model relying entirely on self-attention to compute representations of its input and output without using sequence- aligned RNNs or convolution. In the following sections, we will describe the Transformer, motivate self-attention and discuss its advantages over models such as [17, 18] and [9]. 3 Model Architecture Most competitive neural sequence transduction models have an encoder-decoder structure [5, 2, 35]. Here, the encoder maps an input sequence of symbol representations (x1, ..., xn) to a sequence of continuous representations z = (z1, ..., zn). Given z, the decoder then generates an output sequence (y1, ..., ym) of symbols one element at a time. At each step the model is auto-regressive [10], consuming the previously generated\n",

For translation tasks, the Transformer can be trained significantly faster than architectures based on recurrent or convolutional layers. On both WMT 2014 English-to-German and WMT 2014 English-to-French translation tasks, we achieve a new state of the art.       "source": [
        "# MODULE 8: CROSS-PAPER RESEARCH REPORT GENERATOR\n",
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "REPORT_STOPWORDS = {\n",
        "    \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"with\", \"from\", \"that\", \"this\",\n",
        "    \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"why\", \"how\", \"does\", \"can\",\n",
        "    \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"is\", \"in\", \"of\", \"to\", \"a\", \"an\",\n",
        "    \"on\", \"by\", \"about\", \"into\", \"using\", \"use\", \"research\",\n",
        "    \"study\", \"studies\", \"paper\", \"papers\", \"explain\", \"describe\"\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def report_keywords(text):\n",
        "    words = re.findall(\n",
        "        r\"\\b[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9-]*\\b\",\n",
        "        (text or \"\").lower()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return list(dict.fromkeys(\n",
        "        word for word in words\n",
        "        if len(word) > 2 and word not in REPORT_STOPWORDS\n",
        "    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def collect_cross_paper_evidence(question, limit=15):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Retrieve relevant passages from all processed PDFs.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question.strip():\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    keywords = report_keywords(question)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not keywords:\n",
        "        print(\"Could not identify useful search terms.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        exists = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name\n",
        "            FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type = 'table'\n",
        "              AND name = 'multi_pdf_passages'\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not exists:\n",
        "            print(\"The multi_pdf_passages table is missing.\")\n",
        "            print(\"Run Module 7 and process at least one PDF first.\")\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT paper_name, page_number,\n",
        "                   passage_number, passage_text\n",
        "            FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    ranked = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper, page, passage_number, text in rows:\n",
        "        text_lower = (text or \"\").lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        matched = [\n",
        "            keyword for keyword in keywords\n",
        "            if re.search(\n",
        "                r\"\\b\" + re.escape(keyword) + r\"\\b\",\n",
        "                text_lower\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if matched:\n",
        "            ranked.append({\n",
        "                \"paper\": paper,\n",
        "                \"page\": page,\n",
        "                \"passage_number\": passage_number,\n",
        "                \"text\": text,\n",
        "                \"matched\": matched,\n",
        "                \"score\": len(matched)\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "    ranked.sort(\n",
        "        key=lambda item: (\n",
        "            item[\"score\"],\n",
        "            len(item[\"text\"])\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        reverse=True\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return ranked[:max(1, min(int(limit), 50))]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def generate_cross_paper_report(question, limit=15):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Create a report with traceable source passages.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    evidence = collect_cross_paper_evidence(question, limit)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not evidence:\n",
        "        print(\"No matching evidence was found.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Try another question or process more PDFs.\")\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    papers = sorted(set(item[\"paper\"] for item in evidence))\n",
        "    keywords = report_keywords(question)\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = [\n",
        "        \"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\",\n",
        "        \"CROSS-PAPER EVIDENCE REPORT\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 65,\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        f\"Research question: {question}\",\n",
        "        f\"Generated: {datetime.now().isoformat(timespec='seconds')}\",\n",
        "        f\"Search terms: {', '.join(keywords)}\",\n",
        "        f\"Matching passages selected: {len(evidence)}\",\n",
        "        f\"Distinct source papers represented: {len(papers)}\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"IMPORTANT METHODOLOGY NOTE\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 65,\n",
        "        (\n",
        "            \"This report retrieves passages using keyword overlap. \"\n",
        "            \"It does not independently verify scientific claims, \"\n",
        "            \"resolve contradictions, or establish causation.\"\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 65,\n",
        "        (\n",
        "            f\"The search found {len(evidence)} matching passages \"\n",
        "            f\"from {len(papers)} distinct paper(s). \"\n",
        "            \"The passages below are candidates for manual review.\"\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"SOURCE PAPERS\",\n",
        "        \"-\" * 65\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper in papers:\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"- {paper}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.extend([\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"RETRIEVED EVIDENCE\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 65,\n",
        "        \"\"\n",
        "    ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    for index, item in enumerate(evidence, start=1):\n",
        "        lines.extend([\n",
        "            f\"[EVIDENCE {index}]\",\n",
        "            f\"Source paper: {item['paper']}\",\n",
        "            f\"Page: {item['page']}\",\n",
        "            f\"Passage number: {item['passage_number']}\",\n",
        "            f\"Matched terms: {', '.join(item['matched'])}\",\n",
        "            \"\",\n",
        "            item[\"text\"],\n",
        "            \"\",\n",
        "            \"-\" * 65,\n",
        "            \"\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.extend([\n",
        "        \"INTERPRETATION AND LIMITATIONS\",\n",
        "        \"=\" * 65,\n",
        "        \"1. Keyword matches do not guarantee relevance to the question.\",\n",
        "        \"2. Retrieved passages may omit important surrounding context.\",\n",
        "        \"3. The number of matches is not a measure of scientific quality.\",\n",
        "        \"4. Verify each passage in the original paper before citing it.\",\n",
        "        \"5. Compare methods, datasets, limitations, and findings manually.\",\n",
        "        \"6. No new scientific conclusion is inferred automatically.\",\n",
        "        \"\"\n",
        "    ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_text = \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "    output_path = \"cross_paper_research_report.txt\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(output_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        file.write(report_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(report_text[:12000])\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nFull report saved to: {output_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return output_path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_cross_paper_report():\n",
        "    print(\"===== CROSS-PAPER RESEARCH REPORT =====\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    question = input(\n",
        "        \"Enter your research question: \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    limit_text = input(\n",
        "        \"Maximum evidence passages (default 15): \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        limit = int(limit_text) if limit_text else 15\n",
        "    except ValueError:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a whole number.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_path = generate_cross_paper_report(question, limit)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if report_path:\n",
        "        choice = input(\n",
        "            \"\\nDownload the report? (y/n): \"\n",
        "        ).strip().lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if choice == \"y\":\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                from google.colab import files\n",
        "                files.download(report_path)\n",
        "            except ImportError:\n",
        "                print(\"Find the report in the notebook file browser.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "run_cross_paper_report()"
      ],

            "forms the foundation of the Extended Neural GPU [16], ByteNet [18] and ConvS2S [9], all of which use convolutional neural networks as basic building block, computing hidden representations in parallel for all input and output positions. In these models, the number of operations required to relate signals from two arbitrary input or output positions grows in the distance between positions, linearly for ConvS2S and logarithmically for ByteNet. This makes it more difficult to learn dependencies between distant positions [ 12]. In the Transformer this is reduced to a constant number of operations, albeit at the cost of reduced effective resolution due to averaging attention-weighted positions, an effect we counteract with Multi-Head Attention as described in section 3.2. Self-attention, sometimes called intra-attention is an attention mechanism relating different positions of a single sequence in order to compute a representation of the sequence. Self-attention has been used successfully in a variety of tasks including reading comprehension, abstractive summarization, textual entailment and learning task-independent sentence representations [4, 27, 28, 22]. End-to-end memory networks are based on a recurrent attention mechanism instead of sequence-\n",

Attention Is All You Need Ashish Vaswani∗ Google Brain avaswani@google.com Noam Shazeer∗ Google Brain noam@google.com Niki Parmar∗ Google Research nikip@google.com Jakob Uszkoreit∗ Google Research usz@google.com Llion Jones∗ Google Research llion@google.com Aidan N. Gomez∗ † University of Toronto aidan@cs.toronto.edu Łukasz Kaiser∗ Google Brain lukaszkaiser@google.com Illia Polosukhin∗ ‡ illia.polosukhin@gmail.com Abstract The dominant sequence transduction models are based on complex recurrent or convolutional neural networks that include an encoder and a decoder. The best performing models also connect the encoder and decoder through an attention mechanism. We propose a new simple network architecture, the Transformer, based solely on attention mechanisms, dispensing with recurrence and convolutions entirely. Experiments on two machine translation tasks show these models to be superior in quality while being more parallelizable and requiring significantly less time to train. Our model achieves 28.4 BLEU on the WMT 2014 English- to-German translation task, improving over the existing best results, including ensembles, by over 2 BLEU. On\n",

            "-----------------------------------------------------------------\n",
            "\n",
            "[EVIDENCE 6]\n",
            "Source paper: neural networks (1).pdf\n",
            "Page: 2\n",
            "Passage number: 1\n",
            "Matched terms: neural, networks\n",
            "\n",
            "1 Introduction Recurrent neural networks, long short-term memory [13] and gated recurrent [7] neural networks in particular, have been firmly established as state of the art approaches in sequence modeling and transduction problems such as language modeling and machine translation [ 35, 2, 5]. Numerous efforts have since continued to push the boundaries of recurrent language models and encoder-decoder architectures [38, 24, 15]. Recurrent models typically factor computation along the symbol positions of the input and output sequences. Aligning the positions to steps in computation time, they generate a sequence of hidden states ht, as a function of the previous hidden state ht−1 and the input for position t. This inherently sequential nature precludes parallelization within training examples, which becomes critical at longer sequence lengths, as memory constraints limit batching across examples. Recent work has achieved significant improvements in computational efficiency through factorization tricks [21] and conditional computation [32], while also improving model performance in case of the latter. The fundamental constraint of sequential computation, however, remains. Attention mechanisms have become an integral part of compelling sequence modeling and transduc-\n",
            "\n",
            "-----------------------------------------------------------------\n",
            "\n",
            "[EVIDENCE 7]\n",
            "Source paper: neural networks (1).pdf\n",
            "Page: 2\n",
            "Passage number: 4\n",
            "Matched terms: neural, networks\n",
            "\n",
            "successfully in a variety of tasks including reading comprehension, abstractive summarization, textual entailment and learning task-independent sentence representations [4, 27, 28, 22]. End-to-end memory networks are based on a recurrent attention mechanism instead of sequence- aligned recurrence and have been shown to perform well on simple-language question answering and language modeling tasks [34]. To the best of our knowledge, however, the Transformer is the first transduction model relying entirely on self-attention to compute representations of its input and output without using sequence- aligned RNNs or convolution. In the following sections, we will describe the Transformer, motivate self-attention and discuss its advantages over models such as [17, 18] and [9]. 3 Model Architecture Most competitive neural sequence transduction models have an encoder-decoder structure [5, 2, 35]. Here, the encoder maps an input sequence of symbol representations (x1, ..., xn) to a sequence of continuous representations z = (z1, ..., zn). Given z, the decoder then generates an output sequence (y1, ..., ym) of symbols one element at a time. At each step the model is auto-regressive [10], consuming the previously generated\n",

Making generation less sequential is another research goals of ours. The code we used to train and evaluate our models is available at https://github.com/ tensorflow/tensor2tensor. Acknowled\n",

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },

      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import hashlib\n",
        "from collections import Counter\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_evidence_reviews():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Create a review table without deleting existing research data.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence_reviews (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                paper_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                page_number INTEGER,\n",
        "                passage_number INTEGER,\n",
        "                claim TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                review_label TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                matched_terms TEXT,\n",
        "                passage_hash TEXT,\n",
        "                reviewed_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                UNIQUE(paper_name, page_number, passage_number, claim)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "    print(\"Evidence review table is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def normalize_passage(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Normalize text for duplicate checking.\"\"\"\n",
        "    text = (text or \"\").lower()\n",
        "    text = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", text)\n",
        "    text = re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9 ]\", \"\", text)\n",
        "    return text.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def audit_multi_pdf_evidence():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Find exact normalized duplicates and low-information passages.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"multi_pdf_passages\" not in tables:\n",
        "            print(\"The multi_pdf_passages table is missing.\")\n",
        "            print(\"Run Module 7 first, then upload and process PDFs.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT paper_name, page_number, passage_number, passage_text\n",
        "            FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "            ORDER BY paper_name, page_number, passage_number\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No passages found. Upload PDFs using Module 7 first.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    hashes = Counter()\n",
        "    paper_counts = Counter()\n",
        "    low_information = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper, page, passage_no, text in rows:\n",
        "        normalized = normalize_passage(text)\n",
        "        if normalized:\n",
        "            digest = hashlib.sha256(normalized.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()\n",
        "            hashes[digest] += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        paper_counts[paper] += 1\n",
        "        word_count = len(normalized.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "        if word_count < 25:\n",
        "            low_information.append(\n",
        "                (paper, page, passage_no, word_count)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    duplicate_groups = sum(1 for count in hashes.values() if count > 1)\n",
        "    duplicate_passages = sum(\n",
        "        count - 1 for count in hashes.values() if count > 1\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n=== EVIDENCE QUALITY AUDIT ===\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Total passages: {len(rows)}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Papers represented: {len(paper_counts)}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Exact duplicate groups: {duplicate_groups}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Extra duplicate passages: {duplicate_passages}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Short passages flagged: {len(low_information)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n--- Passages per paper ---\")\n",
        "    for paper, count in paper_counts.most_common():\n",
        "        print(f\"{paper}: {count} passages\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n--- Short passages to review ---\")\n",
        "    if low_information:\n",
        "        for paper, page, passage_no, count in low_information[:30]:\n",
        "            print(\n",
        "                f\"{paper} | page {page} | passage {passage_no} \"\n",
        "                f\"| {count} words\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(\"No passages were flagged by the short-length check.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nNote: short text is not automatically bad evidence.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Duplicate text may come from overlapping PDF extraction.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def classify_passage_against_claim(claim, passage):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Conservative lexical screening.\n",
        "    This is not semantic reasoning or proof of contradiction.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    claim_words = {\n",
        "        word for word in re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\", claim.lower())\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    passage_words = set(\n",
        "        re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\", passage.lower())\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    common = sorted(claim_words & passage_words)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not common:\n",
        "        return \"Unclear\", []\n",
        "\n",
        "    lower_text = passage.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    challenge_cues = [\n",
        "        \"not\", \"no evidence\", \"fails to\", \"failed to\",\n",
        "        \"does not\", \"did not\", \"however\", \"in contrast\",\n",
        "        \"contrary\", \"unable to\", \"no significant\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    support_cues = [\n",
        "        \"shows\", \"showed\", \"demonstrates\", \"demonstrated\",\n",
        "        \"improves\", \"improved\", \"increases\", \"increased\",\n",
        "        \"supports\", \"supported\", \"consistent with\",\n",
        "        \"significant improvement\", \"outperformed\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    has_challenge = any(cue in lower_text for cue in challenge_cues)\n",
        "    has_support = any(cue in lower_text for cue in support_cues)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Avoid a confident label if both kinds of cues occur.\n",
        "    if has_challenge and not has_support:\n",
        "        label = \"Potentially challenging\"\n",
        "    elif has_support and not has_challenge:\n",
        "        label = \"Potentially supporting\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        label = \"Unclear\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return label, common\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def review_claim_against_saved_passages(claim, limit=30):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Screen saved passages and store the review results.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if not claim or not claim.strip():\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a research claim.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    initialize_evidence_reviews()\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"multi_pdf_passages\" not in tables:\n",
        "            print(\"Run Module 7 and process PDFs before reviewing claims.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT paper_name, page_number, passage_number, passage_text\n",
        "            FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No passages available. Process PDFs using Module 7 first.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    claim_terms = set(\n",
        "        re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\", claim.lower())\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    scored = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper, page, passage_no, passage in rows:\n",
        "        passage = passage or \"\"\n",
        "        passage_terms = set(\n",
        "            re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\\b\", passage.lower())\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        overlap = claim_terms & passage_terms\n",
        "\n",
        "        if overlap:\n",
        "            label, matched = classify_passage_against_claim(\n",
        "                claim, passage\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            scored.append((\n",
        "                len(overlap), paper, page, passage_no,\n",
        "                passage, label, matched\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    scored.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)\n",
        "    selected = scored[:max(1, int(limit))]\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        for score, paper, page, passage_no, passage, label, matched in selected:\n",
        "            digest = hashlib.sha256(\n",
        "                normalize_passage(passage).encode(\"utf-8\")\n",
        "            ).hexdigest()\n",
        "\n",
        "            conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                INSERT INTO evidence_reviews (\n",
        "                    paper_name, page_number, passage_number, claim,\n",
        "                    review_label, matched_terms, passage_hash, reviewed_at\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "                ON CONFLICT(\n",
        "                    paper_name, page_number, passage_number, claim\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                    review_label = excluded.review_label,\n",
        "                    matched_terms = excluded.matched_terms,\n",
        "                    passage_hash = excluded.passage_hash,\n",
        "                    reviewed_at = excluded.reviewed_at\n",
        "            \"\"\", (\n",
        "                paper, page, passage_no, claim, label,\n",
        "                \", \".join(matched), digest, datetime.now().isoformat()\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n=== CLAIM REVIEW ===\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Claim: {claim}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Matching passages reviewed: {len(selected)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, item in enumerate(selected, start=1):\n",
        "        score, paper, page, passage_no, passage, label, matched = item\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n{i}. {label}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Paper: {paper} | Page: {page} | Passage: {passage_no}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Shared words: {', '.join(matched)}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Passage: {passage[:700]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not selected:\n",
        "        print(\"No passages shared keywords with this claim.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nImportant: labels are preliminary keyword-based screening. \"\n",
        "        \"Read the original passage and surrounding context before \"\n",
        "        \"deciding whether it supports or challenges the claim.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_saved_evidence_reviews():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Display recent saved claim reviews.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"evidence_reviews\" not in tables:\n",
        "            print(\"No review table yet. Run a claim review first.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, paper_name, page_number, claim,\n",
        "                   review_label, matched_terms, reviewed_at\n",
        "            FROM evidence_reviews\n",
        "            ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "            LIMIT 30\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No saved evidence reviews yet.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"\\nReview #{row[0]} | {row[4]}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Paper: {row[1]} | Page: {row[2]}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Claim: {row[3]}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Shared terms: {row[5]}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Reviewed: {row[6]}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_evidence_quality_menu():\n",
        "    initialize_evidence_reviews()\n",
        "\n",
        "    while True:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n=== EVIDENCE QUALITY MENU ===\")\n",
        "        print(\"1. Audit saved PDF passages\")\n",
        "        print(\"2. Review passages against a research claim\")\n",
        "        print(\"3. Show saved claim reviews\")\n",
        "        print(\"0. Exit\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        choice = input(\"Choose an option: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if choice == \"1\":\n",
        "            audit_multi_pdf_evidence()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"2\":\n",
        "            claim = input(\"Enter a research claim: \").strip()\n",
        "            limit_text = input(\n",
        "                \"Maximum passages to review (default 30): \"\n",
        "            ).strip()\n",
        "            limit = int(limit_text) if limit_text.isdigit() else 30\n",
        "            review_claim_against_saved_passages(claim, limit)\n",
        "        elif choice == \"3\":\n",
        "            show_saved_evidence_reviews()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"0\":\n",
        "            print(\"Evidence quality review closed.\")\n",
        "            break\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"Invalid choice. Enter 1, 2, 3, or 0.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "run_evidence_quality_menu()"
