      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import csv\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_citation_tracker():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research_citations (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                claim TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                paper_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                page_number INTEGER,\n",
        "                passage_number INTEGER,\n",
        "                quoted_evidence TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                citation_note TEXT,\n",
        "                created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                UNIQUE(\n",
        "                    claim, paper_name, page_number,\n",
        "                    passage_number, quoted_evidence\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "    print(\"Citation tracker is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def find_citation_sources(search_term, limit=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Find source passages using keyword matching.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"multi_pdf_passages\" not in tables:\n",
        "            print(\"The PDF passage library is missing.\")\n",
        "            print(\"Run Module 7 and process your PDFs first.\")\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT paper_name, page_number, passage_number, passage_text\n",
        "            FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    terms = {\n",
        "        word.lower()\n",
        "        for word in search_term.split()\n",
        "        if len(word) >= 3\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper, page, passage_no, passage in rows:\n",
        "        passage_lower = (passage or \"\").lower()\n",
        "        matched = sorted(\n",
        "            term for term in terms if term in passage_lower\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if matched:\n",
        "            results.append({\n",
        "                \"paper\": paper,\n",
        "                \"page\": page,\n",
        "                \"passage_number\": passage_no,\n",
        "                \"passage\": passage,\n",
        "                \"matched_terms\": matched,\n",
        "                \"score\": len(matched)\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "    results.sort(key=lambda item: item[\"score\"], reverse=True)\n",
        "    return results[:max(1, int(limit))]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def add_research_citation(\n",
        "    claim, paper_name, page_number, passage_number,\n",
        "    quoted_evidence, citation_note=\"\"\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Save a manually selected source passage.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if not claim.strip() or not paper_name.strip():\n",
        "        print(\"Claim and paper name are required.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not quoted_evidence.strip():\n",
        "        print(\"Evidence passage cannot be empty.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO research_citations (\n",
        "                claim, paper_name, page_number, passage_number,\n",
        "                quoted_evidence, citation_note, created_at\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "            ON CONFLICT(\n",
        "                claim, paper_name, page_number,\n",
        "                passage_number, quoted_evidence\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                citation_note = excluded.citation_note,\n",
        "                created_at = excluded.created_at\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            claim.strip(),\n",
        "            paper_name.strip(),\n",
        "            page_number,\n",
        "            passage_number,\n",
        "            quoted_evidence.strip(),\n",
        "            citation_note.strip(),\n",
        "            datetime.now().isoformat()\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Citation saved successfully.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def create_citation_for_claim():\n",
        "    claim = input(\"Enter the research claim: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not claim:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a claim.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    limit_text = input(\n",
        "        \"How many source passages should be shown? (default 10): \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "    limit = int(limit_text) if limit_text.isdigit() else 10\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = find_citation_sources(claim, limit)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not results:\n",
        "        print(\"No matching passages found.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n=== POSSIBLE SOURCES ===\")\n",
        "    for i, result in enumerate(results, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n{i}. {result['paper']}\")\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"Page: {result['page']} | \"\n",
        "            f\"Passage: {result['passage_number']}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        print(f\"Keyword matches: {', '.join(result['matched_terms'])}\")\n",
        "        print(result[\"passage\"][:900])\n",
        "\n",
        "    selection = input(\n",
        "        \"\\nEnter the number of the source you manually checked, \"\n",
        "        \"or press Enter to cancel: \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not selection.isdigit():\n",
        "        print(\"Citation creation cancelled.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    index = int(selection) - 1\n",
        "    if index < 0 or index >= len(results):\n",
        "        print(\"Invalid source number.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    source = results[index]\n",
        "    print(\"\\nCheck that the passage really supports your claim.\")\n",
        "    note = input(\n",
        "        \"Citation note (optional, e.g. 'supports method'): \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_research_citation(\n",
        "        claim=claim,\n",
        "        paper_name=source[\"paper\"],\n",
        "        page_number=source[\"page\"],\n",
        "        passage_number=source[\"passage_number\"],\n",
        "        quoted_evidence=source[\"passage\"],\n",
        "        citation_note=note\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_research_citations():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, claim, paper_name, page_number,\n",
        "                   passage_number, quoted_evidence, citation_note\n",
        "            FROM research_citations\n",
        "            ORDER BY id DESC\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No citations saved yet.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n=== SAVED RESEARCH CITATIONS ===\")\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nCitation ID: {row[0]}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Claim: {row[1]}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Source: {row[2]}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Page: {row[3]} | Passage: {row[4]}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Evidence: {row[5][:700]}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Note: {row[6] or 'No note'}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_research_citations():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, claim, paper_name, page_number,\n",
        "                   passage_number, quoted_evidence,\n",
        "                   citation_note, created_at\n",
        "            FROM research_citations\n",
        "            ORDER BY id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No citations to export. Create a citation first.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    filename = \"research_citations.csv\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(filename, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",

        "        writer.writerow([\n",

        "            \"passage_number\", \"quoted_evidence\", \"citation_note\",\n",
        "            \"created_at\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "        writer.writerows(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Exported {len(rows)} citations to {filename}\")\n",
        "    files.download(filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import csv\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_bibliography():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Create a bibliography table without changing existing tables.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research_bibliography (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                source_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                source_id TEXT,\n",
        "                title TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                authors TEXT,\n",
        "                publication_year INTEGER,\n",
        "                doi TEXT,\n",
        "                paper_url TEXT,\n",
        "                citation_style TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'APA',\n",
        "                added_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                UNIQUE(source_type, source_id, title)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "    print(\"Bibliography table is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_table_columns(conn, table_name):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Return the column names for an existing table.\"\"\"\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "            f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table_name}\")'\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def import_discovered_papers():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Import paper metadata from the existing discovery table.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"discovered_papers\" not in tables:\n",
        "            print(\n",
        "                \"The discovered_papers table was not found. \"\n",
        "                \"Run Module 5 to discover academic papers first.\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        columns = get_table_columns(conn, \"discovered_papers\")\n",
        "        required = {\"title\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not required.issubset(columns):\n",
        "            print(\"The discovered_papers table has an unexpected schema.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        def field(column, fallback=\"NULL\"):\n",
        "            return column if column in columns else fallback\n",
        "\n",
        "        id_col = field(\"openalex_id\", \"CAST(id AS TEXT)\")\n",
        "        authors_col = field(\"authors\")\n",
        "        year_col = field(\"publication_year\")\n",
        "        doi_col = field(\"doi\")\n",
        "        url_col = field(\"paper_url\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        query = f\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT {id_col}, title, {authors_col},\n",
        "                   {year_col}, {doi_col}, {url_col}\n",
        "            FROM discovered_papers\n",
        "            WHERE title IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(title) != ''\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(query).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        imported = 0\n",
        "        for source_id, title, authors, year, doi, url in rows:\n",
        "            conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                INSERT INTO research_bibliography (\n",
        "                    source_type, source_id, title, authors,\n",
        "                    publication_year, doi, paper_url,\n",
        "                    citation_style, added_at\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'APA', ?)\n",
        "                ON CONFLICT(source_type, source_id, title)\n",
        "                DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                    authors = excluded.authors,\n",
        "                    publication_year = excluded.publication_year,\n",
        "                    doi = excluded.doi,\n",
        "                    paper_url = excluded.paper_url\n",
        "            \"\"\", (\n",
        "                \"OpenAlex\",\n",
        "                str(source_id) if source_id is not None else None,\n",
        "                title.strip(),\n",
        "                authors or \"\",\n",
        "                year,\n",
        "                doi or \"\",\n",
        "                url or \"\",\n",
        "                datetime.now().isoformat()\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            imported += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Imported or updated {imported} discovered papers.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def format_authors(authors):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Clean author text; preserves the order supplied by the source.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if not authors:\n",
        "        return \"Author unknown\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(authors, str):\n",
        "        cleaned = authors.strip()\n",
        "        return cleaned if cleaned else \"Author unknown\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(authors)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def format_bibliography_entry(row, style=\"APA\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Format a bibliography entry using available metadata.\"\"\"\n",
        "    title = (row[\"title\"] or \"Untitled\").strip()\n",
        "    authors = format_authors(row[\"authors\"])\n",
        "    year = str(row[\"publication_year\"]) if row[\"publication_year\"] else \"n.d.\"\n",
        "    doi = (row[\"doi\"] or \"\").strip()\n",
        "    url = (row[\"paper_url\"] or \"\").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Normalize DOI URLs where possible.\n",
        "    if doi:\n",
        "        doi = doi.strip()\n",
        "        if doi.startswith(\"https://doi.org/\"):\n",
        "            doi_url = doi\n",
        "        elif doi.startswith(\"http://doi.org/\"):\n",
        "            doi_url = \"https://doi.org/\" + doi.split(\"doi.org/\", 1)[1]\n",
        "        elif doi.startswith(\"doi:\"):\n",
        "            doi_url = \"https://doi.org/\" + doi[4:].strip()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            doi_url = \"https://doi.org/\" + doi\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        doi_url = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if style.upper() == \"IEEE\":\n",
        "        entry = f'{authors}, \"{title},\" {year}.'\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        entry = f\"{authors} ({year}). {title}.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if doi_url:\n",
        "        entry += f\" {doi_url}\"\n",
        "    elif url:\n",
        "        entry += f\" {url}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return entry\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_bibliography():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT id, title, authors, publication_year,\n",
        "                   doi, paper_url, citation_style\n",
        "            FROM research_bibliography\n",
        "            ORDER BY\n",
        "                COALESCE(authors, ''),\n",
        "                COALESCE(publication_year, 0),\n",
        "                title\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"Your bibliography is empty.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Choose option 1 to import discovered papers.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n=== RESEARCH BIBLIOGRAPHY ({len(rows)} papers) ===\")\n",
        "    for number, row in enumerate(rows, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n{number}. {format_bibliography_entry(dict(row))}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_bibliography_csv():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT title, authors, publication_year, doi,\n",
        "                   paper_url, source_type, source_id\n",
        "            FROM research_bibliography\n",
        "            ORDER BY title\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No bibliography entries to export.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    filename = \"research_bibliography.csv\"\n",
        "    with open(filename, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        writer = csv.writer(file)\n",
        "        writer.writerow([\n",
        "            \"title\", \"authors\", \"publication_year\", \"doi\",\n",
        "            \"paper_url\", \"source_type\", \"source_id\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "        writer.writerows(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Exported {len(rows)} entries to {filename}\")\n",
        "    files.download(filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_bibliography_text():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT title, authors, publication_year,\n",
        "                   doi, paper_url\n",
        "            FROM research_bibliography\n",
        "            ORDER BY COALESCE(authors, ''), title\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No bibliography entries to export.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    filename = \"research_bibliography.txt\"\n",
        "    with open(filename, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        file.write(\"RESEARCH BIBLIOGRAPHY\\n\\n\")\n",
        "        for row in rows:\n",
        "            file.write(format_bibliography_entry(dict(row)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Created {filename}\")\n",
        "    files.download(filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_bibliography_menu():\n",
        "    initialize_bibliography()\n",
        "\n",
        "    while True:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n=== MODULE 11: AUTOMATIC BIBLIOGRAPHY ===\")\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import math\n",
        "from collections import Counter\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "SEARCH_STOPWORDS = {\n",
        "    \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"with\", \"that\",\n",
        "    \"this\", \"from\", \"into\", \"how\", \"why\", \"what\", \"when\", \"where\",\n",
        "    \"does\", \"can\", \"could\", \"would\", \"should\", \"has\", \"have\", \"had\",\n",
        "    \"about\", \"using\", \"use\", \"their\", \"there\", \"these\", \"those\",\n",
        "    \"which\", \"will\", \"not\", \"but\", \"our\", \"its\", \"than\", \"then\"\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_tokens(text):\n",
        "    words = re.findall(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z0-9]+\\b\", (text or \"\").lower())\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        word for word in words\n",
        "        if len(word) > 1 and word not in SEARCH_STOPWORDS\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_discovered_papers():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"discovered_papers\" not in tables:\n",
        "            print(\"Paper database not found. Run Module 5 first.\")\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "\n",
        "        columns = {\n",
        "            row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA table_info(discovered_papers)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        required = {\"title\"}\n",
        "        if not required.issubset(columns):\n",
        "            print(\"The discovered_papers table has an unexpected schema.\")\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "\n",
        "        def field(name, fallback=\"NULL\"):\n",
        "            return name if name in columns else fallback\n",
        "\n",
        "        query = f\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT\n",
        "                {field('openalex_id', 'CAST(id AS TEXT)')} AS paper_id,\n",
        "                title,\n",
        "                {field('authors')} AS authors,\n",
        "                {field('publication_year')} AS year,\n",
        "                {field('doi')} AS doi,\n",
        "                {field('abstract')} AS abstract,\n",
        "                {field('cited_by_count', '0')} AS citations,\n",
        "                {field('paper_url')} AS paper_url\n",
        "            FROM discovered_papers\n",
        "            WHERE title IS NOT NULL\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(query).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"paper_id\": row[0],\n",
        "            \"title\": row[1] or \"\",\n",
        "            \"authors\": row[2] or \"Author unknown\",\n",
        "            \"year\": row[3],\n",
        "            \"doi\": row[4] or \"\",\n",
        "            \"abstract\": row[5] or \"\",\n",
        "            \"citations\": row[6] or 0,\n",
        "            \"paper_url\": row[7] or \"\"\n",
        "        }\n",
        "        for row in rows\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def rank_research_papers(question, limit=10):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Rank locally saved papers using transparent text matching.\"\"\"\n",
        "    query_tokens = search_tokens(question)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not query_tokens:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a more descriptive research question.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    papers = load_discovered_papers()\n",
        "    if not papers:\n",
        "        print(\"No saved papers found. Run Module 5 first.\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    query_set = set(query_tokens)\n",
        "    ranked = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for paper in papers:\n",
        "        title_tokens = search_tokens(paper[\"title\"])\n",
        "        abstract_tokens = search_tokens(paper[\"abstract\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        title_set = set(title_tokens)\n",
        "        abstract_set = set(abstract_tokens)\n",
        "\n",
        "        title_overlap = len(query_set & title_set)\n",
        "        abstract_overlap = len(query_set & abstract_set)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Title matches receive more weight than abstract matches.\n",
        "        title_score = title_overlap / max(1, len(query_set))\n",
        "        abstract_score = abstract_overlap / max(1, len(query_set))\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Small, capped recency bonus. Missing years receive no bonus.\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            year = int(paper[\"year\"])\n",
        "            current_year = 2026\n",
        "            recency_score = max(\n",
        "                0.0, min(1.0, (year - 2000) / (current_year - 2000))\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n",
        "            recency_score = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Log scaling prevents very highly cited papers from dominating.\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            citation_count = max(0, int(paper[\"citations\"]))\n",
        "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n",
        "            citation_count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        citation_score = min(\n",
        "            1.0, math.log1p(citation_count) / math.log1p(1000)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        relevance_score = (\n",
        "            0.65 * title_score\n",
        "            + 0.30 * abstract_score\n",
        "            + 0.03 * recency_score\n",
        "            + 0.02 * citation_score\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        ranked.append({\n",
        "            **paper,\n",
        "            \"score\": relevance_score,\n",
        "            \"title_matches\": sorted(query_set & title_set),\n",
        "            \"abstract_matches\": sorted(query_set & abstract_set)\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    ranked.sort(key=lambda item: item[\"score\"], reverse=True)\n",
        "    return ranked[:max(1, int(limit))]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_advanced_research_search():\n",
        "    print(\"=== MODULE 12: ADVANCED RESEARCH SEARCH ===\")\n",
        "    print(\"Searches papers already saved in your database.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    question = input(\n",
        "        \"\\nEnter your research question or topic: \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    limit_text = input(\n",
        "        \"Maximum results (default 10): \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    limit = int(limit_text) if limit_text.isdigit() else 10\n",
        "    results = rank_research_papers(question, limit)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not results:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n=== TOP {len(results)} PAPER RESULTS ===\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for index, paper in enumerate(results, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n{index}. {paper['title']}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Relevance score: {paper['score']:.3f}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Authors: {paper['authors']}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Year: {paper['year'] or 'Unknown'}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Citations: {paper['citations']}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Title matches: {', '.join(paper['title_matches']) or 'None'}\")\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            \"Abstract matches: \"\n",
        "            + (\", \".join(paper['abstract_matches']) or \"None\")\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if paper[\"doi\"]:\n",
        "            doi = paper[\"doi\"]\n",
        "            if doi.startswith(\"http\"):\n",
        "                print(f\"DOI: {doi}\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                print(f\"DOI: https://doi.org/{doi}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if paper[\"paper_url\"]:\n",
        "            print(f\"Paper link: {paper['paper_url']}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nRanking notes:\")\n",
        "    print(\"- This searches papers already saved by Module 5.\")\n",
        "    print(\"- Higher scores indicate stronger keyword overlap.\")\n",
        "    print(\"- Scores are ranking aids, not probabilities of relevance.\")\n",
        "    print(\"- Review the original papers before relying on their findings.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "run_advanced_research_search()"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import math\n",
        "import statistics\n",
        "import csv\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "\n",
        "STATISTICS_REPORT_FILE = \"research_statistics_report.txt\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def parse_numbers(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Convert comma-separated input into a list of numbers.\"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        values = [float(item.strip()) for item in text.split(\",\")\n",
        "                  if item.strip()]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if len(values) < 2:\n",
        "            print(\"Enter at least two numeric values.\")\n",
        "            return None\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not all(math.isfinite(value) for value in values):\n",
        "            print(\"Please enter only finite numbers.\")\n",
        "            return None\n",
        "\n",
        "        return values\n",
        "\n",
        "    except ValueError:\n",
        "        print(\"Invalid input. Example: 0.81, 0.84, 0.79\")\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def calculate_descriptive_statistics(values):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Calculate common descriptive statistics.\"\"\"\n",
        "    count = len(values)\n",
        "    mean_value = statistics.mean(values)\n",
        "    median_value = statistics.median(values)\n",
        "    std_dev = statistics.stdev(values)\n",
        "    standard_error = std_dev / math.sqrt(count)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Approximate 95% interval using 1.96.\n",
        "    # This is a normal approximation, not an exact small-sample interval.\n",
        "    margin = 1.96 * standard_error\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"count\": count,\n",
        "        \"mean\": mean_value,\n",
        "        \"median\": median_value,\n",
        "        \"minimum\": min(values),\n",
        "        \"maximum\": max(values),\n",
        "        \"range\": max(values) - min(values),\n",
        "        \"sample_std_dev\": std_dev,\n",
        "        \"standard_error\": standard_error,\n",
        "        \"approx_95_ci_low\": mean_value - margin,\n",
        "        \"approx_95_ci_high\": mean_value + margin\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def print_statistics(results):\n",
        "    print(\"\\n=== DESCRIPTIVE STATISTICS ===\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Number of measurements: {results['count']}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Mean: {results['mean']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Median: {results['median']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Minimum: {results['minimum']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Maximum: {results['maximum']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Range: {results['range']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Sample standard deviation: {results['sample_std_dev']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Standard error: {results['standard_error']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"Approximate 95% confidence interval for the mean: \"\n",
        "        f\"[{results['approx_95_ci_low']:.6f}, \"\n",
        "        f\"{results['approx_95_ci_high']:.6f}]\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def pearson_correlation(x_values, y_values):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Calculate Pearson correlation without external libraries.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if len(x_values) != len(y_values) or len(x_values) < 2:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Both groups must have the same length, at least 2.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    mean_x = statistics.mean(x_values)\n",
        "    mean_y = statistics.mean(y_values)\n",
        "\n",
        "    numerator = sum(\n",
        "        (x - mean_x) * (y - mean_y)\n",
        "        for x, y in zip(x_values, y_values)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    denominator_x = sum((x - mean_x) ** 2 for x in x_values)\n",
        "    denominator_y = sum((y - mean_y) ** 2 for y in y_values)\n",
        "\n",
        "    denominator = math.sqrt(denominator_x * denominator_y)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if denominator == 0:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Correlation is undefined when a group has no variation.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return numerator / denominator\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def compare_groups(group_a, group_b):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Compare means and calculate an approximate Welch t statistic.\"\"\"\n",
        "    mean_a = statistics.mean(group_a)\n",
        "    mean_b = statistics.mean(group_b)\n",
        "\n",
        "    var_a = statistics.variance(group_a)\n",
        "    var_b = statistics.variance(group_b)\n",
        "\n",
        "    se_difference = math.sqrt(\n",
        "        var_a / len(group_a) + var_b / len(group_b)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    difference = mean_b - mean_a\n",
        "\n",
        "    if se_difference == 0:\n",
        "        t_statistic = None\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        t_statistic = difference / se_difference\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"group_a_count\": len(group_a),\n",
        "        \"group_b_count\": len(group_b),\n",
        "        \"group_a_mean\": mean_a,\n",
        "        \"group_b_mean\": mean_b,\n",
        "        \"difference_b_minus_a\": difference,\n",
        "        \"welch_t_statistic\": t_statistic\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_statistics_report(report_lines):\n",
        "    with open(STATISTICS_REPORT_FILE, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        file.write(\"\\n\".join(report_lines))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nReport saved as {STATISTICS_REPORT_FILE}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def analyze_one_group():\n",
        "    name = input(\"Enter experiment name: \").strip() or \"Unnamed experiment\"\n",
        "    text = input(\n",
        "        \"Enter measurements separated by commas: \"\n",
        "    ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    values = parse_numbers(text)\n",
        "    if values is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = calculate_descriptive_statistics(values)\n",
        "    print_statistics(results)\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = [\n",
        "        \"RESEARCH STATISTICS REPORT\",\n",
        "        f\"Experiment: {name}\",\n",
        "        f\"Created: {datetime.now().isoformat()}\",\n",
        "        f\"Measurements: {values}\",\n",
        "        \"\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, value in results.items():\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"{key}: {value}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_statistics_report(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def analyze_correlation():\n",
        "    print(\"\\nEnter paired measurements in the same order.\")\n",
        "    x_values = parse_numbers(\n",
        "        input(\"X values (comma-separated): \").strip()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if x_values is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    y_values = parse_numbers(\n",
        "        input(\"Y values (comma-separated): \").strip()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if y_values is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        correlation = pearson_correlation(x_values, y_values)\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nPearson correlation: {correlation:.6f}\")\n",
        "        print(\"Correlation does not establish causation.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Use paired observations that are meaningfully related.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except ValueError as error:\n",
        "        print(error)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def analyze_group_comparison():\n",
        "    print(\"\\nEnter measurements for two groups.\")\n",
        "    group_a = parse_numbers(\n",
        "        input(\"Group A values: \").strip()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if group_a is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    group_b = parse_numbers(\n",
        "        input(\"Group B values: \").strip()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if group_b is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = compare_groups(group_a, group_b)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n=== GROUP COMPARISON ===\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Group A mean: {results['group_a_mean']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Group B mean: {results['group_b_mean']:.6f}\")\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"Difference (B - A): \"\n",
        "        f\"{results['difference_b_minus_a']:.6f}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if results[\"welch_t_statistic\"] is not None:\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            \"Welch t statistic (approximate): \"\n",
        "            f\"{results['welch_t_statistic']:.6f}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        print(\"This is not a p-value or a significance test result.\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(\"Welch t statistic is undefined for these measurements.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"For a reliable statistical test, also calculate degrees of \"\n",
        "        \"freedom and a p-value, and check the study design assumptions.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_measurements_csv():\n",
        "    values = parse_numbers(\n",
        "        input(\"Enter measurements separated by commas: \").strip()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if values is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    filename = \"research_measurements.csv\"\n",
        "    with open(filename, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        writer = csv.writer(file)\n",
        "        writer.writerow([\"measurement_number\", \"value\"])\n",
        "        for index, value in enumerate(values, start=1):\n",
        "            writer.writerow([index, value])\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Created {filename}\")\n",
        "    files.download(filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_statistics_menu():\n",
        "    while True:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n=== MODULE 13: STATISTICAL ANALYSIS ===\")\n",
        "        print(\"1. Descriptive statistics\")\n",
        "        print(\"2. Pearson correlation\")\n",
        "        print(\"3. Compare two groups\")\n",
        "        print(\"4. Export measurements to CSV\")\n",
        "        print(\"0. Exit\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        choice = input(\"Choose an option: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if choice == \"1\":\n",
        "            analyze_one_group()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"2\":\n",
        "            analyze_correlation()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"3\":\n",
        "            analyze_group_comparison()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"4\":\n",
        "            export_measurements_csv()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"0\":\n",
        "            print(\"Statistics module closed.\")\n",
        "            break\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"Invalid choice. Enter 1, 2, 3, 4, or 0.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "run_statistics_menu()"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import csv\n",
        "import statistics\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def initialize_experiment_comparison():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiment_runs (\n",
        "                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                experiment_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                model_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                dataset_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                metric_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "                metric_value REAL NOT NULL,\n",
        "                parameters TEXT,\n",
        "                notes TEXT,\n",
        "                run_date TEXT NOT NULL\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "    print(\"Experiment comparison database is ready.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def record_experiment_run(\n",
        "    experiment_name,\n",
        "    model_name,\n",
        "    dataset_name,\n",
        "    metric_name,\n",
        "    metric_value,\n",
        "    parameters=\"\",\n",
        "    notes=\"\"\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Save one measured experimental result.\"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        metric_value = float(metric_value)\n",
        "    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n",
        "        print(\"Metric value must be numeric.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            INSERT INTO experiment_runs (\n",
        "                experiment_name, model_name, dataset_name,\n",
        "                metric_name, metric_value, parameters, notes, run_date\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "        \"\"\", (\n",
        "            experiment_name.strip(),\n",
        "            model_name.strip(),\n",
        "            dataset_name.strip(),\n",
        "            metric_name.strip(),\n",
        "            metric_value,\n",
        "            parameters.strip(),\n",
        "            notes.strip(),\n",
        "            datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Experiment run saved.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def add_experiment_run_interactive():\n",
        "    print(\"\\nEnter experiment details.\")\n",
        "    experiment_name = input(\"Experiment name: \").strip()\n",
        "    model_name = input(\"Model name: \").strip()\n",
        "    dataset_name = input(\"Dataset name: \").strip()\n",
        "    metric_name = input(\"Metric name (e.g. accuracy): \").strip()\n",
        "    metric_value = input(\"Measured metric value: \").strip()\n",
        "    parameters = input(\"Parameters (optional): \").strip()\n",
        "    notes = input(\"Notes (optional): \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    required = [\n",
        "        experiment_name, model_name, dataset_name,\n",
        "        metric_name, metric_value\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not all(required):\n",
        "        print(\"Please fill in all five required fields.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    record_experiment_run(\n",
        "        experiment_name,\n",
        "        model_name,\n",
        "        dataset_name,\n",
        "        metric_name,\n",
        "        metric_value,\n",
        "        parameters,\n",
        "        notes\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_experiment_runs():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        return conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT *\n",
        "            FROM experiment_runs\n",
        "            ORDER BY experiment_name, run_date, id\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_experiment_runs():\n",
        "    rows = get_experiment_runs()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No runs recorded yet. Choose option 1 first.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n=== RECORDED EXPERIMENT RUNS: {len(rows)} ===\")\n",
        "    for row in rows:\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"\\nRun ID: {row['id']}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Experiment: {row['experiment_name']}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Model: {row['model_name']}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Dataset: {row['dataset_name']}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"{row['metric_name']}: {row['metric_value']}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Parameters: {row['parameters'] or 'Not provided'}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Notes: {row['notes'] or 'None'}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"Date: {row['run_date']}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def compare_experiment_runs():\n",
        "    rows = get_experiment_runs()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No experiment runs available to compare.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    experiment_names = sorted({\n",
        "        row[\"experiment_name\"] for row in rows\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nAvailable experiments:\")\n",
        "    for i, name in enumerate(experiment_names, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"{i}. {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    selection = input(\"Choose an experiment number: \").strip()\n",
        "    if not selection.isdigit():\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a valid number.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    index = int(selection) - 1\n",
        "    if not 0 <= index < len(experiment_names):\n",
        "        print(\"Experiment number is out of range.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    chosen = experiment_names[index]\n",
        "    selected_rows = [\n",
        "        row for row in rows\n",
        "        if row[\"experiment_name\"] == chosen\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    metrics = {}\n",
        "    for row in selected_rows:\n",
        "        key = (\n",
        "            row[\"model_name\"],\n",
        "            row[\"dataset_name\"],\n",
        "            row[\"metric_name\"]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        metrics.setdefault(key, []).append(row[\"metric_value\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n=== COMPARISON: {chosen} ===\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for (model, dataset, metric), values in metrics.items():\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nModel: {model}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Dataset: {dataset}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Metric: {metric}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Runs: {len(values)}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Values: {values}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Mean: {statistics.mean(values):.6f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if len(values) > 1:\n",
        "            print(f\"Sample standard deviation: {statistics.stdev(values):.6f}\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"Standard deviation: requires at least two runs.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_experiment_runs():\n",
        "    rows = get_experiment_runs()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        print(\"No experiment runs to export.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    filename = \"experiment_runs.csv\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(filename, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        writer = csv.writer(file)\n",
        "        writer.writerow([\n",
        "            \"id\", \"experiment_name\", \"model_name\",\n",
        "            \"dataset_name\", \"metric_name\", \"metric_value\",\n",
        "            \"parameters\", \"notes\", \"run_date\"\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "        for row in rows:\n",
        "            writer.writerow([\n",
        "                row[\"id\"], row[\"experiment_name\"],\n",
        "                row[\"model_name\"], row[\"dataset_name\"],\n",
        "                row[\"metric_name\"], row[\"metric_value\"],\n",
        "                row[\"parameters\"], row[\"notes\"], row[\"run_date\"]\n",
        "            ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Exported {len(rows)} runs to {filename}\")\n",
        "    files.download(filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_experiment_comparison_menu():\n",
        "    initialize_experiment_comparison()\n",
        "\n",

        "        choice = input(\"Choose an option: \").strip()\n",

        "            add_experiment_run_interactive()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"2\":\n",
        "            show_experiment_runs()\n",
        "        elif choice == \"3\":\n",

        "        else:\n",

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_database_tables():\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_table_count(table_name):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Count rows only when the table exists.\"\"\"\n",
        "    tables = get_database_tables()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if table_name not in tables:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    # table_name comes only from the internal list below.\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        result = conn.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table_name}\"'\n",
        "        ).fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return result[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_table_preview(table_name, limit=5):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Return a small preview without changing the database.\"\"\"\n",
        "    tables = get_database_tables()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if table_name not in tables:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT * FROM \"{table_name}\" LIMIT ?',\n",
        "            (limit,)\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [dict(row) for row in rows]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_integrated_research_dashboard():\n",
        "    if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):\n",
        "        print(\"research.db was not found in this Colab runtime.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Run your earlier setup cells or restore your database backup.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Tables used by modules throughout the project.\n",
        "    tracked_tables = [\n",
        "        \"research_questions\",\n",
        "        \"research_papers\",\n",
        "        \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "        \"paper_library\",\n",
        "        \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "        \"question_paper_links\",\n",
        "        \"multi_pdf_passages\",\n",
        "        \"evidence_reviews\",\n",
        "        \"research_citations\",\n",
        "        \"research_bibliography\",\n",
        "        \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "        \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "        \"experiments\",\n",
        "        \"experiment_summaries\",\n",
        "        \"experiment_runs\",\n",
        "        \"workspace_notes\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    tables = get_database_tables()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"       AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — WORKSPACE DASHBOARD\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(f\"Generated: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Database: {DB_PATH}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    total_records = 0\n",
        "    existing_tables = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n--- DATABASE OVERVIEW ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for table in tracked_tables:\n",
        "        count = get_table_count(table)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if count is None:\n",
        "            print(f\"{table}: not created yet\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(f\"{table}: {count} records\")\n",
        "            total_records += count\n",
        "            existing_tables += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nTracked tables present: {existing_tables}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Total records in tracked tables: {total_records}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Research questions\n",
        "    if \"research_questions\" in tables:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n--- RECENT RESEARCH QUESTIONS ---\")\n",
        "        for row in get_table_preview(\"research_questions\", 5):\n",
        "            print(row)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Discovered academic papers\n",
        "    if \"discovered_papers\" in tables:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n--- RECENTLY DISCOVERED PAPERS ---\")\n",
        "        for row in get_table_preview(\"discovered_papers\", 5):\n",
        "            print(f\"Title: {row.get('title', 'Untitled')}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"Year: {row.get('publication_year', 'Unknown')}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"DOI: {row.get('doi', 'Not provided')}\")\n",
        "            print(\"-\" * 40)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # PDF evidence\n",
        "    if \"multi_pdf_passages\" in tables:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n--- PDF EVIDENCE LIBRARY ---\")\n",
        "        with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "            rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT paper_name, COUNT(*)\n",
        "                FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "                GROUP BY paper_name\n",
        "                ORDER BY paper_name\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if rows:\n",
        "            for paper, count in rows:\n",
        "                print(f\"{paper}: {count} passages\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"No PDF passages saved yet.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Citation tracker\n",
        "    if \"research_citations\" in tables:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n--- CITATION TRACKER ---\")\n",
        "        for row in get_table_preview(\"research_citations\", 5):\n",
        "            print(\n",
        "                f\"Claim: {row.get('claim', '')}\\n\"\n",
        "                f\"Paper: {row.get('paper_name', '')}\\n\"\n",
        "                f\"Page: {row.get('page_number', '')}\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Experiments\n",
        "    for table in [\"experiments\", \"experiment_runs\"]:\n",
        "        if table in tables:\n",
        "            print(f\"\\n--- RECENT {table.upper()} ---\")\n",
        "            for row in get_table_preview(table, 5):\n",
        "                print(row)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Knowledge graph\n",
        "    if \"knowledge_nodes\" in tables and \"knowledge_edges\" in tables:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n--- KNOWLEDGE GRAPH ---\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Nodes: {get_table_count('knowledge_nodes')}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"Connections: {get_table_count('knowledge_edges')}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n--- DASHBOARD STATUS ---\")\n",
        "    print(\"Dashboard generation complete.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Missing tables are optional modules that have not been initialized.\")\n",
        "    print(\"This dashboard only reads your database; it does not modify it.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_integrated_dashboard():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Save a plain-text dashboard snapshot.\"\"\"\n",
        "    import contextlib\n",
        "    import io\n",
        "\n",
        "    buffer = io.StringIO()\n",
        "\n",
        "    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buffer):\n",
        "        build_integrated_research_dashboard()\n",
        "\n",
        "    report = buffer.getvalue()\n",
        "    filename = \"integrated_research_dashboard.txt\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(filename, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "        file.write(report)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(report)\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nDashboard snapshot saved to {filename}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from google.colab import files\n",
        "        files.download(filename)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        print(\"Automatic download is unavailable. The report is saved locally.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_integrated_dashboard_menu():\n",
        "    while True:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n=== MODULE 15: INTEGRATED DASHBOARD ===\")\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "EXPECTED_TABLES = [\n",
        "    \"research_questions\",\n",
        "    \"research_papers\",\n",
        "    \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "    \"paper_library\",\n",
        "    \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "    \"question_paper_links\",\n",
        "    \"multi_pdf_passages\",\n",
        "    \"evidence_reviews\",\n",
        "    \"research_citations\",\n",
        "    \"research_bibliography\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "    \"experiments\",\n",
        "    \"experiment_summaries\",\n",
        "    \"experiment_runs\",\n",
        "    \"workspace_notes\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_project_audit():\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — PROJECT AUDIT\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"Time:\", datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):\n",
        "        print(\"\\nERROR: research.db was not found.\")\n",
        "        print(\"Check that you are using the correct Colab runtime.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "            # 1. Verify SQLite database integrity.\n",
        "            integrity = conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "            integrity_messages = [row[0] for row in integrity]\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"\\n1. DATABASE INTEGRITY\")\n",
        "            for message in integrity_messages:\n",
        "                print(message)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # 2. Find actual tables.\n",
        "            actual_tables = {\n",
        "                row[0]\n",
        "                for row in conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                    SELECT name\n",
        "                    FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "                    WHERE type = 'table'\n",
        "                \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"\\n2. TABLE AUDIT\")\n",
        "            present_count = 0\n",
        "            missing_tables = []\n",
        "\n",
        "            for table in EXPECTED_TABLES:\n",
        "                if table in actual_tables:\n",
        "                    count = conn.execute(\n",
        "                        f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table}\"'\n",
        "                    ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "                    print(f\"[PRESENT] {table}: {count} records\")\n",
        "                    present_count += 1\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    print(f\"[MISSING] {table}\")\n",
        "                    missing_tables.append(table)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # 3. Check required tables for important columns.\n",
        "            print(\"\\n3. REQUIRED COLUMN CHECK\")\n",
        "            required_columns = {\n",
        "                \"discovered_papers\": [\"title\", \"doi\", \"abstract\"],\n",
        "                \"multi_pdf_passages\": [\n",
        "                    \"paper_name\", \"page_number\", \"passage_text\"\n",
        "                ],\n",
        "                \"research_citations\": [\n",
        "                    \"claim\", \"paper_name\", \"page_number\"\n",
        "                ],\n",
        "                \"experiment_runs\": [\n",
        "                    \"experiment_name\", \"metric_name\", \"metric_value\"\n",
        "                ]\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            for table, required in required_columns.items():\n",
        "                if table not in actual_tables:\n",
        "                    print(f\"[SKIP] {table}: table is missing\")\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                columns = {\n",
        "                    row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                        f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")'\n",
        "                    ).fetchall()\n",
        "                }\n",
        "\n",
        "                missing_columns = [\n",
        "                    column for column in required\n",
        "                    if column not in columns\n",
        "                ]\n",
        "\n",
        "                if missing_columns:\n",
        "                    print(\n",
        "                        f\"[CHECK] {table}: missing columns \"\n",
        "                        f\"{missing_columns}\"\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    print(f\"[OK] {table}: required columns present\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # 4. List indexes.\n",
        "            print(\"\\n4. INDEX SUMMARY\")\n",
        "            indexes = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT name, tbl_name\n",
        "                FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "                WHERE type = 'index'\n",
        "                  AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "                ORDER BY tbl_name, name\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if indexes:\n",
        "                for index_name, table_name in indexes:\n",
        "                    print(f\"{table_name}: {index_name}\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                print(\"No custom indexes found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # 5. Basic data quality checks.\n",
        "            print(\"\\n5. BASIC DATA QUALITY\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"discovered_papers\" in actual_tables:\n",
        "                columns = {\n",
        "                    row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                        \"PRAGMA table_info(discovered_papers)\"\n",
        "                    ).fetchall()\n",
        "                }\n",
        "\n",
        "                if \"title\" in columns:\n",
        "                    empty_titles = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                        SELECT COUNT(*)\n",
        "                        FROM discovered_papers\n",
        "                        WHERE title IS NULL OR TRIM(title) = ''\n",
        "                    \"\"\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "                    print(f\"Discovered papers with empty titles: {empty_titles}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                if {\"doi\", \"title\"}.issubset(columns):\n",
        "                    missing_doi = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                        SELECT COUNT(*)\n",
        "                        FROM discovered_papers\n",
        "                        WHERE doi IS NULL OR TRIM(doi) = ''\n",
        "                    \"\"\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "                    print(f\"Papers without a DOI: {missing_doi}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"multi_pdf_passages\" in actual_tables:\n",
        "                empty_passages = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                    SELECT COUNT(*)\n",
        "                    FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "                    WHERE passage_text IS NULL\n",
        "                       OR TRIM(passage_text) = ''\n",
        "                \"\"\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(f\"Empty PDF passages: {empty_passages}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # 6. Summary.\n",
        "            print(\"\\n6. AUDIT SUMMARY\")\n",
        "            print(f\"Expected tables: {len(EXPECTED_TABLES)}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"Tables present: {present_count}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"Tables missing: {len(missing_tables)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if integrity_messages == [\"ok\"]:\n",
        "                print(\"Database integrity: PASS\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                print(\"Database integrity: CHECK REQUIRED\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if missing_tables:\n",
        "                print(\"\\nMissing tables may mean their modules have\")\n",
        "                print(\"not been run. They are not automatically errors.\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                print(\"All expected module tables are present.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"\\nAudit finished. No records were changed.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except sqlite3.Error as error:\n",
        "        print(\"\\nDATABASE ERROR:\", error)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "run_project_audit()"

        "import os\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "BACKUP_PATH = \"research_backup.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    print(\"ERROR: research.db was not found.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    source = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "    backup = sqlite3.connect(BACKUP_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        source.backup(backup)\n",
        "        result = backup.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if result == \"ok\":\n",
        "            print(\"Backup created successfully!\")\n",
        "            print(\"Database integrity:\", result)\n",
        "            print(\"Backup file:\", BACKUP_PATH)\n",
        "            files.download(BACKUP_PATH)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"Backup integrity check failed:\", result)\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        backup.close()\n",
        "        source.close()"
      ],

            "application/javascript": [
              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },

        "import os\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "required_tables = [\n",
        "    \"research_questions\",\n",
        "    \"research_papers\",\n",
        "    \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "    \"paper_library\",\n",
        "    \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "    \"question_paper_links\",\n",
        "    \"multi_pdf_passages\",\n",
        "    \"evidence_reviews\",\n",
        "    \"research_citations\",\n",
        "    \"research_bibliography\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "    \"experiments\",\n",
        "    \"experiment_summaries\",\n",
        "    \"experiment_runs\",\n",
        "    \"workspace_notes\",\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    print(\"ERROR: research.db was not found.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Make sure you are using the same Colab runtime as before.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    connection = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        cursor = connection.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "        integrity = cursor.execute(\n",
        "            \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        existing_tables = {\n",
        "            row[\"name\"]\n",
        "            for row in cursor.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master \"\n",
        "                \"WHERE type = 'table'\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "        print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — INTEGRATION TEST\")\n",
        "        print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "        print(\"Database integrity:\", integrity)\n",
        "\n",
        "        passed = 0\n",
        "        failed = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        for table in required_tables:\n",
        "            if table not in existing_tables:\n",
        "                print(f\"[FAIL] Missing table: {table}\")\n",
        "                failed += 1\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                count = cursor.execute(\n",
        "                    f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table}\"'\n",
        "                ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(f\"[PASS] {table}: readable ({count} records)\")\n",
        "                passed += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            except sqlite3.Error as error:\n",
        "                print(f\"[FAIL] {table}: {error}\")\n",
        "                failed += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"-\" * 45)\n",
        "        print(\"Tables tested:\", len(required_tables))\n",
        "        print(\"Passed:\", passed)\n",
        "        print(\"Failed:\", failed)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if integrity == \"ok\" and failed == 0:\n",
        "            print(\"RESULT: DATABASE INTEGRATION CHECK PASSED\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"RESULT: CHECK THE FAILURES ABOVE\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        connection.close()"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import os\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    print(\"ERROR: research.db was not found.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[\"name\"]\n",
        "            for row in con.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "        print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — WORKFLOW TEST\")\n",
        "        print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 1. Check saved research questions\n",
        "        if \"research_questions\" in tables:\n",
        "            questions = con.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT * FROM research_questions LIMIT 5\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "            print(\"\\n1. Saved research questions:\", len(questions))\n",
        "            for q in questions:\n",
        "                print(\" -\", dict(q))\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"\\n1. Research questions table missing\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 2. Check discovered academic papers\n",
        "        if \"discovered_papers\" in tables:\n",
        "            papers = con.execute(\n",
        "                \"\"\"SELECT title, publication_year, doi\n",
        "                   FROM discovered_papers LIMIT 5\"\"\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "            print(\"\\n2. Discovered papers:\", len(papers))\n",
        "            for p in papers:\n",
        "                print(\" -\", p[\"title\"], \"|\", p[\"publication_year\"],\n",
        "                      \"| DOI:\", p[\"doi\"])\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"\\n2. Discovered papers table missing\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 3. Check evidence saved from PDFs\n",
        "        if \"multi_pdf_passages\" in tables:\n",
        "            passages = con.execute(\n",
        "                \"\"\"SELECT paper_name, page_number, passage_text\n",
        "                   FROM multi_pdf_passages LIMIT 5\"\"\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "            print(\"\\n3. PDF evidence passages:\", len(passages))\n",
        "            for p in passages:\n",
        "                excerpt = (p[\"passage_text\"] or \"\").replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n",
        "                print(\" -\", p[\"paper_name\"], \"| page\", p[\"page_number\"])\n",
        "                print(\"   \", excerpt[:180])\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"\\n3. PDF passages table missing\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 4. Check citation traceability\n",
        "        if \"research_citations\" in tables:\n",
        "            citation_count = con.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM research_citations\"\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "            print(\"\\n4. Saved citations:\", citation_count)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"\\n4. Citations table missing\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 5. Create a simple diagnostic report\n",
        "        report = [\n",
        "            \"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — WORKFLOW DIAGNOSTIC\",\n",
        "            \"Database integrity: \" + con.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0],\n",
        "            \"Research questions: \" + str(\n",
        "                con.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM research_questions\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "            ) if \"research_questions\" in tables else \"Research questions table missing\",\n",
        "            \"Discovered papers: \" + str(\n",
        "                con.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM discovered_papers\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "            ) if \"discovered_papers\" in tables else \"Discovered papers table missing\",\n",
        "            \"PDF passages: \" + str(\n",
        "                con.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM multi_pdf_passages\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "            ) if \"multi_pdf_passages\" in tables else \"PDF passages table missing\",\n",
        "            \"Citations: \" + str(\n",
        "                con.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM research_citations\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "            ) if \"research_citations\" in tables else \"Citations table missing\",\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        report_path = \"workflow_diagnostic.txt\"\n",
        "        with open(report_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "            f.write(\"\\n\".join(report))\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\n5. Diagnostic report created:\", report_path)\n",
        "        print(\"\\nWorkflow diagnostic finished.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except sqlite3.Error as e:\n",
        "        print(\"Database query error:\", e)\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        con.close()"
      ],
