      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "con = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    passages = con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT paper_name, page_number, passage_number, passage_text\n",
        "        FROM multi_pdf_passages\n",
        "        ORDER BY paper_name, page_number, passage_number\n",
        "        LIMIT 20\n",
        "    \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not passages:\n",
        "        print(\"No PDF passages found.\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(\"AVAILABLE PASSAGES\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        for i, p in enumerate(passages, 1):\n",
        "            excerpt = (p[\"passage_text\"] or \"\").replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n",
        "            print(f\"{i}. Paper: {p['paper_name']}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"   Page: {p['page_number']}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"   Passage number: {p['passage_number']}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"   Evidence: {excerpt[:250]}\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        choice = input(\n",
        "            f\"Choose a passage number (1-{len(passages)}): \"\n",
        "        ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not choice.isdigit() or not 1 <= int(choice) <= len(passages):\n",
        "            print(\"Invalid selection. No citation saved.\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            p = passages[int(choice) - 1]\n",
        "            claim = input(\n",
        "                \"Enter the research claim this passage may support: \"\n",
        "            ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not claim:\n",
        "                print(\"Claim cannot be empty. No citation saved.\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                note = input(\n",
        "                    \"Citation note (optional; press Enter to skip): \"\n",
        "                ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "                con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                    INSERT INTO research_citations\n",
        "                    (claim, paper_name, page_number, passage_number,\n",
        "                     quoted_evidence, citation_note, created_at)\n",
        "                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)\n",
        "                \"\"\", (\n",
        "                    claim,\n",
        "                    p[\"paper_name\"],\n",
        "                    p[\"page_number\"],\n",
        "                    p[\"passage_number\"],\n",
        "                    p[\"passage_text\"],\n",
        "                    note\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "                con.commit()\n",
        "                print(\"\\nCitation saved successfully!\")\n",
        "                print(\"Paper:\", p[\"paper_name\"])\n",
        "                print(\"Page:\", p[\"page_number\"])\n",
        "                print(\"Passage:\", p[\"passage_number\"])\n",
        "                print(\"Remember to verify that the evidence supports your claim.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    con.close()"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"

The best performing models also connect the encoder and decoder through an attention mechanism. We propose a new simple network architecture, the Transformer, based solely on attention mechanisms, dispensing with recurrence and convolutions entirely.         "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "# Find the existing database\n",
        "possible_paths = [\n",
        "    \"/content/research.db\",\n",
        "    \"research.db\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = next(\n",
        "    (p for p in possible_paths if os.path.exists(p)),\n",
        "    None\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if DB_PATH is None:\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db was not found. Check that your Colab database still exists.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "cursor = conn.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "def table_exists(name):\n",
        "    cursor.execute(\n",
        "        \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?\",\n",
        "        (name,)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    return cursor.fetchone() is not None\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_columns(name):\n",
        "    cursor.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{name}\")')\n",
        "    return [row[\"name\"] for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n",
        "\n",
        "if not table_exists(\"research_citations\"):\n",
        "    conn.close()\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"The research_citations table does not exist.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Read existing citations using the actual available columns\n",
        "citation_columns = get_columns(\"research_citations\")\n",
        "cursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM research_citations ORDER BY id\")\n",
        "citations = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build a readable bibliography from the saved citation records\n",
        "lines = [\n",
        "    \"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\",\n",
        "    \"Research Bibliography Draft\",\n",
        "    \"Generated: \" + datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\"),\n",
        "    \"=\" * 60,\n",
        "    \"\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "if not citations:\n",
        "    lines.append(\"No saved citations were found.\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"Save at least one citation before generating the bibliography.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    for number, citation in enumerate(citations, start=1):\n",
        "        paper = (\n",
        "            citation.get(\"paper_name\")\n",
        "            or citation.get(\"title\")\n",
        "            or \"Unknown paper\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        page = citation.get(\"page_number\")\n",
        "        passage = citation.get(\"passage_number\")\n",
        "        claim = citation.get(\"claim\") or \"\"\n",
        "        evidence = citation.get(\"quoted_evidence\") or \"\"\n",
        "        note = citation.get(\"citation_note\") or \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"[{number}] {paper}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if page is not None:\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"    Page: {page}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if passage is not None:\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"    Passage: {passage}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if claim:\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"    Claim: {claim}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if evidence:\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"    Evidence: {evidence}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if note:\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"    Note: {note}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.extend([\n",
        "        \"VERIFICATION REMINDER\",\n",
        "        \"Confirm each paper's author, title, publication year, venue,\",\n",
        "        \"and DOI from the original source before using this bibliography\",\n",
        "        \"in an academic submission.\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"This is a traceability draft, not a verified citation style.\"\n",
        "    ])\n",
        "\n",
        "output_path = \"/content/research_bibliography.txt\"\n",
        "with open(output_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "    file.write(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n",
        "\n",
        "# Add generated bibliography records to the existing table if compatible.\n",
        "# The text export is still created if the table schema differs.\n",
        "if table_exists(\"research_bibliography\"):\n",
        "    bibliography_columns = get_columns(\"research_bibliography\")\n",
        "    print(\"Existing bibliography table found.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Its columns are:\", \", \".join(bibliography_columns))\n",
        "    print(\"Your existing table was left unchanged.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"No bibliography table found; no table was created.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nBibliography draft created:\")\n",
        "print(output_path)\n",
        "print(\"Citation records included:\", len(citations))\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    from google.colab import files\n",
        "    files.download(output_path)\n",
        "except ImportError:\n",
        "    print(\"Download the text file from the Colab Files panel.\")"

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import re\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "# Find the existing database\n",
        "DB_PATH = next(\n",
        "    (p for p in [\"/content/research.db\", \"research.db\"]\n",
        "     if os.path.exists(p)),\n",
        "    None\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if DB_PATH is None:\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db not found. Make sure your existing database is available.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "cursor = conn.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "def existing_tables():\n",
        "    cursor.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "        WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "        ORDER BY name\n",
        "    \"\"\")\n",
        "    return [r[\"name\"] for r in cursor.fetchall()]\n",
        "\n",
        "def columns(table):\n",
        "    cursor.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")')\n",
        "    return [r[\"name\"] for r in cursor.fetchall()]\n",
        "\n",
        "def search_table(table, query, limit=10):\n",
        "    cols = columns(table)\n",
        "    if not cols:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Search only text-like columns, avoiding numeric IDs\n",
        "    text_cols = []\n",
        "    for col in cols:\n",
        "        cursor.execute(\n",
        "            f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")'\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        info = {r[\"name\"]: r[\"type\"] for r in cursor.fetchall()}\n",
        "        if any(x in (info.get(col) or \"\").upper()\n",
        "               for x in [\"TEXT\", \"CHAR\", \"CLOB\"]):\n",
        "            text_cols.append(col)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text_cols:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    conditions = \" OR \".join(\n",
        "        f'CAST(\"{col}\" AS TEXT) LIKE ?' for col in text_cols\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    params = [f\"%{query}%\"] * len(text_cols)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        cursor.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT * FROM \"{table}\" WHERE {conditions} LIMIT ?',\n",
        "            params + [limit]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]\n",
        "    except sqlite3.Error:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_results(table, rows):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- {table} ({len(rows)} result(s)) ---\")\n",
        "    for i, row in enumerate(rows, 1):\n",
        "        print(f\"\\nResult {i}\")\n",
        "        for key, value in row.items():\n",
        "            if value is not None and str(value).strip():\n",
        "                text = str(value)\n",
        "                if len(text) > 700:\n",
        "                    text = text[:700] + \"...\"\n",
        "                print(f\"{key}: {text}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "tables = existing_tables()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — INTERACTIVE RESEARCH ASSISTANT\")\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "# Locate existing database\n",
        "DB_PATH = next(\n",
        "    (p for p in [\"/content/research.db\", \"research.db\"]\n",
        "     if os.path.exists(p)),\n",
        "    None\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if DB_PATH is None:\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db was not found. Please restore your existing database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "cursor = conn.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_tables():\n",
        "    cursor.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "        WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "        ORDER BY name\n",
        "    \"\"\")\n",
        "    return [row[\"name\"] for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_columns(table):\n",
        "    cursor.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")')\n",
        "    return [row[\"name\"] for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n",
        "\n",
        "def count_rows(table):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        cursor.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM \"{table}\"')\n",
        "        return cursor.fetchone()[\"n\"]\n",
        "    except sqlite3.Error:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "tables = get_tables()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Verify database integrity\n",
        "cursor.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\")\n",
        "integrity = cursor.fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Table summaries\n",
        "summary = []\n",
        "for table in tables:\n",
        "    summary.append({\n",
        "        \"table\": table,\n",
        "        \"records\": count_rows(table),\n",
        "        \"columns\": len(get_columns(table))\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "# Select useful high-level counts\n",
        "def first_existing(*names):\n",
        "    return next((name for name in names if name in tables), None)\n",
        "\n",
        "important_tables = {\n",
        "    \"Research questions\": first_existing(\"research_questions\"),\n",
        "    \"Discovered papers\": first_existing(\"discovered_papers\"),\n",
        "    \"Paper library\": first_existing(\"research_papers\"),\n",
        "    \"Evidence\": first_existing(\"research_evidence\"),\n",
        "    \"PDF passages\": first_existing(\"multi_pdf_passages\"),\n",
        "    \"Citations\": first_existing(\"research_citations\"),\n",
        "    \"Bibliography\": first_existing(\"research_bibliography\"),\n",
        "    \"Experiments\": first_existing(\"experiments\"),\n",
        "    \"Experiment runs\": first_existing(\"experiment_runs\"),\n",
        "    \"Knowledge nodes\": first_existing(\"knowledge_nodes\"),\n",
        "    \"Knowledge edges\": first_existing(\"knowledge_edges\"),\n",
        "    \"Workspace notes\": first_existing(\"workspace_notes\")\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "report = []\n",
        "report.append(\"=\" * 65)\n",
        "report.append(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — MASTER DASHBOARD\")\n",
        "report.append(\"=\" * 65)\n",
        "report.append(\"Generated: \" + datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"))\n",
        "report.append(\"Database: \" + DB_PATH)\n",
        "report.append(\"Database integrity: \" + str(integrity))\n",
        "report.append(\"Total tables: \" + str(len(tables)))\n",
        "report.append(\"\")\n",
        "report.append(\"KEY PROJECT COUNTS\")\n",
        "report.append(\"-\" * 65)\n",
        "\n",
        "for label, table in important_tables.items():\n",
        "    if table:\n",
        "        report.append(f\"{label}: {count_rows(table)}\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        report.append(f\"{label}: table not found\")\n",
        "\n",
        "report.append(\"\")\n",
        "report.append(\"ALL TABLES\")\n",
        "report.append(\"-\" * 65)\n",
        "\n",
        "for item in summary:\n",
        "    report.append(\n",
        "        f\"{item['table']}: {item['records']} records, \"\n",
        "        f\"{item['columns']} columns\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# Add simple project readiness checks\n",
        "report.append(\"\")\n",
        "report.append(\"PROJECT READINESS CHECKS\")\n",
        "report.append(\"-\" * 65)\n",
        "\n",
        "checks = [\n",
        "    (\"Database integrity\", integrity == \"ok\", str(integrity)),\n",
        "    (\"Research questions table\", \"research_questions\" in tables,\n",
        "     \"Table exists\" if \"research_questions\" in tables else \"Table missing\"),\n",
        "    (\"Citation tracking table\", \"research_citations\" in tables,\n",
        "     \"Table exists\" if \"research_citations\" in tables else \"Table missing\"),\n",
        "    (\"PDF passage table\", \"multi_pdf_passages\" in tables,\n",
        "     \"Table exists\" if \"multi_pdf_passages\" in tables else \"Table missing\"),\n",
        "    (\"Experiment tracking table\",\n",
        "     \"experiments\" in tables or \"experiment_runs\" in tables,\n",
        "     \"At least one experiment table exists\"\n",
        "     if (\"experiments\" in tables or \"experiment_runs\" in tables)\n",
        "     else \"No experiment table found\")\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "for label, passed, detail in checks:\n",
        "    status = \"PASS\" if passed else \"CHECK\"\n",
        "    report.append(f\"[{status}] {label}: {detail}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "report.append(\"\")\n",
        "report.append(\"INTERPRETATION\")\n",
        "report.append(\"-\" * 65)\n",
        "report.append(\n",
        "    \"This dashboard summarizes database structure and record counts. \"\n",
        "    \"It does not prove that research claims are scientifically correct.\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "dashboard_text = \"\\n\".join(report)\n",
        "print(dashboard_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save a report without modifying research tables\n",
        "output_path = \"/content/ai_research_master_dashboard.txt\"\n",
        "with open(output_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",

            "application/javascript": [
              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import random\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import sklearn\n",
        "from sklearn.datasets import load_digits\n",
        "from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split\n",
        "from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, precision_score, recall_score, f1_score\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Find the existing database\n",
        "DB_PATH = next(\n",
        "    (p for p in [\"/content/research.db\", \"research.db\"]\n",
        "     if os.path.exists(p)),\n",
        "    None\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if DB_PATH is None:\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db was not found. Restore your existing database first.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. Load a real dataset\n",
        "digits = load_digits()\n",
        "X, y = digits.data, digits.target\n",
        "\n",
        "# 3. Experiment settings\n",
        "SEED = 42\n",
        "TEST_SIZE = 0.20\n",
        "HIDDEN_LAYER_SIZE = 64\n",
        "MAX_ITERATIONS = 300\n",
        "LEARNING_RATE = 0.001\n",
        "\n",
        "random.seed(SEED)\n",
        "np.random.seed(SEED)\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(\n",
        "    X, y,\n",
        "    test_size=TEST_SIZE,\n",
        "    random_state=SEED,\n",
        "    stratify=y\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# 4. Train model\n",
        "model = MLPClassifier(\n",
        "    hidden_layer_sizes=(HIDDEN_LAYER_SIZE,),\n",
        "    max_iter=MAX_ITERATIONS,\n",
        "    learning_rate_init=LEARNING_RATE,\n",
        "    random_state=SEED,\n",
        "    early_stopping=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "model.fit(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "predictions = model.predict(X_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 5. Calculate metrics\n",
        "results = {\n",
        "    \"accuracy\": float(accuracy_score(y_test, predictions)),\n",
        "    \"precision_macro\": float(\n",
        "        precision_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    \"recall_macro\": float(\n",
        "        recall_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    \"f1_macro\": float(\n",
        "        f1_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "settings = {\n",
        "    \"dataset\": \"scikit-learn digits\",\n",
        "    \"model\": \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "    \"hidden_layer_sizes\": [HIDDEN_LAYER_SIZE],\n",
        "    \"max_iter\": MAX_ITERATIONS,\n",
        "    \"learning_rate_init\": LEARNING_RATE,\n",
        "    \"test_size\": TEST_SIZE,\n",
        "    \"seed\": SEED,\n",
        "    \"scikit_learn_version\": sklearn.__version__,\n",
        "    \"numpy_version\": np.__version__\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# 6. Save to the existing database without deleting records\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "cursor = conn.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "cursor.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiment_runs (\n",
        "        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "        experiment_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "        dataset_name TEXT,\n",
        "        model_name TEXT,\n",
        "        settings_json TEXT,\n",
        "        accuracy REAL,\n",
        "        precision_macro REAL,\n",
        "        recall_macro REAL,\n",
        "        f1_macro REAL,\n",
        "        random_seed INTEGER,\n",
        "        created_at TEXT\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Support the existing table if it has a different schema.\n",
        "cursor.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\")\n",
        "existing_columns = {\n",
        "    row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "values = {\n",
        "    \"experiment_name\": \"Digits classification baseline\",\n",
        "    \"dataset_name\": \"scikit-learn digits\",\n",
        "    \"model_name\": \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "    \"settings_json\": json.dumps(settings, sort_keys=True),\n",
        "    \"accuracy\": results[\"accuracy\"],\n",
        "    \"precision_macro\": results[\"precision_macro\"],\n",
        "    \"recall_macro\": results[\"recall_macro\"],\n",
        "    \"f1_macro\": results[\"f1_macro\"],\n",
        "    \"random_seed\": SEED,\n",
        "    \"created_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "insertable = {\n",
        "    key: value for key, value in values.items()\n",
        "    if key in existing_columns\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "if not insertable:\n",
        "    conn.close()\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "        \"The existing experiment_runs table has no compatible columns. \"\n",
        "        \"Your trained results are printed below, but were not saved.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "column_sql = \", \".join(f'\"{key}\"' for key in insertable)\n",
        "placeholder_sql = \", \".join(\"?\" for _ in insertable)\n",
        "\n",
        "cursor.execute(\n",
        "    f\"INSERT INTO experiment_runs ({column_sql}) \"\n",
        "    f\"VALUES ({placeholder_sql})\",\n",
        "    list(insertable.values())\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "conn.commit()\n",
        "run_id = cursor.lastrowid\n",
        "\n",
        "# 7. Show saved experiment history\n",
        "cursor.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\")\n",
        "print(\"\\nEXPERIMENT RUN RESULTS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 45)\n",
        "print(\"Run ID:\", run_id)\n",
        "print(\"Model:\", \"MLPClassifier\")\n",
        "print(\"Dataset:\", \"scikit-learn digits\")\n",
        "for name, value in results.items():\n",
        "    print(f\"{name}: {value:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nSettings:\")\n",
        "print(json.dumps(settings, indent=2))\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nRecent saved runs:\")\n",
        "cursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM experiment_runs ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 5\")\n",
        "for row in cursor.fetchall():\n",
        "    print(row)\n",
        "\n",
        "conn.close()\n",
        "print(\"\\nExperiment finished and saved where compatible.\")"

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import sklearn\n",
        "from sklearn.datasets import load_digits\n",
        "from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split\n",
        "from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import (\n",
        "    accuracy_score, precision_score, recall_score, f1_score\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Find existing database\n",
        "DB_PATH = next(\n",
        "    (p for p in [\"/content/research.db\", \"research.db\"]\n",
        "     if os.path.exists(p)),\n",
        "    None\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if DB_PATH is None:\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\"Existing research.db was not found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Run the experiment again\n",
        "digits = load_digits()\n",
        "X, y = digits.data, digits.target\n",
        "\n",
        "SEED = 42\n",
        "X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(\n",
        "    X, y, test_size=0.2, random_state=SEED, stratify=y\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "model = MLPClassifier(\n",
        "    hidden_layer_sizes=(64,),\n",
        "    max_iter=300,\n",
        "    learning_rate_init=0.001,\n",
        "    random_state=SEED,\n",
        "    early_stopping=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "model.fit(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "predictions = model.predict(X_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "metrics = {\n",
        "    \"accuracy\": float(accuracy_score(y_test, predictions)),\n",
        "    \"precision_macro\": float(\n",
        "        precision_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    \"recall_macro\": float(\n",
        "        recall_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    \"f1_macro\": float(\n",
        "        f1_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "settings = {\n",
        "    \"dataset\": \"scikit-learn digits\",\n",
        "    \"model\": \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "    \"hidden_layer_sizes\": [64],\n",
        "    \"max_iter\": 300,\n",
        "    \"learning_rate_init\": 0.001,\n",
        "    \"test_size\": 0.2,\n",
        "    \"random_seed\": SEED,\n",
        "    \"scikit_learn_version\": sklearn.__version__,\n",
        "    \"numpy_version\": np.__version__\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "cursor = conn.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "cursor.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\")\n",
        "schema = cursor.fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "if not schema:\n",
        "    conn.close()\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Table experiment_runs was not found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "columns = [row[\"name\"] for row in schema]\n",
        "required = [\n",
        "    row[\"name\"] for row in schema\n",
        "    if row[\"notnull\"] and row[\"dflt_value\"] is None\n",
        "    and row[\"pk\"] == 0\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Existing experiment_runs columns:\", columns)\n",
        "print(\"Required fields:\", required)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Identify common field names used by different schema designs.\n",
        "base = {\n",
        "    \"experiment_name\": \"Digits classification baseline\",\n",
        "    \"name\": \"Digits classification baseline\",\n",
        "    \"dataset_name\": \"scikit-learn digits\",\n",
        "    \"dataset\": \"scikit-learn digits\",\n",
        "    \"model_name\": \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "    \"model\": \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "    \"settings_json\": json.dumps(settings, sort_keys=True),\n",
        "    \"parameters\": json.dumps(settings, sort_keys=True),\n",
        "    \"config\": json.dumps(settings, sort_keys=True),\n",
        "    \"random_seed\": SEED,\n",
        "    \"seed\": SEED,\n",
        "    \"created_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n",
        "    \"run_date\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n",
        "    \"timestamp\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n",
        "    \"metric_name\": \"accuracy\",\n",
        "    \"metric_value\": metrics[\"accuracy\"],\n",
        "    \"metric\": \"accuracy\",\n",
        "    \"value\": metrics[\"accuracy\"],\n",
        "    \"accuracy\": metrics[\"accuracy\"],\n",
        "    \"precision_macro\": metrics[\"precision_macro\"],\n",
        "    \"recall_macro\": metrics[\"recall_macro\"],\n",
        "    \"f1_macro\": metrics[\"f1_macro\"],\n",
        "    \"notes\": \"Metrics from a reproducible digits classification run.\"\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# Prepare a row for the existing table, including its required fields.\n",
        "row_values = {}\n",
        "for col in columns:\n",
        "    if col in base:\n",
        "        row_values[col] = base[col]\n",
        "    elif col in (\"id\", \"run_id\") and any(\n",
        "        r[\"name\"] == col and r[\"pk\"] for r in schema\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "    elif any(r[\"name\"] == col and r[\"notnull\"]\n",
        "             and r[\"dflt_value\"] is None and r[\"pk\"] == 0\n",
        "             for r in schema):\n",
        "        # Unknown mandatory columns cannot safely be guessed.\n",
        "        row_values[col] = None\n",
        "\n",
        "# A metric_name/metric_value schema usually expects one row per metric.\n",
        "if \"metric_name\" in columns:\n",
        "    saved = 0\n",
        "    for metric_name, metric_value in metrics.items():\n",
        "        values = dict(row_values)\n",
        "        values[\"metric_name\"] = metric_name\n",
        "        if \"metric_value\" in columns:\n",
        "            values[\"metric_value\"] = metric_value\n",
        "        elif \"value\" in columns:\n",
        "            values[\"value\"] = metric_value\n",
        "        elif \"metric\" in columns:\n",
        "            values[\"metric\"] = metric_name\n",
        "\n",
        "        # If the schema has other mandatory fields we cannot safely map,\n",
        "        # stop before inserting incomplete data.\n",
        "        unresolved = [\n",
        "            r[\"name\"] for r in schema\n",
        "            if r[\"notnull\"] and r[\"dflt_value\"] is None\n",
        "            and r[\"pk\"] == 0\n",
        "            and (r[\"name\"] not in values or values[r[\"name\"]] is None)\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "        if unresolved:\n",
        "            print(\"Cannot safely insert; unmapped required fields:\", unresolved)\n",
        "            print(\"No experiment rows were inserted.\")\n",
        "            conn.close()\n",
        "            raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "                \"Your table has additional required fields. \"\n",
        "                \"The existing data was left unchanged.\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        insert_values = {\n",
        "            k: v for k, v in values.items()\n",
        "            if k in columns and v is not None\n",
        "        }\n",
        "        names = \", \".join(f'\"{k}\"' for k in insert_values)\n",
        "        marks = \", \".join(\"?\" for _ in insert_values)\n",
        "        cursor.execute(\n",
        "            f'INSERT INTO experiment_runs ({names}) VALUES ({marks})',\n",
        "            list(insert_values.values())\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        saved += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    conn.commit()\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nSaved {saved} metric records.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    unresolved = [\n",
        "        r[\"name\"] for r in schema\n",
        "        if r[\"notnull\"] and r[\"dflt_value\"] is None\n",
        "        and r[\"pk\"] == 0\n",
        "        and (r[\"name\"] not in row_values or row_values[r[\"name\"]] is None)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if unresolved:\n",
        "        conn.close()\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            \"Cannot safely save to this schema. Unmapped required columns: \"\n",
        "            + \", \".join(unresolved)\n",
        "            + \". No experiment rows were inserted.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    insert_values = {\n",
        "        k: v for k, v in row_values.items()\n",
        "        if k in columns and v is not None\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    names = \", \".join(f'\"{k}\"' for k in insert_values)\n",
        "    marks = \", \".join(\"?\" for _ in insert_values)\n",
        "    cursor.execute(\n",
        "        f'INSERT INTO experiment_runs ({names}) VALUES ({marks})',\n",
        "        list(insert_values.values())\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    conn.commit()\n",
        "    print(\"\\nSaved experiment record.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nEXPERIMENT METRICS\")\n",
        "for name, value in metrics.items():\n",

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, timezone\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import sklearn\n",
        "from sklearn.datasets import load_digits\n",
        "from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split\n",
        "from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import (\n",
        "    accuracy_score,\n",
        "    precision_score,\n",
        "    recall_score,\n",
        "    f1_score\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Find the existing database\n",
        "DB_PATH = next(\n",
        "    (p for p in [\"/content/research.db\", \"research.db\"]\n",
        "     if os.path.exists(p)),\n",
        "    None\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if DB_PATH is None:\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db not found. Do not create a replacement database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# Train the model\n",
        "digits = load_digits()\n",
        "X, y = digits.data, digits.target\n",
        "\n",
        "SEED = 42\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(\n",
        "    X, y,\n",
        "    test_size=0.2,\n",
        "    random_state=SEED,\n",
        "    stratify=y\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "model = MLPClassifier(\n",
        "    hidden_layer_sizes=(64,),\n",
        "    max_iter=300,\n",
        "    learning_rate_init=0.001,\n",
        "    random_state=SEED,\n",
        "    early_stopping=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "model.fit(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "predictions = model.predict(X_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "metrics = {\n",
        "    \"accuracy\": float(accuracy_score(y_test, predictions)),\n",
        "    \"precision_macro\": float(\n",
        "        precision_score(\n",
        "            y_test, predictions,\n",
        "            average=\"macro\", zero_division=0\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    \"recall_macro\": float(\n",
        "        recall_score(\n",
        "            y_test, predictions,\n",
        "            average=\"macro\", zero_division=0\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    \"f1_macro\": float(\n",
        "        f1_score(\n",
        "            y_test, predictions,\n",
        "            average=\"macro\", zero_division=0\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "parameters = {\n",
        "    \"dataset\": \"scikit-learn digits\",\n",
        "    \"model\": \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "    \"hidden_layer_sizes\": [64],\n",
        "    \"max_iter\": 300,\n",
        "    \"learning_rate_init\": 0.001,\n",
        "    \"test_size\": 0.2,\n",
        "    \"random_seed\": SEED,\n",
        "    \"scikit_learn_version\": sklearn.__version__,\n",
        "    \"numpy_version\": np.__version__\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# Open the existing database and inspect its schema\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=60)\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 60000\")\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    cursor = conn.cursor()\n",
        "    cursor.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\")\n",
        "    schema = cursor.fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not schema:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"experiment_runs table was not found.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    columns = {col[\"name\"] for col in schema}\n",
        "\n",
        "    required_columns = {\n",
        "        \"experiment_name\",\n",
        "        \"model_name\",\n",
        "        \"dataset_name\",\n",
        "        \"metric_name\",\n",
        "        \"metric_value\",\n",
        "        \"parameters\",\n",
        "        \"notes\",\n",
        "        \"run_date\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    missing = required_columns - columns\n",
        "    if missing:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            \"Unexpected schema. Missing columns: \"\n",
        "            + \", \".join(sorted(missing))\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_date = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n",
        "    saved_ids = []\n",
        "\n",

        "                    metric_value,\n",

        "                    notes,\n",
        "                    run_date\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n",
        "            \"\"\", (\n",
        "                \"Digits classification baseline\",\n",
        "                \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "                \"scikit-learn digits\",\n",
        "                metric_name,\n",
        "                metric_value,\n",
        "                json.dumps(parameters, sort_keys=True),\n",
        "                \"Held-out test-set metric; random seed 42.\",\n",
        "                run_date\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            saved_ids.append(cursor.lastrowid)\n",

        "    print(\"Database:\", DB_PATH)\n",

        "    conn.close()"

      "source": [
        "# STEP 1: Safely release old SQLite connections and check the database.\n",
        "# This cell does NOT delete data, tables, or the database.\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import glob\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import time\n",
        "\n",
        "# Find the existing database\n",
        "search_locations = [\n",
        "    \"/content/research.db\",\n",
        "    \"research.db\",\n",
        "    \"/content/drive/MyDrive/research.db\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "db_path = next(\n",
        "    (p for p in search_locations if os.path.isfile(p)),\n",
        "    None\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if db_path is None:\n",
        "    matches = glob.glob(\"/content/**/research.db\", recursive=True)\n",
        "    if matches:\n",
        "        db_path = matches[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "if db_path is None:\n",
        "    print(\"I could not find research.db in the usual Colab locations.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Do not create a new database. Check the Files panel in Colab for research.db.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"Database found:\", os.path.abspath(db_path))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Close old direct SQLite connections from earlier notebook cells.\n",
        "    closed = 0\n",
        "    for name, obj in list(globals().items()):\n",
        "        if isinstance(obj, sqlite3.Connection):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                obj.close()\n",
        "                closed += 1\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Closed old SQLite connections:\", closed)\n",
        "    time.sleep(2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Test whether the database can accept a write lock.\n",
        "    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=60)\n",
        "    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 60000\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE\")\n",
        "        conn.rollback()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"SUCCESS: The database is accessible for writing.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Inspect existing table; do not modify its contents.\n",
        "        columns = conn.execute(\n",
        "            \"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\"\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\nExisting experiment_runs columns:\")\n",
        "        for col in columns:\n",
        "            print(f\"{col[1]} | {col[2]} | NOT NULL={bool(col[3])}\")\n",

        "# STEP 3: Test database writing and inspect saved experiment runs safely\n",
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import time\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    print(\"ERROR: Database not found. Stop here; do not create a replacement.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    conn = None\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=60)\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 60000\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Attempt to obtain a write lock without changing any records.\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE\")\n",
        "        conn.rollback()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"SUCCESS: SQLite write access is available.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        columns = conn.execute(\n",
        "            \"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\"\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\nEXPERIMENT_RUNS SCHEMA\")\n",
        "        for col in columns:\n",
        "            print(\n",
        "                f\"{col[1]} | type={col[2]} | \"\n",
        "                f\"not_null={bool(col[3])} | primary_key={bool(col[5])}\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        count = conn.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM experiment_runs\"\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "        print(\"\\nSaved experiment runs:\", count)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if count:\n",
        "            rows = conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT * FROM experiment_runs ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 10\"\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "            names = [col[1] for col in columns]\n",
        "            print(\"\\nMost recent records:\")\n",
        "            for row in rows:\n",
        "                print(dict(zip(names, row)))\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\nNEXT: Database is ready for the experiment-saving step.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:\n",
        "        print(\"DATABASE STILL LOCKED:\", str(e))\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            \"\\nIn Colab, select Runtime > Interrupt execution. \"\n",
        "            \"Wait for cells to stop, then rerun this cell.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        if conn is not None:\n",
        "            conn.close()"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",
        "# STEP 4: Experiment Comparison and Performance Report\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from sklearn.datasets import load_digits\n",
        "from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split\n",
        "from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import (\n",
        "    accuracy_score,\n",
        "    precision_score,\n",
        "    recall_score,\n",
        "    f1_score\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db was not found. Stop rather than creating a new database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Load a real sample dataset\n",
        "digits = load_digits()\n",
        "X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(\n",
        "    digits.data,\n",
        "    digits.target,\n",
        "    test_size=0.2,\n",
        "    random_state=42,\n",
        "    stratify=digits.target\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. Compare three model configurations\n",
        "configurations = [\n",
        "    {\"name\": \"MLP_LR_0.001\", \"learning_rate\": 0.001, \"hidden_layers\": (64,)},\n",
        "    {\"name\": \"MLP_LR_0.01\",  \"learning_rate\": 0.01,  \"hidden_layers\": (64,)},\n",
        "    {\"name\": \"MLP_LR_0.0005\", \"learning_rate\": 0.0005, \"hidden_layers\": (64,)}\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for config in configurations:\n",
        "    model = MLPClassifier(\n",
        "        hidden_layer_sizes=config[\"hidden_layers\"],\n",
        "        learning_rate_init=config[\"learning_rate\"],\n",
        "        max_iter=300,\n",
        "        random_state=42,\n",
        "        early_stopping=True\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    model.fit(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "    predictions = model.predict(X_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "    results.append({\n",
        "        \"experiment_name\": config[\"name\"],\n",
        "        \"model_name\": \"MLPClassifier\",\n",
        "        \"dataset_name\": \"sklearn_digits\",\n",
        "        \"learning_rate\": config[\"learning_rate\"],\n",
        "        \"accuracy\": float(accuracy_score(y_test, predictions)),\n",
        "        \"precision_macro\": float(\n",
        "            precision_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"recall_macro\": float(\n",
        "            recall_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"f1_macro\": float(\n",
        "            f1_score(y_test, predictions, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "# 3. Rank by accuracy, then F1\n",
        "results.sort(\n",
        "    key=lambda r: (r[\"accuracy\"], r[\"f1_macro\"]),\n",
        "    reverse=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 68)\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — EXPERIMENT COMPARISON\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 68)\n",
        "print(\"Dataset: sklearn digits\")\n",
        "print(\"Training samples:\", len(X_train))\n",
        "print(\"Test samples:\", len(X_test))\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "for rank, result in enumerate(results, start=1):\n",
        "    print(f\"RANK {rank}: {result['experiment_name']}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Learning rate: {result['learning_rate']}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Accuracy:      {result['accuracy']:.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Macro precision: {result['precision_macro']:.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Macro recall:    {result['recall_macro']:.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Macro F1:        {result['f1_macro']:.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(\"-\" * 68)\n",
        "\n",
        "best = results[0]\n",
        "print(\"\\nBEST CONFIGURATION:\", best[\"experiment_name\"])\n",
        "print(\"Best test accuracy:\", f\"{best['accuracy']:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# 4. Save a report file without touching the database\n",
        "report_path = \"/content/experiment_comparison_report.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "report = {\n",
        "    \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "    \"dataset\": \"sklearn_digits\",\n",
        "    \"split\": {\"test_size\": 0.2, \"random_state\": 42},\n",
        "    \"ranking_method\": \"accuracy descending, then macro F1 descending\",\n",
        "    \"results\": results,\n",
        "    \"best_experiment\": best[\"experiment_name\"]\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(report_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "    json.dump(report, file, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nReport saved to:\", report_path)\n",
        "print(\"This step did not modify research.db.\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {

            "application/javascript": [
              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },
        {
          "output_type": "display_data",
          "data": {
            "text/plain": [
              "<IPython.core.display.Javascript object>"
            ],
            "application/javascript": [

            "text/plain": [
              "<IPython.core.display.Javascript object>"
            ],
            "application/javascript": [
              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },

          "output_type": "display_data",

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",
        "# STEP 7: Automated Research Quality Audit\n",
        "# READ-ONLY: does not modify research.db\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "OUTPUT_PATH = \"/content/research_quality_audit.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db not found. Do not create a replacement database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "    uri=True,\n",
        "    timeout=30\n",
        ")\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "def table_exists(table_name):\n",
        "    row = conn.execute(\n",
        "        \"SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master \"\n",
        "        \"WHERE type='table' AND name=?\",\n",
        "        (table_name,)\n",
        "    ).fetchone()\n",
        "    return row is not None\n",
        "\n",
        "def columns_of(table_name):\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        row[\"name\"]\n",
        "        for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "            f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table_name}\")'\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "def count_rows(table_name):\n",
        "    return conn.execute(\n",
        "        f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table_name}\"'\n",
        "    ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "audit = {\n",
        "    \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "    \"database\": DB_PATH,\n",
        "    \"read_only\": True,\n",
        "    \"tables\": {},\n",
        "    \"checks\": [],\n",
        "    \"warnings\": [],\n",
        "    \"recommendations\": []\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "expected_tables = [\n",
        "    \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "    \"evidence_reviews\",\n",
        "    \"experiment_runs\",\n",
        "    \"experiment_summaries\",\n",
        "    \"experiments\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "    \"multi_pdf_passages\",\n",
        "    \"paper_library\",\n",
        "    \"question_paper_links\",\n",
        "    \"research_bibliography\",\n",
        "    \"research_citations\",\n",
        "    \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "    \"research_papers\",\n",
        "    \"research_questions\",\n",
        "    \"workspace_notes\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Database inventory\n",
        "existing_tables = {\n",
        "    row[\"name\"]\n",
        "    for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "        \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "missing_tables = [\n",
        "    table for table in expected_tables\n",
        "    if table not in existing_tables\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "audit[\"tables\"][\"expected\"] = len(expected_tables)\n",
        "audit[\"tables\"][\"found\"] = len(existing_tables)\n",
        "audit[\"tables\"][\"missing\"] = missing_tables\n",
        "audit[\"checks\"].append({\n",
        "    \"name\": \"Expected table inventory\",\n",
        "    \"status\": \"PASS\" if not missing_tables else \"WARNING\",\n",
        "    \"details\": (\n",
        "        \"All expected tables exist.\"\n",
        "        if not missing_tables\n",
        "        else \"Some expected tables are missing.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "})\n",
        "\n",
        "for table in sorted(existing_tables):\n",
        "    if table == \"sqlite_sequence\":\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "    audit[\"tables\"][table] = count_rows(table)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. SQLite integrity check\n",
        "integrity = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "audit[\"checks\"].append({\n",
        "    \"name\": \"SQLite integrity\",\n",
        "    \"status\": \"PASS\" if integrity == \"ok\" else \"FAIL\",\n",
        "    \"details\": integrity\n",
        "})\n",
        "\n",
        "# 3. Research paper metadata checks\n",
        "for table in [\"discovered_papers\", \"research_papers\", \"paper_library\"]:\n",
        "    if table not in existing_tables:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    cols = columns_of(table)\n",
        "    possible_title_cols = [\n",
        "        c for c in [\"title\", \"paper_title\", \"name\"] if c in cols\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    possible_doi_cols = [\n",
        "        c for c in [\"doi\", \"DOI\"] if c in cols\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if possible_title_cols:\n",
        "        title_col = possible_title_cols[0]\n",
        "        missing_titles = conn.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table}\" '\n",
        "            f'WHERE \"{title_col}\" IS NULL OR TRIM(\"{title_col}\") = \"\"'\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        audit[\"checks\"].append({\n",
        "            \"name\": f\"{table}: missing titles\",\n",
        "            \"status\": \"PASS\" if missing_titles == 0 else \"WARNING\",\n",
        "            \"details\": f\"Records with missing titles: {missing_titles}\"\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    if possible_doi_cols:\n",
        "        doi_col = possible_doi_cols[0]\n",
        "        missing_dois = conn.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table}\" '\n",
        "            f'WHERE \"{doi_col}\" IS NULL OR TRIM(\"{doi_col}\") = \"\"'\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        audit[\"checks\"].append({\n",
        "            \"name\": f\"{table}: missing DOI\",\n",
        "            \"status\": \"INFO\" if missing_dois else \"PASS\",\n",
        "            \"details\": (\n",
        "                f\"Records without DOI: {missing_dois}. \"\n",
        "                \"Some legitimate papers do not have a DOI.\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "# 4. Citation completeness\n",
        "if \"research_citations\" in existing_tables:\n",
        "    cols = columns_of(\"research_citations\")\n",
        "    citation_fields = [\n",
        "        c for c in [\n",
        "            \"claim\", \"paper_name\", \"quoted_evidence\",\n",
        "            \"page_number\", \"citation_note\"\n",
        "        ] if c in cols\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if citation_fields:\n",
        "        conditions = [\n",
        "            f'(\"{c}\" IS NULL OR TRIM(CAST(\"{c}\" AS TEXT)) = \"\")'\n",
        "            for c in citation_fields\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "        sql = (\n",
        "            'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"research_citations\" WHERE '\n",
        "            + \" OR \".join(conditions)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        incomplete = conn.execute(sql).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        audit[\"checks\"].append({\n",
        "            \"name\": \"Citation completeness\",\n",
        "            \"status\": \"PASS\" if incomplete == 0 else \"WARNING\",\n",
        "            \"details\": f\"Citations with at least one missing checked field: {incomplete}\"\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "# 5. Knowledge graph checks\n",
        "if \"knowledge_edges\" in existing_tables:\n",
        "    cols = columns_of(\"knowledge_edges\")\n",
        "    source_col = next(\n",
        "        (c for c in [\"source_id\", \"source_node_id\", \"from_node_id\"]\n",
        "         if c in cols), None\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    target_col = next(\n",
        "        (c for c in [\"target_id\", \"target_node_id\", \"to_node_id\"]\n",
        "         if c in cols), None\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if source_col and target_col and \"knowledge_nodes\" in existing_tables:\n",
        "        node_cols = columns_of(\"knowledge_nodes\")\n",
        "        node_id = \"id\" if \"id\" in node_cols else None\n",
        "\n",
        "        if node_id:\n",
        "            bad_sources = conn.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM knowledge_edges e '\n",
        "                f'WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM knowledge_nodes n '\n",
        "                f'WHERE n.\"{node_id}\" = e.\"{source_col}\")'\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "            bad_targets = conn.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM knowledge_edges e '\n",
        "                f'WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM knowledge_nodes n '\n",
        "                f'WHERE n.\"{node_id}\" = e.\"{target_col}\")'\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "            audit[\"checks\"].append({\n",
        "                \"name\": \"Knowledge graph references\",\n",
        "                \"status\": (\n",
        "                    \"PASS\" if bad_sources == 0 and bad_targets == 0\n",
        "                    else \"WARNING\"\n",
        "                ),\n",
        "                \"details\": (\n",
        "                    f\"Edges with missing source nodes: {bad_sources}; \"\n",
        "                    f\"missing target nodes: {bad_targets}\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "# 6. Experiment record checks\n",
        "if \"experiment_runs\" in existing_tables:\n",
        "    cols = columns_of(\"experiment_runs\")\n",
        "    checks = [\n",
        "        c for c in [\n",
        "            \"experiment_name\", \"model_name\", \"dataset_name\",\n",
        "            \"metric_name\", \"metric_value\", \"notes\"\n",
        "        ] if c in cols\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if checks:\n",
        "        conditions = [\n",
        "            f'(\"{c}\" IS NULL OR TRIM(CAST(\"{c}\" AS TEXT)) = \"\")'\n",
        "            for c in checks if c != \"metric_value\"\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "        if \"metric_value\" in checks:\n",
        "            conditions.append('\"metric_value\" IS NULL')\n",
        "\n",
        "        if conditions:\n",
        "            incomplete = conn.execute(\n",
        "                'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM experiment_runs WHERE '\n",
        "                + \" OR \".join(conditions)\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "            audit[\"checks\"].append({\n",
        "                \"name\": \"Experiment run completeness\",\n",
        "                \"status\": \"PASS\" if incomplete == 0 else \"WARNING\",\n",
        "                \"details\": f\"Runs with missing checked values: {incomplete}\"\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "# 7. Warnings and recommendations\n",
        "for check in audit[\"checks\"]:\n",
        "    if check[\"status\"] in [\"FAIL\", \"WARNING\"]:\n",
        "        audit[\"warnings\"].append(check)\n",
        "\n",
        "if missing_tables:\n",
        "    audit[\"recommendations\"].append(\n",
        "        \"Review the missing table names before adding more features.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"experiment_runs\" in existing_tables:\n",
        "    run_count = count_rows(\"experiment_runs\")\n",
        "    if run_count == 0:\n",
        "        audit[\"recommendations\"].append(\n",
        "            \"Save at least one verified experiment run before relying on \"\n",
        "            \"the experiment tracking feature.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"research_citations\" in existing_tables:\n",
        "    if count_rows(\"research_citations\") == 0:\n",
        "        audit[\"recommendations\"].append(\n",
        "            \"Add a real source-backed citation with page and evidence details.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "if not audit[\"recommendations\"]:\n",
        "    audit[\"recommendations\"].append(\n",
        "        \"Review warnings manually, verify a database backup, and run an \"\n",
        "        \"end-to-end project test.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "# 8. Save audit output as a separate file\n",
        "with open(OUTPUT_PATH, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(audit, f, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 9. Display results\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 65)\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — QUALITY AUDIT\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 65)\n",
        "print(\"Database:\", DB_PATH)\n",
        "print(\"Tables found:\", audit[\"tables\"][\"found\"])\n",
        "print(\"Missing expected tables:\", missing_tables or \"None\")\n",
        "print(\"SQLite integrity:\", integrity)\n",
        "print(\"\\nTABLE RECORD COUNTS\")\n",
        "\n",
        "for name, value in audit[\"tables\"].items():\n",
        "    if isinstance(value, int):\n",
        "        print(f\"  {name}: {value}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nCHECK RESULTS\")\n",
        "for check in audit[\"checks\"]:\n",
        "    print(f\"[{check['status']}] {check['name']}: {check['details']}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nRECOMMENDATIONS\")\n",
        "for item in audit[\"recommendations\"]:\n",
        "    print(\"-\", item)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nAudit report saved to:\", OUTPUT_PATH)\n",
        "print(\"Database was opened read-only; no records were changed.\")"
      ],
