    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",
        "# STEP 9: Unified Research Command Center\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "REPORT_PATH = \"/content/research_command_center.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db not found. Do not create a replacement database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "    uri=True,\n",
        "    timeout=30\n",
        ")\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_tables():\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        row[\"name\"]\n",
        "        for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "tables = get_tables()\n",
        "\n",
        "def safe_count(table):\n",
        "    if table not in tables:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    return conn.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table}\"').fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "def table_columns(table):\n",
        "    if table not in tables:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        row[\"name\"]\n",
        "        for row in conn.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")')\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build the dashboard from existing tables only.\n",
        "sections = {\n",
        "    \"Research questions\": \"research_questions\",\n",
        "    \"Discovered papers\": \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "    \"Saved research papers\": \"research_papers\",\n",
        "    \"Paper library\": \"paper_library\",\n",
        "    \"Research evidence\": \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "    \"Multi-PDF passages\": \"multi_pdf_passages\",\n",
        "    \"Citations\": \"research_citations\",\n",
        "    \"Bibliography entries\": \"research_bibliography\",\n",
        "    \"Knowledge graph nodes\": \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "    \"Knowledge graph edges\": \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "    \"Experiments\": \"experiments\",\n",
        "    \"Experiment runs\": \"experiment_runs\",\n",
        "    \"Experiment summaries\": \"experiment_summaries\",\n",
        "    \"Evidence reviews\": \"evidence_reviews\",\n",
        "    \"Question-paper links\": \"question_paper_links\",\n",
        "    \"Workspace notes\": \"workspace_notes\"\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "counts = {}\n",
        "for label, table in sections.items():\n",
        "    count = safe_count(table)\n",
        "    if count is not None:\n",
        "        counts[label] = count\n",
        "\n",
        "integrity = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Read experiment results when compatible columns are present.\n",
        "experiment_preview = []\n",
        "if \"experiment_runs\" in tables:\n",
        "    cols = table_columns(\"experiment_runs\")\n",
        "    preferred = [\n",
        "        c for c in [\n",
        "            \"id\", \"experiment_name\", \"model_name\",\n",
        "            \"dataset_name\", \"metric_name\", \"metric_value\",\n",
        "            \"run_date\"\n",
        "        ] if c in cols\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if preferred:\n",
        "        sql = (\n",
        "            \"SELECT \" + \", \".join(f'\"{c}\"' for c in preferred)\n",
        "            + ' FROM \"experiment_runs\" LIMIT 10'\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        experiment_preview = [\n",
        "            dict(row) for row in conn.execute(sql).fetchall()\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Read research questions using their actual schema.\n",
        "question_preview = []\n",
        "if \"research_questions\" in tables:\n",
        "    cols = table_columns(\"research_questions\")\n",
        "    title_col = next(\n",
        "        (c for c in [\"question\", \"research_question\", \"title\", \"text\"]\n",
        "         if c in cols),\n",
        "        None\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if title_col:\n",
        "        question_preview = [\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT \"{title_col}\" FROM research_questions LIMIT 10'\n",
        "            ).fetchall()\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Status summaries\n",
        "total_records = sum(counts.values())\n",
        "empty_sections = [label for label, count in counts.items() if count == 0]\n",
        "\n",
        "dashboard = {\n",
        "    \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "    \"database\": DB_PATH,\n",
        "    \"database_integrity\": integrity,\n",
        "    \"total_records_across_listed_tables\": total_records,\n",
        "    \"counts\": counts,\n",
        "    \"empty_sections\": empty_sections,\n",
        "    \"research_question_preview\": question_preview,\n",
        "    \"experiment_run_preview\": experiment_preview,\n",
        "    \"notes\": [\n",
        "        \"This dashboard reads the existing database without changing it.\",\n",
        "        \"Counts across tables are not unique entities; related records can be counted more than once.\",\n",
        "        \"A populated table does not by itself prove that its data is accurate.\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(REPORT_PATH, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(dashboard, f, indent=2, default=str)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 65)\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — COMMAND CENTER\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 65)\n",
        "print(\"Generated:\", dashboard[\"generated_at\"])\n",
        "print(\"SQLite integrity:\", integrity)\n",
        "print(\"Total records across listed tables:\", total_records)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nPROJECT MODULES\")\n",
        "for label, count in counts.items():\n",
        "    print(f\"{label:<28} {count:>6}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if question_preview:\n",
        "    print(\"\\nSAVED RESEARCH QUESTIONS\")\n",
        "    for i, question in enumerate(question_preview, 1):\n",
        "        print(f\"{i}. {question}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if experiment_preview:\n",
        "    print(\"\\nRECENT EXPERIMENT RUNS\")\n",
        "    for record in experiment_preview:\n",
        "        print(record)\n",
        "\n",
        "if empty_sections:\n",
        "    print(\"\\nEMPTY MODULES TO REVIEW\")\n",
        "    for section in empty_sections:\n",
        "        print(\"-\", section)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nDashboard report saved:\", REPORT_PATH)\n",
        "print(\"No database records were modified.\")"
      ],

          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/",

        },
        "id": "3UMEkh2-dqES",
        "outputId": "e6fe61e1-374f-430b-df2f-4c17a6605ebc"

          "data": {
            "text/plain": [
              "<IPython.core.display.Javascript object>"
            ],
            "application/javascript": [
              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },
        {
          "output_type": "display_data",
          "data": {
            "text/plain": [

            ],

              "download(\"download_d6c54384-d75a-4f25-87ca-c491b6710a04\", \"research_command_center.json\", 1175)"
            ]

        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",

        "\n",

        "\n",
        "readme = r\"\"\"# AI Research Scientist\n",
        "\n",
        "A Python-based research assistant project for organizing research questions,\n",
        "discovering scientific papers, searching evidence, tracking citations,\n",
        "connecting concepts, and comparing machine-learning experiments.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Project goals\n",
        "\n",
        "- Organize research questions and scientific literature.\n",
        "- Store evidence passages and citation details.\n",
        "- Explore relationships between research concepts.\n",
        "- Track experiments and evaluate model performance.\n",
        "- Generate reports that help researchers review their work.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Features\n",
        "\n",
        "### Research management\n",
        "- Research-question storage and planning.\n",
        "- Paper discovery and metadata organization.\n",
        "- Local paper library and passage search.\n",
        "- Multi-paper evidence retrieval.\n",
        "\n",
        "### Evidence and citations\n",
        "- Evidence passage storage.\n",
        "- Citation and bibliography tracking.\n",
        "- Evidence-quality review helpers.\n",
        "- Links between research questions and papers.\n",
        "\n",
        "### Knowledge graph\n",
        "- Knowledge-node and relationship storage.\n",
        "- Concept search.\n",
        "- Connections between research concepts.\n",
        "\n",
        "### Experiments and analysis\n",
        "- Experiment tracking.\n",
        "- Model comparison on the scikit-learn digits dataset.\n",
        "- Accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score reporting.\n",
        "- Experiment comparison reports.\n",
        "\n",
        "### Reports and audits\n",
        "- Research quality audit.\n",
        "- Database inventory and integrity checks.\n",
        "- Command-center dashboard.\n",
        "- JSON and Markdown report generation.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Technology\n",
        "\n",
        "- Python\n",
        "- SQLite\n",
        "- scikit-learn\n",
        "- NumPy\n",
        "- pypdf (for PDF text extraction)\n",
        "- Google Colab\n",
        "\n",
        "## Database\n",
        "\n",
        "The project uses a SQLite database named `research.db`.\n",
        "\n",
        "**Important:** Keep a separate backup of this file. Do not delete or recreate\n",
        "the database to fix a lock or coding error.\n",
        "\n",
        "The database contains tables for research questions, papers, evidence,\n",
        "citations, knowledge-graph relationships, experiments, and workspace notes.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Running in Google Colab\n",
        "\n",
        "1. Open your Google Colab notebook.\n",
        "2. Upload or locate your existing `research.db`.\n",
        "3. Install any missing Python libraries required by the cell you want to run.\n",
        "4. Run the relevant project cells in order.\n",
        "5. Review the printed output and generated reports.\n",
        "6. Download important reports and back up the database.\n",
        "\n",
        "Example dependency installation cell:\n",
        "\n",
        "```python\n",
        "%pip install scikit-learn numpy pypdf\n",
        "```\n",
        "\n",
        "Only install the packages required by the modules you use. Some may already\n",
        "be installed in Colab.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Experiment evaluation\n",
        "\n",
        "The demonstration compares multilayer perceptron configurations using\n",
        "the scikit-learn digits dataset. It records metrics such as:\n",
        "\n",
        "- Accuracy\n",
        "- Macro precision\n",
        "- Macro recall\n",
        "- Macro F1-score\n",
        "\n",
        "The results depend on the dataset split, model settings, and random seed.\n",
        "A single run does not establish statistical significance.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Data integrity and safety\n",
        "\n",
        "- Keep a verified backup before major changes.\n",
        "- Prefer read-only database inspection for audits.\n",
        "- Avoid deleting SQLite journal, WAL, or shared-memory files manually.\n",
        "- Check the database schema before inserting records.\n",
        "- Treat paper metadata and extracted text as information that needs verification.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Current limitations\n",
        "\n",
        "- Keyword-based evidence retrieval is not the same as deep semantic reasoning.\n",
        "- Automatically extracted concepts and quality scores may be imperfect.\n",
        "- Discovered paper metadata should be checked against the original publication.\n",
        "- The project does not independently establish scientific truth.\n",
        "- Research conclusions require source verification and human judgment.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Future improvements\n",
        "\n",
        "- Semantic search using local embedding models.\n",
        "- More robust PDF parsing and section detection.\n",
        "- Repeated experiments with confidence intervals.\n",
        "- Automated tests for every database operation.\n",
        "- A web interface for research workflows.\n",
        "- Stronger provenance checks for claims and citations.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Reproducibility\n",
        "\n",
        "Record dataset, preprocessing, model configuration, random seeds, metrics,\n",
        "and software versions for every important experiment.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Author\n",
        "\n",
        "Add your name and contact details before publishing this project.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Disclaimer\n",
        "\n",
        "This is an educational research-assistant project. Its output should not be\n",

              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",

              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",
        "# STEP 12: End-to-End Project Test\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "REPORT_PATH = \"/content/AI_Research_Scientist_Test_Report.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "def record_test(name, passed, details):\n",
        "    results.append({\n",
        "        \"test\": name,\n",
        "        \"status\": \"PASS\" if passed else \"FAIL\",\n",
        "        \"details\": str(details)\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    record_test(\"Database exists\", False, f\"Not found: {DB_PATH}\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    record_test(\n",
        "        \"Database exists\",\n",
        "        True,\n",
        "        f\"{DB_PATH} ({os.path.getsize(DB_PATH)} bytes)\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    conn = None\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        # Open the existing database in read-only mode.\n",
        "        conn = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "            f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "            uri=True,\n",
        "            timeout=30\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "        record_test(\"Database opens read-only\", True, \"Connection successful\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Integrity test\n",
        "        integrity = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "        record_test(\n",
        "            \"SQLite integrity\",\n",
        "            integrity == \"ok\",\n",
        "            integrity\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Required tables\n",
        "        required_tables = [\n",
        "            \"research_questions\",\n",
        "            \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "            \"research_papers\",\n",
        "            \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "            \"research_citations\",\n",
        "            \"research_bibliography\",\n",
        "            \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "            \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "            \"experiments\",\n",
        "            \"experiment_runs\",\n",
        "            \"experiment_summaries\",\n",
        "            \"workspace_notes\"\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        existing_tables = {\n",
        "            row[\"name\"]\n",
        "            for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        for table in required_tables:\n",
        "            record_test(\n",
        "                f\"Table: {table}\",\n",
        "                table in existing_tables,\n",
        "                \"Found\" if table in existing_tables else \"Missing\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Row counts for each available project table\n",
        "        table_counts = {}\n",
        "        for table in sorted(existing_tables):\n",
        "            if table == \"sqlite_sequence\":\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            count = conn.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table}\"'\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "            table_counts[table] = count\n",
        "\n",
        "        record_test(\n",
        "            \"Research questions table query\",\n",
        "            \"research_questions\" in existing_tables,\n",
        "            f\"Rows: {table_counts.get('research_questions', 0)}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        record_test(\n",
        "            \"Knowledge graph query\",\n",
        "            \"knowledge_nodes\" in existing_tables\n",
        "            and \"knowledge_edges\" in existing_tables,\n",
        "            (\n",
        "                f\"Nodes: {table_counts.get('knowledge_nodes', 0)}, \"\n",
        "                f\"edges: {table_counts.get('knowledge_edges', 0)}\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        record_test(\n",
        "            \"Experiment tracking table query\",\n",
        "            \"experiment_runs\" in existing_tables,\n",
        "            f\"Rows: {table_counts.get('experiment_runs', 0)}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Check schema needed for experiment records\n",
        "        if \"experiment_runs\" in existing_tables:\n",
        "            columns = {\n",
        "                row[\"name\"]\n",
        "                for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                    \"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            }\n",
        "            expected_columns = {\n",
        "                \"experiment_name\",\n",
        "                \"model_name\",\n",
        "                \"dataset_name\",\n",
        "                \"metric_name\",\n",
        "                \"metric_value\",\n",
        "                \"notes\"\n",
        "            }\n",
        "            missing_columns = sorted(expected_columns - columns)\n",
        "\n",
        "            record_test(\n",
        "                \"Experiment schema\",\n",
        "                not missing_columns,\n",
        "                \"All expected columns found\"\n",
        "                if not missing_columns\n",
        "                else f\"Missing columns: {missing_columns}\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Verify any existing experiment metric values are numeric or NULL.\n",
        "        if (\n",
        "            \"experiment_runs\" in existing_tables\n",
        "            and \"metric_value\" in {\n",
        "                row[\"name\"]\n",
        "                for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                    \"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            }\n",
        "        ):\n",
        "            bad_values = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT COUNT(*)\n",
        "                FROM experiment_runs\n",
        "                WHERE metric_value IS NOT NULL\n",
        "                  AND typeof(metric_value) NOT IN ('integer', 'real')\n",
        "            \"\"\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "            record_test(\n",
        "                \"Experiment metric data types\",\n",
        "                bad_values == 0,\n",
        "                f\"Non-numeric metric values: {bad_values}\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        conn.close()\n",
        "        conn = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as error:\n",
        "        record_test(\n",
        "            \"Database inspection\",\n",
        "            False,\n",
        "            f\"{type(error).__name__}: {error}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        if conn is not None:\n",
        "            conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Summarize test results\n",
        "passed = sum(r[\"status\"] == \"PASS\" for r in results)\n",
        "failed = sum(r[\"status\"] == \"FAIL\" for r in results)\n",
        "\n",
        "report = {\n",
        "    \"project\": \"AI Research Scientist\",\n",
        "    \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "    \"database_path\": DB_PATH,\n",
        "    \"mode\": \"read-only\",\n",
        "    \"passed\": passed,\n",
        "    \"failed\": failed,\n",
        "    \"total_tests\": len(results),\n",
        "    \"overall_status\": \"PASS\" if failed == 0 else \"REVIEW REQUIRED\",\n",
        "    \"table_counts\": table_counts if os.path.isfile(DB_PATH) else {},\n",
        "    \"tests\": results\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(REPORT_PATH, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — END-TO-END TEST\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "\n",
        "for item in results:\n",
        "    print(f\"[{item['status']}] {item['test']}: {item['details']}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nPassed:\", passed)\n",
        "print(\"Failed:\", failed)\n",
        "print(\"Overall status:\", report[\"overall_status\"])\n",
        "print(\"Report saved:\", REPORT_PATH)\n",
        "print(\"\\nNo database records were modified.\")"
      ],

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",
        "# STEP 14: Unified Project Launcher\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "OUTPUT_PATH = \"/content/AI_Research_Scientist_Project_Status.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_header():\n",
        "    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n",
        "    print(\"       AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — PROJECT LAUNCHER\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "\n",
        "def connect_readonly():\n",
        "    if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "        print(\"Database not found:\", DB_PATH)\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    return sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "        f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "        uri=True,\n",
        "        timeout=30\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "def database_summary():\n",
        "    conn = connect_readonly()\n",
        "    if conn is None:\n",
        "        return {\"database_found\": False}\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        integrity = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "        tables = [\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master \"\n",
        "                \"WHERE type='table' AND name != 'sqlite_sequence' \"\n",
        "                \"ORDER BY name\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        counts = {}\n",
        "        for table in tables:\n",
        "            counts[table] = conn.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{table}\"'\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\nDatabase:\", DB_PATH)\n",
        "        print(\"Integrity:\", integrity)\n",
        "        print(\"Tables:\", len(tables))\n",
        "        print(\"\\nRecord counts:\")\n",
        "        for table, count in counts.items():\n",
        "            print(f\"  {table:<28} {count:>6}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"database_found\": True,\n",
        "            \"integrity\": integrity,\n",
        "            \"tables\": len(tables),\n",
        "            \"record_counts\": counts\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "def experiment_summary():\n",
        "    conn = connect_readonly()\n",
        "    if conn is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        exists = conn.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master \"\n",
        "            \"WHERE type='table' AND name='experiment_runs'\"\n",
        "        ).fetchone()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not exists:\n",
        "            print(\"\\nThe experiment_runs table is missing.\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        columns = {\n",
        "            row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        required = {\"experiment_name\", \"metric_name\", \"metric_value\"}\n",
        "        if not required.issubset(columns):\n",
        "            print(\"\\nExperiment table has a different schema.\")\n",
        "            print(\"Columns:\", sorted(columns))\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT experiment_name, metric_name, metric_value\n",
        "            FROM experiment_runs\n",
        "            ORDER BY experiment_name, metric_name\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"\\nSAVED EXPERIMENT METRICS\")\n",
        "        if not rows:\n",
        "            print(\"No experiment metrics saved yet.\")\n",
        "            print(\"The database write-lock issue still needs to be resolved.\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            for name, metric, value in rows:\n",
        "                print(f\"{name} | {metric}: {value}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "def project_files():\n",
        "    print(\"\\nPROJECT FILES\")\n",
        "    candidates = [\n",
        "        \"/content/AI_Research_Scientist\",\n",
        "        \"/content/experiment_comparison_report.json\",\n",
        "        \"/content/research_quality_audit.json\",\n",
        "        \"/content/research_command_center.json\",\n",
        "        \"/content/AI_Research_Scientist_Test_Report.json\",\n",
        "        \"/content/AI_Research_Scientist_Documentation.zip\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    found = []\n",
        "    for path in candidates:\n",
        "        if os.path.isfile(path):\n",
        "            size = os.path.getsize(path)\n",
        "            print(f\"[FILE] {path} ({size:,} bytes)\")\n",
        "            found.append(path)\n",
        "        elif os.path.isdir(path):\n",
        "            print(f\"[FOLDER] {path}\")\n",
        "            for name in sorted(os.listdir(path)):\n",
        "                full_path = os.path.join(path, name)\n",
        "                if os.path.isfile(full_path):\n",
        "                    print(\n",
        "                        f\"   - {name} \"\n",
        "                        f\"({os.path.getsize(full_path):,} bytes)\"\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                    found.append(full_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not found:\n",
        "        print(\"No optional report files found yet.\")\n",
        "    return found\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_status(summary, files_found):\n",
        "    report = {\n",
        "        \"project\": \"AI Research Scientist\",\n",
        "        \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"database\": summary,\n",
        "        \"files_found\": files_found,\n",
        "        \"note\": (\n",
        "            \"Status report only. Database was opened read-only. \"\n",
        "            \"No records were changed.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(OUTPUT_PATH, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nStatus report saved:\", OUTPUT_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "while True:\n",
        "    show_header()\n",
        "    print(\"1. View database status\")\n",
        "    print(\"2. View experiment metrics\")\n",
        "    print(\"3. Find project reports and documentation\")\n",
        "    print(\"4. Generate complete project status report\")\n",
        "    print(\"5. Exit\")\n",
        "    choice = input(\"\\nChoose 1-5: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if choice == \"1\":\n",
        "        database_summary()\n",
        "    elif choice == \"2\":\n",
        "        experiment_summary()\n",
        "    elif choice == \"3\":\n",
        "        project_files()\n",
        "    elif choice == \"4\":\n",
        "        summary = database_summary()\n",
        "        files_found = project_files()\n",
        "        save_status(summary, files_found)\n",
        "    elif choice == \"5\":\n",
        "        print(\"Project launcher closed.\")\n",
        "        break\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(\"Please enter a number from 1 to 5.\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "LRJeRSoceOUK",
        "outputId": "39cc633a-dc8f-4d9e-b759-2ff1727bebc9"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": [
        {
          "output_type": "stream",
          "name": "stdout",
          "text": [
            "\n",
            "============================================================\n",
            "       AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — PROJECT LAUNCHER\n",
            "============================================================\n",
            "1. View database status\n",
            "2. View experiment metrics\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",
        "# STEP 15: Experiment Visualizer\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "RESULTS_PATH = \"/content/experiment_comparison_report.json\"\n",
        "CHART_PATH = \"/content/AI_Research_Experiment_Comparison.png\"\n",
        "\n",
        "results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "# Option A: Use the model comparison report created in Step 4.\n",
        "if os.path.isfile(RESULTS_PATH):\n",
        "    with open(RESULTS_PATH, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "        report = json.load(f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for item in report.get(\"results\", []):\n",
        "        if \"experiment_name\" in item and \"accuracy\" in item:\n",
        "            results.append({\n",
        "                \"name\": item[\"experiment_name\"],\n",
        "                \"accuracy\": float(item[\"accuracy\"]),\n",
        "                \"precision\": float(item.get(\"precision_macro\", 0)),\n",
        "                \"recall\": float(item.get(\"recall_macro\", 0)),\n",
        "                \"f1\": float(item.get(\"f1_macro\", 0))\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "# Option B: If no report exists, read saved metrics from the database.\n",
        "if not results and os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    conn = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "        f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "        uri=True,\n",
        "        timeout=30\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        tables = {\n",
        "            row[0] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"experiment_runs\" in tables:\n",
        "            columns = {\n",
        "                row[1] for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "                    \"PRAGMA table_info(experiment_runs)\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            needed = {\n",
        "                \"experiment_name\", \"metric_name\", \"metric_value\"\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            if needed.issubset(columns):\n",
        "                rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                    SELECT experiment_name, metric_name, metric_value\n",
        "                    FROM experiment_runs\n",
        "                    WHERE metric_value IS NOT NULL\n",
        "                    ORDER BY experiment_name\n",
        "                \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "                grouped = {}\n",
        "                for experiment, metric, value in rows:\n",
        "                    metric_key = str(metric).lower()\n",
        "                    if metric_key in (\n",
        "                        \"accuracy\", \"precision\", \"precision_macro\",\n",
        "                        \"recall\", \"recall_macro\", \"f1\", \"f1_macro\"\n",
        "                    ):\n",
        "                        grouped.setdefault(experiment, {})[\n",
        "                            metric_key\n",
        "                        ] = float(value)\n",
        "\n",
        "                for name, metrics in grouped.items():\n",
        "                    accuracy = metrics.get(\"accuracy\")\n",
        "                    if accuracy is None:\n",
        "                        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                    results.append({\n",
        "                        \"name\": name,\n",
        "                        \"accuracy\": accuracy,\n",
        "                        \"precision\": metrics.get(\n",
        "                            \"precision_macro\",\n",
        "                            metrics.get(\"precision\", 0)\n",
        "                        ),\n",
        "                        \"recall\": metrics.get(\n",
        "                            \"recall_macro\",\n",
        "                            metrics.get(\"recall\", 0)\n",
        "                        ),\n",
        "                        \"f1\": metrics.get(\n",
        "                            \"f1_macro\",\n",
        "                            metrics.get(\"f1\", 0)\n",
        "                        )\n",
        "                    })\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "if not results:\n",
        "    print(\"No compatible experiment metrics were found.\")\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"Run Step 4 to generate experiment_comparison_report.json, \"\n",
        "        \"or save metrics into experiment_runs first.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    names = [r[\"name\"] for r in results]\n",
        "    metrics = {\n",
        "        \"Accuracy\": [r[\"accuracy\"] for r in results],\n",
        "        \"Macro Precision\": [r[\"precision\"] for r in results],\n",
        "        \"Macro Recall\": [r[\"recall\"] for r in results],\n",
        "        \"Macro F1\": [r[\"f1\"] for r in results]\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Chart 1: Accuracy by configuration\n",
        "    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))\n",
        "    plt.bar(names, metrics[\"Accuracy\"])\n",
        "    plt.title(\"Model Accuracy Comparison\")\n",
        "    plt.xlabel(\"Experiment\")\n",
        "    plt.ylabel(\"Accuracy\")\n",
        "    plt.ylim(0, 1.05)\n",
        "    plt.xticks(rotation=20, ha=\"right\")\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    plt.savefig(CHART_PATH, dpi=160)\n",
        "    plt.show()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Chart 2: Compare all available metrics\n",
        "    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))\n",
        "    for metric_name, values in metrics.items():\n",
        "        plt.plot(names, values, marker=\"o\", label=metric_name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.title(\"Experiment Metric Comparison\")\n",
        "    plt.xlabel(\"Experiment\")\n",
        "    plt.ylabel(\"Score\")\n",
        "    plt.ylim(0, 1.05)\n",
        "    plt.xticks(rotation=20, ha=\"right\")\n",
        "    plt.legend()\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    plt.show()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nEXPERIMENT RESULTS\")\n",
        "    for result in results:\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"{result['name']}: \"\n",
        "            f\"accuracy={result['accuracy']:.4f}, \"\n",
        "            f\"precision={result['precision']:.4f}, \"\n",
        "            f\"recall={result['recall']:.4f}, \"\n",
        "            f\"F1={result['f1']:.4f}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nChart saved to:\", CHART_PATH)"

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST\n",
        "# STEP 16: Evidence-Based Research Assistant\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "from collections import Counter\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "REPORT_PATH = \"/content/AI_Research_Evidence_Report.md\"\n",
        "JSON_PATH = \"/content/AI_Research_Evidence_Report.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db was not found. Do not create a replacement database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "def tokenize(text):\n",
        "    return set(re.findall(r\"[a-zA-Z0-9]+\", str(text).lower()))\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_col(columns, candidates):\n",
        "    return next((c for c in candidates if c in columns), None)\n",
        "\n",
        "conn = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "    uri=True,\n",
        "    timeout=30\n",
        ")\n",
        "conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    tables = {\n",
        "        row[\"name\"]\n",
        "        for row in conn.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Detect available evidence tables and their actual schemas.\n",
        "    source_tables = [\n",
        "        \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "        \"multi_pdf_passages\",\n",
        "        \"research_papers\",\n",
        "        \"paper_library\",\n",
        "        \"discovered_papers\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    available = []\n",
        "    for table in source_tables:\n",
        "        if table in tables:\n",
        "            cols = {\n",
        "                row[\"name\"]\n",
        "                for row in conn.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")')\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            text_col = get_col(\n",
        "                cols,\n",
        "                [\n",
        "                    \"passage_text\", \"evidence_text\", \"text\", \"content\",\n",
        "                    \"passage\", \"abstract\", \"summary\", \"excerpt\"\n",
        "                ]\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            title_col = get_col(\n",
        "                cols,\n",
        "                [\"paper_title\", \"title\", \"paper_name\", \"name\"]\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            page_col = get_col(\n",
        "                cols,\n",
        "                [\"page_number\", \"page\", \"page_num\"]\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            doi_col = get_col(cols, [\"doi\", \"DOI\"])\n",
        "            url_col = get_col(cols, [\"url\", \"paper_url\", \"source_url\"])\n",
        "            id_col = get_col(cols, [\"id\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "            if text_col:\n",
        "                available.append({\n",
        "                    \"table\": table,\n",
        "                    \"text\": text_col,\n",
        "                    \"title\": title_col,\n",
        "                    \"page\": page_col,\n",
        "                    \"doi\": doi_col,\n",
        "                    \"url\": url_col,\n",
        "                    \"id\": id_col\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Evidence sources available:\")\n",
        "    for source in available:\n",
        "        print(\"-\", source[\"table\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not available:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            \"No recognized text or evidence columns were found. \"\n",
        "            \"No database changes were made.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    question = input(\"\\nEnter your research question: \").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\"Please enter a research question.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    question_terms = tokenize(question)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Generic terms carry less meaning in keyword retrieval.\n",
        "    stop_words = {\n",
        "        \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"which\", \"who\", \"why\", \"how\",\n",
        "        \"does\", \"do\", \"did\", \"is\", \"are\", \"was\", \"were\", \"the\",\n",
        "        \"a\", \"an\", \"and\", \"or\", \"to\", \"of\", \"in\", \"on\", \"for\",\n",
        "        \"with\", \"by\", \"from\", \"can\", \"could\", \"would\", \"should\"\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    query_terms = question_terms - stop_words\n",
        "\n",
        "    passages = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for source in available:\n",
        "        table = source[\"table\"]\n",
        "        text_col = source[\"text\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        selected = [f'\"{text_col}\" AS body']\n",
        "        for key in [\"title\", \"page\", \"doi\", \"url\", \"id\"]:\n",
        "            col = source[key]\n",
        "            selected.append(\n",
        "                f'\"{col}\" AS \"{key}\"' if col else f'NULL AS \"{key}\"'\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        sql = (\n",
        "            \"SELECT \" + \", \".join(selected)\n",
        "            + f' FROM \"{table}\" WHERE \"{text_col}\" IS NOT NULL'\n",
        "            + f' AND TRIM(CAST(\"{text_col}\" AS TEXT)) != \"\" LIMIT 5000'\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        for row in conn.execute(sql):\n",
        "            body = str(row[\"body\"])\n",
        "            body_terms = tokenize(body)\n",
        "            overlap = query_terms & body_terms\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not query_terms:\n",
        "                score = 0.0\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                score = len(overlap) / len(query_terms)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if score > 0:\n",
        "                passages.append({\n",
        "                    \"source_table\": table,\n",
        "                    \"title\": row[\"title\"] or \"Untitled source\",\n",
        "                    \"page\": row[\"page\"],\n",
        "                    \"doi\": row[\"doi\"],\n",
        "                    \"url\": row[\"url\"],\n",
        "                    \"record_id\": row[\"id\"],\n",
        "                    \"score\": round(score, 4),\n",
        "                    \"matched_terms\": sorted(overlap),\n",
        "                    \"text\": body[:1800]\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "    passages.sort(\n",
        "        key=lambda item: (\n",
        "            item[\"score\"],\n",
        "            len(item[\"matched_terms\"])\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        reverse=True\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    top_passages = passages[:10]\n",
        "\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    conn.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create a source-grounded report. This tool retrieves evidence;\n",
        "# it does not invent an answer when evidence is missing.\n",
        "report = {\n",
        "    \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "    \"question\": question,\n",
        "    \"retrieval_method\": \"keyword overlap\",\n",
        "    \"evidence_matches\": len(passages),\n",
        "    \"results\": top_passages,\n",
        "    \"limitations\": [\n",
        "        \"Keyword overlap is not semantic understanding.\",\n",
        "        \"A matching passage does not prove that a claim is correct.\",\n",
        "        \"Verify the original paper and page before citing a claim.\",\n",
        "        \"No evidence match means only that this search found no keyword match.\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(JSON_PATH, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)\n",
        "\n",
        "lines = [\n",
        "    \"# AI Research Scientist — Evidence Report\",\n",
        "    \"\",\n",
        "    f\"**Generated:** {report['generated_at']}\",\n",
        "    \"\",\n",
        "    \"## Research question\",\n",
        "    \"\",\n",
        "    question,\n",
        "    \"\",\n",
        "    \"## Retrieval method\",\n",
        "    \"\",\n",
        "    \"Keyword overlap ranking across recognized saved evidence and paper tables.\",\n",
        "    \"\",\n",
        "    \"## Evidence matches\",\n",
        "    \"\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "if not top_passages:\n",
        "    lines += [\n",
        "        \"No matching evidence was found in the searched saved records.\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"This does not mean that no scientific evidence exists. Try alternate \"\n",
        "        \"keywords or add relevant source passages to the research library.\",\n",
        "        \"\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    for index, item in enumerate(top_passages, 1):\n",
        "        lines += [\n",
        "            f\"### {index}. {item['title']}\",\n",
        "            \"\",\n",
        "            f\"- Source table: `{item['source_table']}`\",\n",
        "            f\"- Record ID: {item['record_id']}\",\n",
        "            f\"- Page: {item['page']}\",\n",
        "            f\"- DOI: {item['doi']}\",\n",
        "            f\"- URL: {item['url']}\",\n",
        "            f\"- Keyword overlap score: {item['score']:.4f}\",\n",
        "            f\"- Matched terms: {', '.join(item['matched_terms'])}\",\n",
        "            \"\",\n",
        "            item[\"text\"],\n",
        "            \"\"\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "lines += [\n",
        "    \"## Limitations\",\n",
        "    \"\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "for limitation in report[\"limitations\"]:\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"- {limitation}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(REPORT_PATH, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    f.write(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n",
        "print(\"RESEARCH EVIDENCE REPORT\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "print(\"Question:\", question)\n",
        "print(\"Evidence matches:\", len(passages))\n",
        "\n",
        "for index, item in enumerate(top_passages, 1):\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"\\n{index}. {item['title']} \"\n",
        "        f\"(score={item['score']:.2f}, table={item['source_table']})\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    print(\"Matched terms:\", \", \".join(item[\"matched_terms\"]))\n",
        "    print(item[\"text\"][:450])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nMarkdown report:\", REPORT_PATH)\n",
        "print(\"JSON report:\", JSON_PATH)\n",
        "print(\"Database was opened read-only; no records were changed.\")"
      ],

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "REPORT_JSON = \"/content/AI_Research_Citation_Audit.json\"\n",
        "REPORT_MD = \"/content/AI_Research_Citation_Audit.md\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        f\"Database not found: {DB_PATH}. \"\n",
        "        \"Check that your existing research.db is available.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# Open the existing database in read-only mode.\n",
        "connection = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "    uri=True,\n",
        "    timeout=5\n",
        ")\n",
        "connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    tables = [\n",
        "        row[\"name\"]\n",
        "        for row in connection.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master \"\n",
        "            \"WHERE type = 'table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    audit = {\n",
        "        \"project\": \"AI Research Scientist\",\n",
        "        \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"database\": DB_PATH,\n",
        "        \"database_modified\": False,\n",
        "        \"tables_checked\": [],\n",
        "        \"findings\": [],\n",
        "        \"summary\": {}\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Check citation-related tables that actually exist.\n",
        "    target_tables = [\n",
        "        \"research_citations\",\n",
        "        \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "        \"research_bibliography\",\n",
        "        \"evidence_reviews\",\n",
        "        \"multi_pdf_passages\",\n",
        "        \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "        \"research_papers\",\n",
        "        \"paper_library\"\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    checked_rows = 0\n",
        "    incomplete_rows = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for table in target_tables:\n",
        "        if table not in tables:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        columns = [\n",
        "            row[\"name\"]\n",
        "            for row in connection.execute(\n",
        "                f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{table}\")'\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows = connection.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT * FROM \"{table}\" LIMIT 500'\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        audit[\"tables_checked\"].append({\n",
        "            \"table\": table,\n",
        "            \"columns\": columns,\n",
        "            \"rows_sampled\": len(rows)\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "        for row in rows:\n",
        "            checked_rows += 1\n",
        "            record = dict(row)\n",
        "            issues = []\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Check likely source-identification fields.\n",
        "            source_fields = [\n",
        "                c for c in columns\n",
        "                if any(word in c.lower() for word in [\n",
        "                    \"source\", \"paper\", \"title\", \"doi\",\n",
        "                    \"url\", \"citation\", \"reference\"\n",
        "                ])\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            evidence_fields = [\n",
        "                c for c in columns\n",
        "                if any(word in c.lower() for word in [\n",
        "                    \"evidence\", \"quote\", \"passage\", \"claim\",\n",
        "                    \"text\", \"content\", \"abstract\"\n",
        "                ])\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            page_fields = [\n",
        "                c for c in columns\n",
        "                if any(word in c.lower() for word in [\n",
        "                    \"page\", \"page_number\", \"page_no\"\n",
        "                ])\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            if source_fields and not any(\n",
        "                str(record.get(c) or \"\").strip()\n",
        "                for c in source_fields\n",
        "            ):\n",
        "                issues.append(\"No source-identification value found\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if evidence_fields and not any(\n",
        "                str(record.get(c) or \"\").strip()\n",
        "                for c in evidence_fields\n",
        "            ):\n",
        "                issues.append(\"No evidence, claim, or passage text found\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # A page number is useful for quotations, but is not\n",
        "            # universally required for web sources or whole-paper records.\n",
        "            if (\n",
        "                page_fields\n",
        "                and any(word in table.lower() for word in [\n",
        "                    \"citation\", \"evidence\", \"passage\"\n",
        "                ])\n",
        "                and not any(\n",
        "                    str(record.get(c) or \"\").strip()\n",
        "                    for c in page_fields\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            ):\n",
        "                issues.append(\"No page locator recorded\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if issues:\n",
        "                incomplete_rows += 1\n",
        "                audit[\"findings\"].append({\n",
        "                    \"table\": table,\n",
        "                    \"record_id\": record.get(\"id\"),\n",
        "                    \"issues\": issues\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "    audit[\"summary\"] = {\n",
        "        \"tables_checked\": len(audit[\"tables_checked\"]),\n",
        "        \"records_sampled\": checked_rows,\n",
        "        \"records_with_possible_gaps\": incomplete_rows,\n",
        "        \"note\": (\n",
        "            \"This is a structural audit of sampled records, not proof \"\n",
        "            \"that citations are factually correct. Up to 500 rows per \"\n",
        "            \"table were checked.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    connection.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(REPORT_JSON, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "    json.dump(audit, file, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(REPORT_MD, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "    file.write(\"# AI Research Scientist — Citation Audit\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    file.write(f\"Generated: {audit['generated_at']}\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    file.write(\"## Summary\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    for key, value in audit[\"summary\"].items():\n",
        "        file.write(f\"- **{key.replace('_', ' ').title()}:** {value}\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    file.write(\"\\n## Tables checked\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    for item in audit[\"tables_checked\"]:\n",
        "        file.write(\n",
        "            f\"- **{item['table']}** — \"\n",
        "            f\"{item['rows_sampled']} records sampled\\n\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    file.write(\"\\n## Possible citation gaps\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    if not audit[\"findings\"]:\n",
        "        file.write(\n",
        "            \"No structural gaps were detected in the sampled records. \"\n",
        "            \"This does not prove that citations are factually correct.\\n\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        for finding in audit[\"findings\"]:\n",
        "            file.write(\n",
        "                f\"- **{finding['table']}**, \"\n",
        "                f\"record ID `{finding['record_id']}`: \"\n",
        "                f\"{'; '.join(finding['issues'])}\\n\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Citation audit complete.\")\n",
        "print(json.dumps(audit[\"summary\"], indent=2))\n",
        "print(\"\\nReports saved:\")\n",
        "print(REPORT_MD)\n",
        "print(REPORT_JSON)\n",
        "print(\"\\nYour database was opened read-only; its records were not changed.\")"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import shutil\n",
        "import zipfile\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "BASE = \"/content\"\n",
        "DB = os.path.join(BASE, \"research.db\")\n",
        "BACKUP_DIR = os.path.join(BASE, \"AI_Research_Backup\")\n",
        "ZIP_PATH = os.path.join(BASE, \"AI_Research_Scientist_Backup.zip\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"Existing research.db was not found. \"\n",
        "        \"Do not create a replacement database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "os.makedirs(BACKUP_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Make a consistent SQLite backup using SQLite's backup API.\n",
        "source = __import__(\"sqlite3\").connect(DB, timeout=10)\n",
        "backup_path = os.path.join(BACKUP_DIR, \"research.db\")\n",
        "destination = __import__(\"sqlite3\").connect(backup_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    source.backup(destination)\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    destination.close()\n",
        "    source.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Include reports and documentation if they exist.\n",
        "patterns = (\n",
        "    \"AI_Research_\", \"README\", \"research_report\",\n",
        "    \"experiment_report\", \"bibliography\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "for name in os.listdir(BASE):\n",
        "    path = os.path.join(BASE, name)\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        os.path.isfile(path)\n",
        "        and name != os.path.basename(ZIP_PATH)\n",
        "        and name != \"research.db\"\n",
        "        and name != os.path.basename(backup_path)\n",
        "        and name.lower().endswith((\".md\", \".json\", \".csv\", \".txt\", \".png\", \".pdf\"))\n",
        "        and any(name.lower().startswith(p.lower()) for p in patterns)\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        shutil.copy2(path, os.path.join(BACKUP_DIR, name))\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create a fresh ZIP archive of the backup folder.\n",
        "with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_PATH, \"w\", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:\n",
        "    for name in os.listdir(BACKUP_DIR):\n",
        "        path = os.path.join(BACKUP_DIR, name)\n",
        "        if os.path.isfile(path):\n",
        "            archive.write(path, arcname=name)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Verify that the backed-up database can be opened and inspected.\n",
        "check = __import__(\"sqlite3\").connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{backup_path}?mode=ro\", uri=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    tables = check.execute(\n",
        "        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "    ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    check.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"BACKUP CREATED\")\n",
        "print(\"Database backup:\", backup_path)\n",
        "print(\"ZIP archive:\", ZIP_PATH)\n",
        "print(\"Database tables verified:\", tables)\n",
        "print(\"ZIP size:\", round(os.path.getsize(ZIP_PATH) / 1024, 1), \"KB\")\n",
        "print(\"Original database preserved.\")"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db was not found. Do not create a replacement.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "connection = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "    uri=True,\n",
        "    timeout=10\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    integrity = connection.execute(\n",
        "        \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "    ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    foreign_keys = connection.execute(\n",
        "        \"PRAGMA foreign_key_check\"\n",
        "    ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    tables = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT name\n",
        "        FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "        WHERE type = 'table'\n",
        "          AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "        ORDER BY name\n",
        "    \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = {\n",
        "        \"database\": DB_PATH,\n",
        "        \"database_size_bytes\": os.path.getsize(DB_PATH),\n",
        "        \"integrity_check\": [row[0] for row in integrity],\n",
        "        \"foreign_key_violations\": len(foreign_keys),\n",
        "        \"tables\": {}\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    for (table_name,) in tables:\n",
        "        # Table names come from SQLite's own schema.\n",
        "        safe_name = table_name.replace('\"', '\"\"')\n",
        "        count = connection.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{safe_name}\"'\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "        results[\"tables\"][table_name] = count\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"DATABASE INTEGRITY TEST\")\n",
        "    print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if integrity == [(\"ok\",)]:\n",
        "        print(\"\\nPASS: SQLite reports that the database is structurally sound.\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(\"\\nWARNING: Review the integrity-check output.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if foreign_keys:\n",
        "        print(\"WARNING: Foreign-key violations were reported.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_path = \"/content/AI_Research_Database_Test.json\"\n",
        "    with open(report_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nReport saved:\", report_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    connection.close()"
      ],
      "metadata": {

        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "\n",
        "db_path = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(db_path):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db not found. Keep your existing database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "con = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{db_path}?mode=ro\", uri=True, timeout=10\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    tables = con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "        WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "        ORDER BY name\n",
        "    \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    report = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for (table,) in tables:\n",
        "        safe = table.replace('\"', '\"\"')\n",
        "        count = con.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{safe}\"'\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "        report[table] = count\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"PROJECT-WIDE AUDIT\")\n",
        "    print(\"Database size:\", os.path.getsize(db_path), \"bytes\")\n",
        "    print(\"Tables found:\", len(report))\n",
        "    print(\"Tables containing data:\",\n",
        "          sum(count > 0 for count in report.values()))\n",
        "    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))\n",
        "\n",
        "    output = \"/content/AI_Research_Project_Audit.json\"\n",
        "    with open(output, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nAudit report saved:\", output)\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    con.close()"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"Existing research.db not found. Do not recreate it.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "con = None\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=10)\n",
        "    con.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Begin a write transaction without saving any lasting changes.\n",
        "    con.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE\")\n",
        "    con.rollback()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"PASS: A write transaction started successfully.\")\n",
        "    print(\"PASS: The test transaction was rolled back.\")\n",
        "    print(\"The database contents were not intentionally changed.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "except sqlite3.OperationalError as error:\n",
        "    print(\"DATABASE WRITE TEST FAILED:\")\n",
        "    print(error)\n",
        "    print(\"\\nIf you see 'database is locked':\")\n",
        "    print(\"1. Stop other running Colab cells.\")\n",
        "    print(\"2. Check that no other notebook or program is writing\")\n",
        "    print(\"   to research.db.\")\n",
        "    print(\"3. Rerun this cell.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    if con is not None:\n",
        "        con.close()"
      ],
      "metadata": {

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import zipfile\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "BASE = Path(\"/content\")\n",
        "DB = BASE / \"research.db\"\n",
        "README = BASE / \"README_AI_Research_Scientist.md\"\n",
        "OUTPUT = BASE / \"AI_Research_Scientist_Portfolio.zip\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not DB.is_file():\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db is missing. Do not create a replacement.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create a consistent backup of the existing database.\n",
        "BACKUP = BASE / \"research.db\"\n",
        "TEMP_BACKUP = BASE / \"research.db.backup.tmp\"\n",
        "\n",
        "source = sqlite3.connect(str(DB), timeout=10)\n",
        "target = sqlite3.connect(str(TEMP_BACKUP))\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    source.backup(target)\n",
        "    result = target.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    target.close()\n",
        "    source.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "if result != \"ok\":\n",
        "    TEMP_BACKUP.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Database backup integrity check failed.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Replace only the temporary backup destination, never the original DB.\n",
        "shutil = __import__(\"shutil\")\n",
        "shutil.move(str(TEMP_BACKUP), str(BACKUP) + \".backup\")\n",
        "\n",
        "included = [BACKUP, Path(str(BACKUP) + \".backup\")]\n",
        "\n",
        "if README.is_file():\n",
        "    included.append(README)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Include generated project reports when available.\n",
        "for path in BASE.iterdir():\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        path.is_file()\n",
        "        and path.suffix.lower() in {\".md\", \".json\", \".csv\", \".txt\", \".pdf\", \".png\"}\n",
        "        and path.name.startswith((\"AI_Research_\", \"research_report\"))\n",
        "        and path not in included\n",
        "        and path != OUTPUT\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        included.append(path)\n",
        "\n",
        "with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT, \"w\", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:\n",
        "    for path in included:\n",
        "        archive.write(path, arcname=path.name)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Verify that the archive can be opened and read.\n",
        "with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT, \"r\") as archive:\n",
        "    bad_file = archive.testzip()\n",
        "    if bad_file:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(f\"ZIP verification failed: {bad_file}\")\n",
        "    print(\"Files included:\")\n",
        "    for name in archive.namelist():\n",
        "        print(\"-\", name)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nPORTFOLIO ZIP CREATED\")\n",
        "print(\"Location:\", OUTPUT)\n",
        "print(\"Size:\", round(OUTPUT.stat().st_size / 1024, 1), \"KB\")\n",
        "print(\"Database backup integrity:\", result)\n",
        "print(\"ZIP verification: PASS\")"
