        "\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "BASE = Path(\"/content\")\n",
        "DB = BASE / \"research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not DB.exists():\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db not found. Keep your existing database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "con = sqlite3.connect(f\"file:{DB}?mode=ro\", uri=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    integrity = con.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "    tables = con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "        WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "        ORDER BY name\n",
        "    \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    counts = {}\n",
        "    for (table,) in tables:\n",
        "        safe = table.replace('\"', '\"\"')\n",
        "        counts[table] = con.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{safe}\"'\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    con.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "summary = {\n",
        "    \"project\": \"AI Research Scientist\",\n",
        "    \"description\": (\n",
        "        \"A Python and SQLite research workspace for organizing \"\n",
        "        \"papers, evidence, citations, experiments, and knowledge links.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    \"database_integrity\": integrity,\n",
        "    \"database_size_bytes\": DB.stat().st_size,\n",
        "    \"table_count\": len(counts),\n",
        "    \"table_record_counts\": counts,\n",
        "    \"features_to_verify\": [\n",
        "        \"Research paper library and discovery\",\n",
        "        \"Research question management\",\n",
        "        \"Evidence search and reporting\",\n",
        "        \"Citation and bibliography tracking\",\n",
        "        \"Experiment tracking\",\n",
        "        \"Knowledge graph\",\n",
        "        \"Research audit reports\"\n",
        "    ],\n",
        "    \"verification_note\": (\n",
        "        \"Listed features were developed during the project. \"\n",
        "        \"Test each module before claiming it is fully operational.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "out_json = BASE / \"AI_Research_Portfolio_Summary.json\"\n",
        "out_md = BASE / \"AI_Research_Portfolio_Summary.md\"\n",
        "\n",
        "out_json.write_text(\n",
        "    json.dumps(summary, indent=2),\n",
        "    encoding=\"utf-8\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "with out_md.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    f.write(\"# AI Research Scientist\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    f.write(\"## Project Overview\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    f.write(summary[\"description\"] + \"\\n\\n\")\n",

      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "REQUIRED_TABLES = {\n",
        "    \"research_questions\",\n",
        "    \"research_papers\",\n",
        "    \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "    \"research_citations\",\n",
        "    \"experiments\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "    \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "results = {\n",
        "    \"database_exists\": os.path.isfile(DB_PATH),\n",
        "    \"integrity\": \"NOT CHECKED\",\n",
        "    \"required_tables_present\": [],\n",
        "    \"required_tables_missing\": [],\n",
        "    \"tests_passed\": 0,\n",
        "    \"tests_failed\": 0,\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "if not results[\"database_exists\"]:\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"Existing research.db not found. Do not recreate it.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "con = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\", uri=True, timeout=10\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    integrity = con.execute(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "    results[\"integrity\"] = integrity\n",
        "\n",
        "    existing = {\n",
        "        row[0]\n",
        "        for row in con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type='table'\n",
        "        \"\"\")\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    results[\"required_tables_present\"] = sorted(\n",
        "        REQUIRED_TABLES & existing\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    results[\"required_tables_missing\"] = sorted(\n",
        "        REQUIRED_TABLES - existing\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    checks = {\n",
        "        \"Database exists\": results[\"database_exists\"],\n",
        "        \"SQLite integrity is OK\": integrity == \"ok\",\n",
        "        \"Required tables present\": not results[\"required_tables_missing\"],\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    for name, passed in checks.items():\n",
        "        print((\"PASS\" if passed else \"FAIL\") + \": \" + name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    results[\"tests_passed\"] = sum(checks.values())\n",
        "    results[\"tests_failed\"] = len(checks) - results[\"tests_passed\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    output = \"/content/AI_Research_Test_Results.json\"\n",
        "    with open(output, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\nDetailed results:\")\n",
        "    print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))\n",
        "    print(\"\\nSaved:\", output)\n",
        "\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    con.close()"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import json\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"research.db not found. Do not create a replacement.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "con = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "    f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\", uri=True, timeout=10\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "modules = {\n",
        "    \"Research questions\": \"research_questions\",\n",
        "    \"Research papers\": \"research_papers\",\n",
        "    \"Research evidence\": \"research_evidence\",\n",
        "    \"Citation tracking\": \"research_citations\",\n",
        "    \"Experiments\": \"experiments\",\n",
        "    \"Knowledge graph nodes\": \"knowledge_nodes\",\n",
        "    \"Knowledge graph edges\": \"knowledge_edges\",\n",
        "    \"Evidence reviews\": \"evidence_reviews\",\n",
        "    \"Discovered papers\": \"discovered_papers\",\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "results = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    existing = {\n",
        "        row[0] for row in con.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    for label, table in modules.items():\n",
        "        if table not in existing:\n",
        "            results[label] = {\n",
        "                \"status\": \"TABLE MISSING\",\n",
        "                \"records\": None\n",
        "            }\n",
        "            print(f\"NOT FOUND: {label}\")\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        safe = table.replace('\"', '\"\"')\n",
        "        count = con.execute(\n",
        "            f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{safe}\"'\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        results[label] = {\n",
        "            \"status\": \"TABLE PRESENT\",\n",
        "            \"records\": count\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"FOUND: {label} — {count} records\")\n",
        "\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    con.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "report = \"/content/AI_Research_Module_Audit.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(report, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nModule audit saved:\", report)\n",
        "print(\"Note: table presence does not prove the module's code works.\")"
      ],

        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "DB_PATH = \"/content/research.db\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not os.path.isfile(DB_PATH):\n",
        "    print(\"STOP: research.db was not found.\")\n",
        "    print(\"Do not create a new database.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    connection = None\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        connection = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "            f\"file:{DB_PATH}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "            uri=True,\n",
        "            timeout=10\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        result = connection.execute(\n",
        "            \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        tables = connection.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT name\n",
        "            FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "            WHERE type = 'table'\n",
        "              AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "            ORDER BY name\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"DATABASE INTEGRITY:\", result[0][0])\n",
        "        print(\"DATABASE SIZE:\", os.path.getsize(DB_PATH), \"bytes\")\n",
        "        print(\"NUMBER OF TABLES:\", len(tables))\n",
        "        print(\"\\nTables found:\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        for (table,) in tables:\n",
        "            safe = table.replace('\"', '\"\"')\n",
        "            count = connection.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{safe}\"'\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "            print(f\"  {table}: {count} records\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if result == [(\"ok\",)]:\n",
        "            print(\"\\nPASS: Database integrity check succeeded.\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"\\nWARNING: Review the integrity results.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except sqlite3.Error as error:\n",
        "        print(\"DATABASE ERROR:\", error)\n",
        "\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        if connection is not None:\n",
        "            connection.close()"
      ],

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import sqlite3\n",
        "import zipfile\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import shutil\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — FINAL PROJECT CHECK\n",
        "# Original database is preserved.\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "BASE = Path(\"/content\")\n",
        "DB = BASE / \"research.db\"\n",
        "BACKUP = BASE / \"research.db.backup\"\n",
        "README = BASE / \"README_AI_Research_Scientist.md\"\n",
        "AUDIT_JSON = BASE / \"AI_Research_Final_Audit.json\"\n",
        "AUDIT_MD = BASE / \"AI_Research_Final_Audit.md\"\n",
        "STATUS_JSON = BASE / \"AI_Research_Final_Status.json\"\n",
        "ZIP_PATH = BASE / \"AI_Research_Scientist_Final.zip\"\n",
        "\n",
        "if not DB.is_file():\n",
        "    raise FileNotFoundError(\n",
        "        \"STOP: /content/research.db was not found. \"\n",
        "        \"This program will not create a replacement database.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "status = {\n",
        "    \"project\": \"AI Research Scientist\",\n",
        "    \"checked_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "    \"original_database\": str(DB),\n",
        "    \"original_database_preserved\": True,\n",
        "    \"integrity\": \"NOT CHECKED\",\n",
        "    \"write_test\": \"NOT CHECKED\",\n",
        "    \"backup\": \"NOT CREATED\",\n",
        "    \"zip\": \"NOT CREATED\",\n",
        "    \"tables\": {},\n",
        "    \"errors\": []\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Inspect the original database in read-only mode.\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(\n",
        "        f\"{DB.as_uri()}?mode=ro\",\n",
        "        uri=True,\n",
        "        timeout=10\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        integrity_rows = con.execute(\n",
        "            \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "        integrity = [row[0] for row in integrity_rows]\n",
        "        status[\"integrity\"] = (\n",
        "            \"PASS\" if integrity == [\"ok\"] else \"CHECK REQUIRED\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        table_names = [\n",
        "            row[\"name\"]\n",
        "            for row in con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                SELECT name\n",
        "                FROM sqlite_master\n",
        "                WHERE type='table'\n",
        "                  AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'\n",
        "                ORDER BY name\n",
        "            \"\"\")\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        for table in table_names:\n",
        "            safe = table.replace('\"', '\"\"')\n",
        "            columns = [\n",
        "                row[\"name\"]\n",
        "                for row in con.execute(\n",
        "                    f'PRAGMA table_info(\"{safe}\")'\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "            count = con.execute(\n",
        "                f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"{safe}\"'\n",
        "            ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "            status[\"tables\"][table] = {\n",
        "                \"records\": count,\n",
        "                \"columns\": columns\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "        status[\"database_size_bytes\"] = DB.stat().st_size\n",
        "\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        con.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "except Exception as error:\n",
        "    status[\"errors\"].append(\n",
        "        \"Database inspection: \" + str(error)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    integrity = [\"inspection failed\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. Check whether a write transaction can begin.\n",
        "# BEGIN IMMEDIATE acquires a write reservation; rollback leaves\n",
        "# no test record or intentional data change.\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test = sqlite3.connect(str(DB), timeout=5)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        test.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000\")\n",
        "        test.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE\")\n",
        "        test.rollback()\n",
        "        status[\"write_test\"] = \"PASS\"\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        test.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "except sqlite3.Error as error:\n",
        "    status[\"write_test\"] = \"FAILED\"\n",
        "    status[\"errors\"].append(\n",
        "        \"Write-lock test: \" + str(error)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# 3. Create a consistent SQLite backup.\n",
        "# Write to a temporary file first so an old backup is not destroyed\n",
        "# if this backup attempt fails.\n",
        "TEMP_BACKUP = BASE / \"research.db.backup.tmp\"\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    TEMP_BACKUP.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    source = sqlite3.connect(str(DB), timeout=10)\n",
        "    target = sqlite3.connect(str(TEMP_BACKUP), timeout=10)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        source.backup(target)\n",
        "        target.commit()\n",
        "        backup_integrity = target.execute(\n",
        "            \"PRAGMA integrity_check\"\n",
        "        ).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "    finally:\n",
        "        target.close()\n",
        "        source.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if backup_integrity != \"ok\":\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            \"Backup integrity check failed: \" + backup_integrity\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    shutil.move(str(TEMP_BACKUP), str(BACKUP))\n",
        "    status[\"backup\"] = \"PASS\"\n",
        "\n",
        "except Exception as error:\n",
        "    status[\"backup\"] = \"FAILED\"\n",
        "    status[\"errors\"].append(\"Backup: \" + str(error))\n",
        "    TEMP_BACKUP.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 4. Generate project documentation.\n",
        "README.write_text(\n",
        "    \"\"\"# AI Research Scientist\n",
        "\n",
        "## Overview\n",
        "A Python and SQLite research workspace for organizing research\n",
        "questions, papers, evidence, citations, experiments, and\n",
        "knowledge relationships.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Technologies\n",
        "- Python\n",
        "- SQLite\n",
        "- Google Colab\n",
        "- JSON and Markdown\n",
        "\n",
        "## Database\n",
        "The project uses `research.db` for persistent research records.\n",
        "Keep backups before making schema changes.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Main developed areas\n",
        "- Research questions and paper discovery\n",
        "- Evidence search and passage retrieval\n",
        "- Citation and bibliography tracking\n",
        "- Experiment tracking and summaries\n",
        "- Knowledge graph records\n",
        "- Audit and report generation\n",
        "\n",
        "## Running the project\n",
        "Open the project notebook in Google Colab, make sure the existing\n",
        "database is available, and run the relevant code cells.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Testing status\n",
        "Use the generated final audit to review database integrity,\n",
        "write access, backup status, and ZIP verification. Database table\n",
        "presence alone does not prove every feature works.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Before publishing\n",
        "Review notebooks and files for API keys, passwords, private data,\n",
        "and material you do not have permission to share.\n",
        "\"\"\",\n",
        "    encoding=\"utf-8\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# 5. Write a readable audit report.\n",
        "AUDIT_JSON.write_text(\n",
        "    json.dumps(status, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),\n",
        "    encoding=\"utf-8\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "with AUDIT_MD.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as file:\n",
        "    file.write(\"# AI Research Scientist — Final Audit\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    file.write(f\"Checked: {status['checked_at']}\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    file.write(f\"- **Database integrity:** {status['integrity']}\\n\")\n",
        "    file.write(f\"- **SQLite write test:** {status['write_test']}\\n\")\n",
        "    file.write(f\"- **Database backup:** {status['backup']}\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    file.write(\"## Table counts\\n\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for table, info in status[\"tables\"].items():\n",
        "        file.write(f\"- `{table}`: {info['records']} records\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    file.write(\"\\n## Issues to review\\n\\n\")\n",
        "    if status[\"errors\"]:\n",
        "        for error in status[\"errors\"]:\n",
        "            file.write(f\"- {error}\\n\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        file.write(\"No errors were recorded by these checks.\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# 6. Build ZIP with only the verified backup and relevant reports.\n",
        "# Never package the live database as a substitute for the backup.\n",
        "package_files = [\n",
        "    README,\n",
        "    AUDIT_JSON,\n",
        "    AUDIT_MD,\n",
        "    STATUS_JSON\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "if BACKUP.is_file():\n",
        "    package_files.append(BACKUP)\n",
        "\n",
        "for path in BASE.iterdir():\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        path.is_file()\n",
        "        and path.suffix.lower() in {\".md\", \".json\", \".csv\", \".txt\", \".pdf\"}\n",
        "        and path.name.startswith((\n",
        "            \"AI_Research_Evidence_Report\",\n",
        "            \"AI_Research_Citation_Audit\",\n",
        "            \"AI_Research_Database_Test\",\n",
        "            \"AI_Research_Project_Audit\",\n",
        "            \"AI_Research_Module_Audit\",\n",
        "            \"AI_Research_Test_Results\",\n",
        "            \"AI_Research_Portfolio_Summary\"\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "        and path not in package_files\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        package_files.append(path)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save an interim status before packaging.\n",
        "STATUS_JSON.write_text(\n",
        "    json.dumps(status, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),\n",
        "    encoding=\"utf-8\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    with zipfile.ZipFile(\n",
        "        ZIP_PATH, \"w\", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED\n",
        "    ) as archive:\n",
        "        for path in package_files:\n",
        "            if path.is_file():\n",
        "                archive.write(path, arcname=path.name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_PATH, \"r\") as archive:\n",
        "        bad_member = archive.testzip()\n",
        "        archive_names = archive.namelist()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if bad_member is not None:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            \"ZIP verification failed at \" + bad_member\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    status[\"zip\"] = \"PASS\"\n",
        "    status[\"zip_size_bytes\"] = ZIP_PATH.stat().st_size\n",
        "    status[\"zip_files\"] = archive_names\n",
        "\n",
        "except Exception as error:\n",
        "    status[\"zip\"] = \"FAILED\"\n",
        "    status[\"errors\"].append(\"ZIP: \" + str(error))\n",
        "\n",
        "# 7. Save the final status.\n",
        "STATUS_JSON.write_text(\n",
        "    json.dumps(status, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),\n",
        "    encoding=\"utf-8\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 52)\n",
        "print(\"AI RESEARCH SCIENTIST — FINAL CHECK RESULTS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 52)\n",
        "print(\"Database integrity:\", status[\"integrity\"])\n",
        "print(\"SQLite write test:\", status[\"write_test\"])\n",
        "print(\"Database backup:\", status[\"backup\"])\n",
        "print(\"ZIP verification:\", status[\"zip\"])\n",
        "print(\"Research tables found:\", len(status[\"tables\"]))\n",
        "print(\"Original database preserved:\", status[\"original_database_preserved\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nTABLE COUNTS\")\n",
        "for table, info in status[\"tables\"].items():\n",
        "    print(f\"  {table}: {info['records']}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nOUTPUT FILES\")\n",
        "for path in [README, AUDIT_JSON, AUDIT_MD, STATUS_JSON, BACKUP, ZIP_PATH]:\n",
        "    if path.is_file():\n",
        "        print(f\"  {path} ({path.stat().st_size:,} bytes)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if status[\"errors\"]:\n",
        "    print(\"\\nITEMS NEEDING ATTENTION\")\n",
        "    for error in status[\"errors\"]:\n",
        "        print(\"  -\", error)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nFINAL STATUS\")\n",
        "if (\n",
        "    status[\"integrity\"] == \"PASS\"\n",
        "    and status[\"backup\"] == \"PASS\"\n",
        "    and status[\"zip\"] == \"PASS\"\n",
        "):\n",
        "    print(\"DATABASE, BACKUP, AND PACKAGE CHECKS PASSED.\")\n",
        "    if status[\"write_test\"] != \"PASS\":\n",
        "        print(\"However, the SQLite write test still needs attention.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"REVIEW THE RESULTS ABOVE BEFORE CALLING THE PROJECT FINISHED.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nDownload the ZIP from the Colab Files panel:\")\n",
        "print(ZIP_PATH)"

        "import zipfile\n",
        "\n",
        "p = Path(\"/content/AI_Research_Scientist_Final.zip\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if not p.exists():\n",
        "    print(\"ZIP NOT FOUND\")\n",
        "    print(\"Run the finalization program I gave you.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    with zipfile.ZipFile(p, \"r\") as z:\n",
        "        print(\"ZIP VERIFIED:\", z.testzip() is None)\n",
        "        print(\"Files included:\", len(z.namelist()))\n",
        "        for name in z.namelist():\n",
        "            print(\"-\", name)\n",
